Future Dynamic 3D Reconstruction: A 3D World Model with Disentangled Ego-Motion
Authors: Nils Morbitzer, Jonathan Evers, Artem Savkin, Thomas Stauner, Nassir Navab, Federico Tombari, Stefano Gasperini
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-16T17:59:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:59:46+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026. Project page: https://fr3d-wm.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Forecasting the evolution of dynamic environments is crucial for autonomous agents. While generative world models have recently achieved high photorealism in 2D video synthesis by mixing ego-motion and environmental dynamics within the image plane, they exhibit physical inconsistencies, such as morphing or vanishing objects, especially over long time horizons. In this paper, we propose FR3D, a world model that predicts a persistent 3D latent representation for future dynamic 3D reconstruction. Unlike prior works that treat the world as a sequence of image-based features, FR3D explicitly decouples the 3D evolution of the scene from the agent's trajectory, treating the inferred ego-motion as a latent proxy for action. This disentanglement resolves the ambiguities between self-motion and world-motion, ensuring geometric consistency into the future. Furthermore, we introduce a teacher-student distillation strategy that leverages the spatial "common sense" of off-the-shelf foundation models, leading to robust zero-shot generalization. Extensive experiments demonstrate FR3D's strong performance for future dynamic 3D reconstruction from monocular observations across multiple datasets, even 2 seconds into the future. Project page: https://fr3d-wm.github.io.
中文标题/摘要
标题：未来动态三维重建：解耦自运动的三维世界模型
预测动态环境的演化对自主代理至关重要。尽管近期生成式世界模型通过在图像平面内混合自运动与环境动态，在二维视频合成中实现了高逼真度，但在长时程下仍存在物理不一致性，如物体变形或消失。本文提出FR3D，一种预测未来动态三维重建的持久三维潜在表示的世界模型。与以往将世界视为图像特征序列的方法不同，FR3D明确将场景的三维演化与代理轨迹解耦，将推断的自运动视为动作的潜在代理。这种解耦消除了自运动与世界运动之间的歧义，确保未来几何一致性。此外，我们引入教师-学生蒸馏策略，利用现成基础模型的空间“常识”，实现稳健的零样本泛化。大量实验表明，FR3D在多个数据集上，仅凭单目观测即可实现强性能的未来动态三维重建，甚至可预测2秒后的场景。项目页面：https://fr3d-wm.github.io。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for physically consistent future scene prediction in autonomous systems, FR3D introduces a 3D world model that disentangles ego-motion from environmental dynamics to enable accurate long-term 3D reconstruction. Instead of treating scenes as image sequences, it predicts a persistent 3D latent representation, using inferred ego-motion as a latent proxy for agent action to resolve ambiguities and ensure geometric fidelity. The model employs a teacher-student distillation approach to transfer spatial common sense from foundation models, enhancing zero-shot generalization. Experiments across multiple datasets show FR3D achieves strong performance in predicting 3D scenes up to two seconds ahead from monocular inputs, outperforming prior methods in consistency and accuracy.
为提升自主系统在动态环境中的物理一致性预测，FR3D提出一种3D世界模型，通过解耦自运动与环境动态，实现长期准确的3D重建。不同于将场景视为图像序列的方法，它推断出持续的3D潜在表示，并将自运动作为动作的潜在代理，从而消除导致物体变形或消失的歧义。模型进一步采用教师-学生蒸馏策略，利用现成基础模型的空间常识提升零样本泛化能力。在多个数据集上的实验表明，FR3D能从单目输入中准确预测未来两秒内的3D场景。
Unified Multimodal Autoregressive Modeling with Shared Context-Visual Tokenizer is Key to Unification
Authors: Wujian Peng, Lingchen Meng, Yuxuan Cai, Xianwei Zhuang, Yuhuan Yang, Rongyao Fang, Chenfei Wu, Junyang Lin, Zuxuan Wu, Shuai Bai
First: 2026-06-16T17:59:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:59:22+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICML2026. Project page https://sharelab-sii.github.io/uniar-web
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Unified Multimodal Modeling aims to integrate visual understanding and generation within a single system. However, existing approaches typically rely on two disparate visual tokenizers, which splits the representation space and hinders truly unified modeling. We propose UniAR, a unified autoregressive framework where a single discrete visual tokenizer serves as the key bridge between understanding and generation, enabling a shared context in which the model can directly interpret its own generated visual tokens without additional re-encoding. UniAR adapts a pretrained vision encoder with multi-level feature fusion and a lookup-free bitwise quantization scheme, preserving both high-level semantics and low-level details while scaling the effective visual vocabulary at minimal cost. Building on this, the unified autoregressive model adopts parallel-bitwise-prediction to jointly predict spatially grouped, multi-level visual codes, substantially reducing visual sequence length and accelerating generation. Finally, a diffusion-based visual decoder operates on discrete visual tokens to decode high-fidelity images. Through large-scale pre-training, followed by supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, UniAR achieves state-of-the-art performance on image generation and image editing while remaining competitive on multimodal understanding benchmarks. The project page is available at https://sharelab-sii.github.io/uniar-web.
中文标题/摘要
标题：共享上下文视觉标记器是统一多模态自回归建模的关键
统一多模态建模旨在在一个系统内整合视觉理解和生成。然而，现有方法通常依赖两个不同的视觉标记器，导致表示空间分裂，阻碍真正统一的建模。我们提出UniAR，一种统一的自回归框架，其中单一离散视觉标记器作为理解与生成之间的关键桥梁，使模型能够在共享上下文中直接解释自身生成的视觉标记，无需额外重编码。UniAR采用预训练视觉编码器，结合多级特征融合与无查找位量化方案，在保持高层语义和低层细节的同时，以最小成本扩展有效视觉词汇表。在此基础上，统一自回归模型采用并行位预测，联合预测空间分组的多级视觉码，显著缩短视觉序列长度并加速生成。最后，基于扩散的视觉解码器在离散视觉标记上操作，解码高保真图像。通过大规模预训练，随后监督微调和强化学习，UniAR在图像生成与图像编辑任务上达到最先进性能，同时在多模态理解基准测试中保持竞争力。项目页面见 https://sharelab-sii.github.io/uniar-web。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the fragmentation caused by separate visual tokenizers in multimodal models, UniAR introduces a unified autoregressive framework using a single discrete visual tokenizer to bridge understanding and generation. It adapts a pretrained vision encoder with multi-level feature fusion and bitwise quantization to preserve semantic and detail fidelity while expanding the visual vocabulary efficiently. The model employs parallel-bitwise-prediction to jointly predict multi-level visual codes, reducing sequence length and speeding up generation, followed by a diffusion decoder operating on discrete tokens. Evaluated across image generation, editing, and multimodal understanding tasks, UniAR achieves state-of-the-art results in generation and remains competitive in understanding benchmarks through large-scale pre-training and reinforcement learning.
为解决多模态系统中使用独立视觉分词器导致的表征割裂问题，作者提出UniAR统一自回归框架，通过单一离散视觉分词器连接理解与生成，实现对自身生成视觉令牌的直接解析。该方法基于预训练视觉编码器，结合多级特征融合与无查找位量化方案，在保持语义与细节保真度的同时低成本扩展视觉词汇表。进一步采用并行位预测机制联合预测空间分组的多级视觉码，缩短序列长度并加速生成，最终由扩散式视觉解码器输出高保真图像。实验表明，UniAR在图像生成与编辑任务上达到最先进水平，同时在多模态理解基准上保持竞争力。
Visual Verification Enables Inference-time Steering and Autonomous Policy Improvement
Authors: Mingtong Zhang, Dhruv Shah
First: 2026-06-16T17:59:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:59:04+00:00
Comments: Website: https://veritas-improvement.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Robots deployed in the real world should learn from their experience and improve over time. This requires a mechanism of practicing and learning from feedback. In this paper, we propose VERITAS, a generator-verifier framework for generalist robot policies for inference-time policy steering and self-improvement. We use a pre-trained generalist robot policy as a ``generator'' and pair it with a gradient-free ``visual verifier'' that evaluates actions at inference time. This framework enables inference-time steering that improves policy performance without additional training. We demonstrate that inference-time verification consistently outperforms vanilla generalists without training on additional demonstration data. Additionally, we demonstrate that the verified rollouts provide effective supervision for offline policy improvement: policies fine-tuned on verified self-generated trajectories achieve consistent performance gains. Notably, we find that post-training with verified rollouts achieves comparable efficiency to expert demonstrations, while requiring no human interventions. Our results highlight inference-time verification as a practical and scalable mechanism for improving robotic policies during deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉验证实现推理时控制与自主策略改进
部署在现实世界中的机器人应从经验中学习并随时间改进，这需要一种通过实践和反馈学习的机制。本文提出VERITAS，一种面向通用机器人策略的生成器-验证器框架，支持推理时策略引导与自我改进。我们使用预训练的通用机器人策略作为“生成器”，并配对一个无梯度的“视觉验证器”，在推理时评估动作。该框架实现无需额外训练即可提升策略性能的推理时引导。我们证明，推理时验证始终优于未经额外演示数据训练的通用策略。此外，我们表明，经验证的轨迹可有效监督离线策略改进：在验证的自生成轨迹上微调的策略可实现持续性能提升。值得注意的是，我们发现，经验证轨迹后训练的效率可媲美专家演示，且无需人工干预。我们的结果凸显推理时验证作为部署期间改进机器人策略的实用且可扩展机制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robots to autonomously improve performance during real-world deployment without additional training, this paper introduces VERITAS, a generator-verifier framework that combines a pre-trained generalist policy with a gradient-free visual verifier to enable inference-time steering and self-improvement. The verifier evaluates actions at inference time, allowing the system to adjust policy behavior dynamically without retraining. Experimental results show that inference-time verification consistently outperforms baseline generalist policies without extra demonstrations, and that fine-tuning policies on verified self-generated trajectories yields significant performance gains. Notably, post-training with verified rollouts achieves performance comparable to expert demonstrations while eliminating human intervention.
为使机器人在真实部署中无需额外训练即可自主提升，本文提出VERITAS框架，结合预训练通用策略与无梯度视觉验证器，在推理时实现策略引导与自我优化。验证器在推理阶段评估动作，使系统能动态调整策略。实验表明，推理时验证持续优于无训练的通用策略，且在验证轨迹上微调策略可稳定提升性能。值得注意的是，验证轨迹后的微调效果可媲美专家演示，且无需人工干预。
Variable-Width Transformers
Authors: Zhaofeng Wu, Oliver Sieberling, Shawn Tan, Rameswar Panda, Yury Polyanskiy, Yoon Kim
First: 2026-06-16T17:59:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:59:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scaling model size, specifically depth and width, has driven significant progress in transformer-based language models. However, most architectures maintain a constant width across all layers, allocating a fixed parameter and computation budget evenly despite different layers potentially playing distinct computational roles. In this work, we empirically investigate nonuniform capacity allocation across network depth by proposing a $\times$-shaped > <former architecture. This design maintains wider early and late layers while narrowing the middle layers, utilizing a parameter-free residual resizing mechanism. Across decoder-only language models ranging from 200M to 2B parameters (dense) and 3B parameters (MoE), our > <former consistently outperforms parameter-matched uniform baselines on language modeling loss. By reducing the average layer width, this architecture also requires fewer overall FLOPs (22% reduction under fitted loss-matched scaling curves) and smaller KV cache memory and I/O cost (15% reduction). In analysis, we show that this bottleneck structure results in qualitatively different representations in residual streams. Overall, our results demonstrate that nonuniform width allocation can result in more resource-optimal scaling of language models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：变宽变换器
模型规模的扩展，特别是深度和宽度，推动了基于变换器的语言模型的显著进展。然而，大多数架构在所有层中保持恒定宽度，尽管不同层可能承担不同的计算角色，仍均匀分配固定参数和计算预算。在本研究中，我们通过提出一种×形架构，实证研究了网络深度中非均匀容量分配的效果。该设计在早期和晚期层保持较宽，中间层则较窄，采用无参数残差重缩放机制。在从200M到2B参数（稠密）和3B参数（MoE）的解码器专用语言模型中，我们的×形架构在语言建模损失上始终优于参数匹配的均匀基线。通过降低平均层宽，该架构还减少了整体FLOPs（在拟合损失匹配的缩放曲线下减少22%）和KV缓存内存及I/O成本（减少15%）。分析表明，这种瓶颈结构导致残差流中的表征质变不同。总体而言，我们的结果表明，非均匀宽度分配可实现语言模型更优的资源效率扩展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of uniform layer widths in transformer models despite varying computational demands across depth, this work proposes a variable-width architecture that widens early and late layers while narrowing middle layers, using a parameter-free residual resizing mechanism. Evaluated across decoder-only models from 200M to 3B parameters, the design consistently outperforms uniform baselines on language modeling loss while reducing overall FLOPs by 22% and KV cache memory/I/O cost by 15% under loss-matched scaling. Analysis reveals that the bottleneck structure generates qualitatively distinct residual representations, demonstrating that nonuniform width allocation enables more resource-efficient scaling.
为应对Transformer模型中各层计算需求差异但宽度固定导致的资源浪费，该研究提出一种早期和晚期层加宽、中间层收窄的变宽架构，通过无参数残差重缩放机制实现。在200M至3B参数的解码器模型上，该架构在语言建模损失上持续优于同等参数量的均匀基线，同时在损失匹配缩放曲线下降低22%的总FLOPs和15%的KV缓存内存与I/O开销。分析表明，瓶颈结构导致残差流呈现质性不同的表征，证明非均匀宽度分配可实现更高效的资源扩展。
MOCHI: Motion Enhancement of Collaborative Human-object Interactions
Authors: Jiye Lee, Yonghun Choi, Jungdam Won
Venue: SIGGRAPH 2026
First: 2026-06-16T17:58:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:58:44+00:00
Comments: SIGGRAPH 2026 Journal (ACM TOG); Project page: https://jiyewise.github.io/projects/MOCHI/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Collaborative human-object interaction shows dynamic and complex movements that require mutual anticipation and continuous adjustment between participants and the shared object. Modeling such collaborative multi-human object interaction (MHOI) scenarios requires high-quality data acquisition as a foundational step; however, this is challenging due to the inherent complexity of MHOI where human-human and human-object interactions occur simultaneously. Such complexity leads to noisy MHOI captures characterized by several artifacts: contact misalignment between hands and objects, motion jitter and temporal inconsistencies in the captured sequences, and missing or incomplete finger-level articulation details. To address these challenges, we present MOCHI (MOtion Enhancement of Collaborative Human-object Interactions), a two-stage framework for enhancing noisy MHOI data. Our approach first generates physically plausible hand grasps through optimization from noisy body input, producing grasps that are both physically plausible and semantically consistent with the body pose, where these optimized grasps are extended into complete hand-object interaction sequences. Consequently, the full-body motion for all participants are refined through a diffusion-based noise optimization framework that uses single-person motion priors. During the optimization process, we introduce optimization objectives to encode human-object and human-human interaction information within these single-person priors. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our pipeline across diverse MHOI data, either acquired by existing capture methods or synthesized by generative models. We further show robustness of our system across varying numbers of participants and types of interactions, and demonstrate various applications including keyframe-based MHOI creation and data augmentation through varying object geometries.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MOCHI：协作人机交互的运动增强
协作人机交互表现出动态且复杂的运动，需要参与者与共享物体之间相互预测和持续调整。建模此类协作多人体人机交互（MHOI）场景需要高质量的数据采集作为基础步骤；然而，由于MHOI固有的复杂性——人类与人类、人类与物体的交互同时发生——这极具挑战性。这种复杂性导致捕获的MHOI数据存在多种伪影：手与物体接触对齐错误、运动抖动及序列时间不一致性，以及缺失或不完整的指部细节。为解决这些问题，我们提出MOCHI（协作人机交互运动增强），一种两阶段框架用于增强噪声MHOI数据。我们的方法首先通过优化从噪声身体输入中生成物理上合理的手部抓握姿态，生成既物理合理又语义上与身体姿态一致的抓握姿态，并将其扩展为完整的手-物体交互序列。随后，通过基于扩散的噪声优化框架，利用单人运动先验对所有参与者的全身运动进行精细化处理。在优化过程中，我们引入优化目标，将人-物体和人-人交互信息编码进这些单人先验中。实验结果表明，我们的流程在多样化的MHOI数据上均有效，无论是通过现有捕获方法获取，还是由生成模型合成。我们进一步展示了系统在不同参与人数和交互类型下的鲁棒性，并演示了多种应用，包括基于关键帧的MHOI创建和通过不同物体几何形状实现的数据增强。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve data quality in collaborative human-object interaction scenarios, where motion artifacts like hand-object misalignment and temporal inconsistencies degrade analysis, MOCHI introduces a two-stage framework. First, it generates physically plausible hand grasps from noisy body inputs and extends them into full interaction sequences. Then, it refines full-body motion using a diffusion-based noise optimization method that incorporates single-person motion priors enriched with human-object and human-human interaction constraints. Experiments show the method effectively enhances noisy MHOI data across diverse capture sources and interaction types, enabling applications such as keyframe-based synthesis and data augmentation via object geometry variation.
为解决协作式人-物交互中因复杂性导致的运动数据噪声问题，MOCHI提出两阶段增强框架。第一阶段通过优化从噪声体态输入中生成物理合理且语义一致的手部抓握姿态，并扩展为完整交互序列；第二阶段利用扩散式去噪框架结合单人运动先验，对全身运动进行优化，其中先验嵌入了人-物与人-人交互约束。实验表明该方法在真实采集与生成数据中均有效提升数据质量，对参与者数量与交互类型具有鲁棒性，支持关键帧生成与基于物体几何的数据增强等应用。
EventDrive: Event Cameras for Vision-Language Driving Intelligence
Authors: Dongyue Lu, Rong Li, Ao Liang, Lingdong Kong, Wei Yin, Lai Xing Ng, Benoit R. Cottereau, Camille Simon Chane, Wei Tsang Ooi
First: 2026-06-16T17:58:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:58:40+00:00
Comments: CVPR2026, 34 pages, 15 figures, 15 tables, project page: https://dylanorange.github.io/projects/eventdrive
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Event cameras sense the world through asynchronous brightness changes with microsecond latency and high dynamic range, offering motion fidelity far beyond frame-based sensors and capturing temporal structure that conventional exposures often miss. These properties make events a powerful complement to RGB in autonomous driving, especially under blur, glare, and rapid motion, where frame-based perception can become unreliable. However, existing event-aware vision-language models remain limited to generic perception and do not reveal how event sensing contributes to reasoning and decision-making across the full driving loop. We present EventDrive, a large-scale benchmark and model suite that unifies event streams, RGB frames, and language supervision across four core dimensions: Perception, Understanding, Prediction, and Planning, covering captions, structured QA, grounding, motion-state recognition, trajectory forecasting, and planning tasks. Building on this foundation, EventDrive-VLM introduces a multi-horizon event pyramid and a temporal-horizon mixture-of-experts module to adaptively encode and fuse asynchronous and frame-based information for downstream reasoning. Comprehensive evaluation across diverse tasks shows that event streams provide substantial gains in temporal precision, motion awareness, and robustness, bringing event sensing into the center of driving intelligence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EventDrive：用于视觉-语言驾驶智能的事件相机
事件相机通过微秒级延迟和高动态范围感知世界中的异步亮度变化，其运动保真度远超帧式传感器，并能捕捉传统曝光常忽略的时间结构。这些特性使事件感知成为自动驾驶中RGB视觉的强大补充，尤其在模糊、眩光和快速运动场景下，帧式感知可能不可靠。然而，现有事件感知的视觉-语言模型仍局限于通用感知，未能揭示事件感知如何在完整驾驶闭环中支持推理与决策。我们提出EventDrive，一个大规模基准与模型套件，统一事件流、RGB帧与语言监督，覆盖感知、理解、预测与规划四大核心维度，包括图像描述、结构化问答、定位、运动状态识别、轨迹预测与规划任务。在此基础上，EventDrive-VLM引入多时间尺度事件金字塔与时间尺度混合专家模块，自适应编码与融合异步与帧式信息，以支持下游推理。在多样化任务上的全面评估表明，事件流显著提升时间精度、运动感知与鲁棒性，将事件感知置于驾驶智能的核心位置。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the superior motion fidelity and high dynamic range of event cameras over frame-based sensors, this work addresses the gap in vision-language models that fail to leverage event data for driving intelligence. EventDrive introduces a large-scale benchmark and model suite integrating event streams, RGB frames, and language supervision across perception, understanding, prediction, and planning tasks. The proposed EventDrive-VLM employs a multi-horizon event pyramid and a temporal-horizon mixture-of-experts module to adaptively fuse asynchronous event and frame-based inputs. Experimental results demonstrate significant improvements in temporal precision, motion awareness, and robustness under challenging conditions like blur, glare, and rapid motion, validating event sensing as a core component for driving intelligence.
鉴于事件相机在运动感知和动态范围方面的优势，本研究旨在填补现有视觉语言模型未能有效利用事件数据进行驾驶智能的空白。EventDrive构建了一个大规模基准和模型套件，将事件流、RGB图像与语言监督统一应用于感知、理解、预测与规划四大核心任务。所提出的EventDrive-VLM采用多时间尺度事件金字塔与时间混合专家模块，自适应融合异步事件与帧图像信息。实验表明，事件数据显著提升时间精度、运动感知能力与系统鲁棒性，确立事件相机在自动驾驶推理中的核心地位。
EBench: Elemental Diagnosis of Generalist Mobile Manipulation Policies
Authors: Ning Gao, Jinliang Zheng, Xing Gao, Haoxiang Ma, Hanqing Wang, Yukai Wang, Jiantong Chen, Zanxin Chen, Shujie Zhang, Mingda Jia, Xuekun Jiang, Zihou Zhu, Xinyu Li, Shuai Wang, Hao Li, Wenzhe Cai, Yuqiang Yang, Xudong Xu, Zhaoyang Lyu, Yao Mu, Tai Wang, Jiangmiao Pang, Jia Zeng, Weinan Zhang, Chunhua Shen
First: 2026-06-16T17:58:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:58:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present EBench, a simulation benchmark that diagnoses generalist mobile manipulation policies beyond a single success-rate scalar. EBench comprises 26 diverse and challenging manipulation tasks annotated along 5 capability dimensions and 4 generalization dimensions. We evaluate state-of-the-art generalist manipulation models including $π_0$, $π_{0.5}$, XVLA, and InternVLA-A1, and reveal that models with near success rates exhibit strikingly different capability profiles: $π_{0.5}$ achieves the highest test success rate and the best train--test retention, whereas InternVLA-A1 dominates mobile manipulation but collapses on dexterous tasks, and XVLA exhibits strengths on a disjoint set of atomic skills compared to other policies. Beyond capability profiling, EBench analyzes the generalization ability from 4 representative perspectives, identifying the impact of different distribution shift factors. The results reveal strengths and weaknesses of models behind an overall score. We hope this benchmark offers a broad set of diagnostic signals to guide iteration on generalist manipulation models.
中文标题/摘要

ReproRepo: Scaling Reproducibility Audits with GitHub Repository Issues
Authors: Shanda Li, Qiuhong Anna Wei, Jingwu Tang, Valerie Chen, Nihar B Shah, Tim Dettmers, Yiming Yang, Ameet Talwalkar
First: 2026-06-16T17:58:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:58:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Reproducing research results from papers and released code is central to scientific progress. Existing works have introduced benchmarks to evaluate whether LLM agents can assist with reproducibility, but they are difficult to scale due to their reliance on substantial manual effort for data curation and evaluation. We introduce ReproRepo, a scalable framework for reproducibility evaluation that leverages human-raised GitHub issues as naturally occurring supervision on realistic reproduction blockers. We instantiate ReproRepo on 1,149 recent machine learning papers from major conferences and evaluate four frontier model-agent configurations. Our results show that LLM agents, even without executing code, can identify many real-world reproducibility problems from paper-repository pairs: the best agent in our study, namely Codex with GPT-5.5, surfaces at least one semantically related human-reported blocker for ~90% of papers in the study. Further analysis shows that agents are particularly effective for surfacing visible failures and identifying the right semantic region, but may still be insufficient in exact localization. ReproRepo can serve as a reusable, scalable framework for future evaluations of LLM agents on real-world reproducibility auditing. Our code is released at https://github.com/LithiumDA/ReproRepo.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ReproRepo：利用 GitHub 仓库问题规模化可复现性审计
从论文和开源代码中复现研究结果是科学进步的核心。现有工作引入基准测试以评估大语言模型代理是否能协助可复现性，但因依赖大量人工数据整理与评估，难以扩展。我们提出 ReproRepo，一个利用 GitHub 人工提交问题作为自然监督信号、评估真实可复现性障碍的可扩展框架。我们在 1,149 篇近期机器学习会议论文上实例化 ReproRepo，并评估四种前沿模型-代理配置。结果表明，即使不执行代码，LLM 代理也能从论文-仓库对中识别大量现实可复现性问题：本研究中表现最佳的代理（Codex 与 GPT-5.5）在约 90% 的论文中能发现至少一个语义相关的人类报告障碍。进一步分析显示，代理在揭示可见失败和定位正确语义区域方面尤为有效，但在精确定位方面仍显不足。ReproRepo 可作为未来评估 LLM 代理在真实世界可复现性审计中表现的可复用、可扩展框架。代码已发布于 https://github.com/LithiumDA/ReproRepo。

为解决机器学习研究可复现性审计难以规模化的问题，作者提出ReproRepo框架，利用GitHub上人类提交的问题作为自然监督信号识别复现障碍。在1149篇近期ML论文上评估四种LLM代理配置后发现，即使不执行代码，最优代理（Codex搭配GPT-5.5）也能为约90%的论文找到语义相关的人类报告障碍。代理在识别明显失败和定位语义区域方面表现良好，但在精确定位上仍显不足。
Sign-Rank, Index, and List Replicability: Connections and Separations
Authors: Ari Blondal, Hamed Hatami, Pooya Hatami, Chavdar Lalov, Sivan Tretiak
First: 2026-06-16T17:57:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:57:16+00:00
Comments: 29 pages, 1 figure
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In learning theory, the sign rank of a binary concept class captures the smallest dimension in which it can be represented by points and halfspaces. Despite tremendous interest, lower bounds on sign rank are notoriously difficult to come by. Two recent approaches to the problem establish lower bounds on sign rank by measures that are easier to analyze: the $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index and the list replicability number. We order these measures, showing that the $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index is upper-bounded by a linear function of the list replicability number. As a main consequence, we obtain a strong separation between sign rank and $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index, thereby resolving a question of Frick, Hosseini, and Vasileuski. This motivates a thorough study of list replicability, the stronger of the two lower-bounding measures. We establish upper bounds on the list replicability number by two combinatorial measures: height and minimum star number. We also prove a fundamental composition result, showing that the product of two concept classes has list replicability number bounded by the sum of the list replicability numbers of the two classes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：符号秩、索引与列表可复制性：关联与分离
在学习理论中，二元概念类的符号秩捕捉了其能被点和半空间表示的最小维度。尽管备受关注，符号秩的下界却极难获得。两种近期方法通过更易分析的度量建立符号秩的下界：$\mathbb{Z}_2$-索引和列表可复制数。我们对这些度量进行排序，表明$\mathbb{Z}_2$-索引被列表可复制数的线性函数上界。作为主要结果，我们得到符号秩与$\mathbb{Z}_2$-索引之间的强分离，从而解决了Frick、Hosseini和Vasileuski提出的问题。这促使我们深入研究列表可复制性——两种下界度量中更强的一个。我们通过两个组合度量——高度和最小星数——建立了列表可复制数的上界。我们还证明了一个基本的组合结果，表明两个概念类的乘积的列表可复制数被两个类的列表可复制数之和所上界。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of proving lower bounds on sign rank, the paper investigates two proxy measures — $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index and list replicability — that are easier to analyze. It establishes that the $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index is linearly bounded by list replicability, leading to a strong separation between sign rank and $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index, resolving a prior open question. The study then focuses on list replicability, proving upper bounds via combinatorial parameters like height and minimum star number, and demonstrating a composition property that the list replicability of a product class is at most the sum of the replicabilities of its components.
由于直接证明符号秩下界极为困难，本文研究了两种更易分析的替代度量——$\mathbb{Z}_2$-指数和列表可复制性，它们均可作为符号秩的下界。作者证明$\mathbb{Z}_2$-指数被列表可复制性线性上界，从而得出符号秩与$\mathbb{Z}_2$-指数的强分离，解决了Frick等人提出的问题。随后，本文聚焦于更强的列表可复制性度量，通过高度和最小星数等组合量建立了其上界，并证明了一个基本的组合性质：两个概念类的乘积的列表可复制性不超过各自可复制性的和。
EvolveNav: Proactive Preflection and Self-Evolving Memory for Zero-Shot Object Goal Navigation
Authors: Qi Chai, Wenhao Shen, Nanjie Yao, Yue Xia, Kaiyong Zhao, Jie Ma, Guosheng Lin, Hao Wang
First: 2026-06-16T17:56:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:56:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Zero-Shot Object-Goal Navigation (ZS-OGN) requires embodied agents to explore and locate target objects without any prior training. To this end, recent methods leverage foundation models. But they typically rely on static priors and lack adaptation, which leads to repeated errors and costly trial and error. In this paper, we propose a self-evolving ZS-OGN framework that enables continuous test-time improvement. Specifically, we build an agentic rule memory by extracting actionable knowledge from past trajectories. Then, we propose a retrieval strategy based on upper confidence bound, selecting effective rules by balancing semantic relevance and historical success. In addition, we introduce a memory-guided preflection module that forecasts potential outcomes before action, reducing inefficient exploration. Extensive experiments show that our method outperforms existing zero-shot baselines, achieving a 10.1\% improvement in success rate with fewer unnecessary steps.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EvolveNav：面向零样本目标导航的主动预思与自进化记忆
零样本目标导航（ZS-OGN）要求具身智能体在无任何先验训练的情况下探索并定位目标物体。为此，近期方法依赖基础模型，但通常依赖静态先验且缺乏自适应能力，导致重复错误和高成本的试错。本文提出一种自进化ZS-OGN框架，支持测试时持续改进。具体而言，我们通过提取过往轨迹中的可操作知识构建代理规则记忆；随后提出基于上置信界（UCB）的检索策略，通过平衡语义相关性与历史成功率选择有效规则；此外，引入记忆引导的预思模块，在行动前预测潜在结果，减少低效探索。大量实验表明，我们的方法优于现有零样本基线，在减少不必要的步骤前提下，成功率提升10.1%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of static foundation models in zero-shot object-goal navigation, this paper introduces EvolveNav, a self-evolving framework that improves agent performance through continuous test-time adaptation. The method constructs an agentic rule memory from past trajectories and employs an upper confidence bound-based retrieval strategy to select effective, contextually relevant rules. It further integrates a memory-guided preflection module that predicts outcomes before action to avoid inefficient exploration. Experimental results demonstrate that EvolveNav significantly outperforms existing zero-shot baselines, achieving a 10.1% higher success rate while reducing unnecessary steps.
为解决现有零样本物体目标导航方法依赖静态先验、缺乏自适应导致重复错误的问题，本文提出EvolveNav框架，通过测试时持续进化提升导航性能。该方法从历史轨迹中构建可执行规则记忆库，并基于上置信界策略平衡语义相关性与历史成功率进行规则检索，同时引入记忆引导的预反思模块，在行动前预测潜在结果以减少无效探索。实验表明，该方法显著优于现有零样本基线，成功率提升10.1%，且减少不必要的步骤。
Adaptive Volumetric Mechanical Property Fields Invariant to Resolution
Authors: Rishit Dagli, Donglai Xiang, Vismay Modi, Xuning Yang, Gavriel State, David I. W. Levin, Maria Shugrina
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-16T17:56:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:56:03+00:00
Comments: Project Page and hi-res paper: https://research.nvidia.com/labs/sil/projects/adavomp/. ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Accurate mechanical properties (or materials) Young's modulus ($E$), Poisson's ratio ($ν$) and density ($ρ$) are essential for reliable physics simulation of digital worlds, but most 3D assets lack this information. We propose AdaVoMP, a method for predicting accurate dense spatially-varying ($E$, $ν$, $ρ$) for input 3D objects across representations, improving the resolution, accuracy, and memory efficiency over the state-of-the-art. The foundation of our technique is a sparse and adaptive voxel structure SAV that efficiently represents both the input 3D shape and the material field output. We replace the fixed-voxel model of the most accurate prior method, VoMP, with a novel sparse transformer encoder-decoder model that learns to generate a unique SAV autoregressively for every input shape to represent its materials, achieving a resolution $16^3\times$ higher than prior art. Experiments show that AdaVoMP estimates more accurate volumetric properties, even with lesser test-time compute than all prior art. This allows us to convert high-resolution complex 3D objects into simulation-ready assets, resulting in realistic deformable simulations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自适应体素化力学属性场，分辨率无关
准确的力学属性（或材料）杨氏模量（$E$）、泊松比（$ν$）和密度（$ρ$）对数字世界的可靠物理模拟至关重要，但大多数3D资产缺乏这些信息。我们提出AdaVoMP，一种用于预测输入3D对象在不同表示下准确密集空间变化的（$E$，$ν$，$ρ$）的方法，相比现有最先进方法，在分辨率、精度和内存效率上均有提升。该技术的基础是一种稀疏且自适应的体素结构SAV，能高效表示输入3D形状和材料场输出。我们用一种新颖的稀疏Transformer编码器-解码器模型替代了最先进方法VoMP的固定体素模型，该模型能自回归地为每个输入形状生成独特的SAV，以表示其材料，实现比前人工作高$16^3$倍的分辨率。实验表明，AdaVoMP即使在测试时计算量更少的情况下，仍能估计出更准确的体素属性。这使我们能够将高分辨率复杂3D对象转换为可进行模拟的资产，从而实现逼真的可变形模拟。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of accurate, spatially-varying mechanical properties in most 3D assets, this work introduces AdaVoMP, a method that predicts dense volumetric fields of Young’s modulus, Poisson’s ratio, and density across 3D object representations. It employs a sparse and adaptive voxel structure (SAV) paired with a novel sparse transformer encoder-decoder to generate material fields autoregressively for each input shape, achieving 16^3 higher resolution than prior methods. Experimental results demonstrate that AdaVoMP delivers more accurate mechanical property estimates while requiring less test-time computation, enabling realistic deformable simulations from high-resolution 3D models.
为解决3D资产缺乏精确空间变化材料属性的问题，AdaVoMP提出一种稀疏自适应体素结构（SAV）与新型稀疏Transformer编码器-解码器模型相结合的方法，该模型可自回归生成每个输入形状对应的材料场。该方法替代了先前方法中固定的体素模型，实现高达16^3倍的分辨率提升，同时提高精度并降低内存占用。实验表明，AdaVoMP在计算开销更少的情况下，对杨氏模量、泊松比和密度的估计精度优于现有方法，可将高分辨率3D对象转化为适用于物理模拟的资产，实现逼真的可变形模拟。
MAJIC: Leveraging Articulatory Motion for Speech-based Emotion Recognition
Authors: Tanmay Srivastava, Paras Bhavnani, Benjir Alvee Islam, Shubham Jain
First: 2026-06-16T17:53:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:53:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce MAJIC, a multimodal emotion recognition system that leverages articulatory motion of the jaw and facial muscles for speech-based emotion recognition (SER). While most SER systems perform well on datasets with strongly expressed emotional speech of trained actors, their performance often degrades when emotional expressions become more subtle. We explore this challenge by engineering features from articulatory motion and integrating them with audio features using a multi-task learning framework. Our key insight is that emotion in speech manifests not only through vocal characteristics but also through distinct articulatory motions: jaw movements, facial muscle vibrations, and speech-induced vibrations. While audio captures features such as pitch and prosody, articulatory motion contains complementary information that is not present in audio alone. We evaluate our system on data collected from 20 participants across multiple sessions, 10 languages, and diverse scenarios, including prompted and conversational speech, showing its robustness across users and settings. MAJIC achieves 93% accuracy and 91% F1 score for emotion classification, outperforming strong audio-based baselines on our dataset.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MAJIC：利用发音器官运动进行基于语音的情感识别
我们提出了MAJIC，一种利用下颌和面部肌肉运动的多模态情感识别系统，用于基于语音的情感识别（SER）。虽然大多数SER系统在演员强烈表达情感的语料上表现良好，但当情感表达变得微妙时，其性能常会下降。我们通过从发音器官运动中提取特征，并结合音频特征，采用多任务学习框架解决这一挑战。我们的核心见解是：语音中的情感不仅通过声学特征体现，也通过独特的发音器官运动体现，包括下颌运动、面部肌肉振动和语音诱导的振动。音频捕捉音高和语调等特征，而发音器官运动则包含音频无法提供的互补信息。我们在20名参与者、多个会话、10种语言和多样化场景（包括提示性与对话式语音）的数据上评估系统，显示其在用户和环境上的鲁棒性。MAJIC在情感分类上达到93%准确率和91%F1分数，优于我们数据集上的强音频基线模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of audio-only systems in recognizing subtle emotional expressions, MAJIC integrates articulatory motion features—such as jaw and facial muscle movements—with audio inputs using a multi-task learning framework. The method captures complementary emotional cues not present in sound alone, including speech-induced vibrations and muscle dynamics. Evaluated across 20 participants, 10 languages, and varied speech contexts, MAJIC achieved 93% accuracy and 91% F1 score, outperforming audio-based baselines and demonstrating robustness across users and settings.
为解决音频系统在识别细微情绪表达时性能下降的问题，MAJIC通过多任务学习框架将下颌与面部肌肉运动特征与音频特征融合，以增强基于语音的情绪识别能力。该方法捕捉了音频无法体现的物理发声过程中的振动与肌肉动态等互补信息。在20名参与者、10种语言及多种语音场景下评估，MAJIC达到93%准确率和91%F1分数，显著优于纯音频基线模型，且在用户与场景间表现稳定。
Learning Red Agent Policy from Observations for Neurosymbolic Autonomous Cyber Agents
Authors: Ankita Samaddar, Sandeep Neema, Daniel Balasubramanian, Xenofon Koutsoukos
First: 2026-06-16T17:50:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:50:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With sophisticated cyber-attacks becoming increasingly prevalent, modern networks require intelligent autonomous cyber-defense agents trained via Reinforcement Learning (RL). These agents employ neurosymbolic approaches such as behavior trees with learning-enabled components (LECs) to learn, reason, adapt, and implement security rules while maintaining critical operations. However, these autonomous networks are partially observable systems, i.e., the cyber-attacker's (red agent's) actions are not observable, making it difficult for the defender to predict red actions, learn red policies, or assess the attacker's intrusion levels. To address this, we propose a Policy Learning Technique using imitation learning to learn policies for partially observable RL agents with discrete states and discrete actions. We apply this technique in an autonomous cyber environment to predict red agent's actions from network observations and defender actions. Integrated with a neurosymbolic cyber-defense agent, our method effectively handles different red policies and achieves high prediction accuracy across diverse simulated scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从观测中学习红方代理策略用于神经符号自主网络代理
随着复杂网络攻击日益普遍，现代网络需要通过强化学习（RL）训练的智能自主网络防御代理。这些代理采用神经符号方法（如带学习能力组件的行为树），以学习、推理、适应并实施安全规则，同时保持关键操作。然而，这些自主网络是部分可观测系统，即红方代理（攻击者）的行为不可观测，导致防御方难以预测红方行动、学习其策略或评估入侵程度。为此，我们提出一种基于模仿学习的策略学习技术，用于学习具有离散状态和离散动作的部分可观测RL代理的策略。我们在自主网络环境中应用该技术，从网络观测和防御方行动中预测红方代理行为。与神经符号网络防御代理集成后，该方法能有效应对不同红方策略，并在多种模拟场景中实现高预测精度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for autonomous cyber-defense systems to counter increasingly sophisticated attacks, this work proposes an imitation learning-based policy learning technique to model the behavior of unseen red agents in partially observable environments. The method leverages network observations and defender actions to infer red agent policies, enabling neurosymbolic defense agents to predict and respond to adversarial moves. Experimental results demonstrate high prediction accuracy across diverse simulated cyber scenarios, effectively handling varied red agent strategies without requiring direct access to attacker actions.
为应对日益复杂的网络攻击，本文提出一种基于模仿学习的策略学习技术，用于在部分可观测环境中建模红方攻击者行为。该方法通过网络观测与防御者动作推断红方策略，从而预测攻击者行动，无需直接访问其内部状态。实验结果表明，该方法在多种模拟网络场景中均能实现高预测准确率，验证了其在神经符号化防御框架下应对多样化红方策略的有效性。
Darshana Graph: A Parallel Commentary Corpus for Comparative Indian Philosophy, with Stylometric and Exploratory Graph Analyses
Authors: Joy Bose
First: 2026-06-16T17:49:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:49:15+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 1 figure. Open Source Code available at https://github.com/joyboseroy/darshana-graph and dataset at https://huggingface.co/datasets/joyboseroy/darshana-graph
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We introduce Darshana Graph, a corpus of over 125,000 text records spanning classical Hindu, Buddhist, and Jain philosophical traditions, drawn from public-domain and openly licensed translations of sources including the Bhagavad Gita, Brahma Sutras, principal Upanishads, the Pali Canon, and core Jain texts. Its distinctive contribution lies in a structurally unique subset of roughly 8,500 Hindu and Jain records in which the same root verse or sutra is aligned across eighteen historical commentators representing five schools of Vedanta and other darshanas, enabling direct comparison of how independent interpretive traditions read identical source material. To our knowledge, no publicly available resource provides comparable cross-commentator alignment at this scale. We present two analyses built on this corpus. First, a transparent stylometric comparison requiring no machine learning measures argumentative style through scriptural citation density, explicit refutation rate, and sentence complexity. It finds a moderate negative correlation between citation density and refutation rate, a marked increase in refutation rate across three commentators in a related doctrinal lineage, and measurable genre-level differences within the Pali Canon itself. Second, we describe a constrained large language model pipeline that extracts typed philosophical relationships between concepts using a predefined relation vocabulary and deterministic post-hoc validation. The resulting graph surfaces cross-school disagreement patterns while also revealing important extraction limitations, including cases where an independent embedding-based analysis disagrees with the graph-derived findings. We release the full corpus, extracted relationship graph, and all source code.
中文标题/摘要
标题：达尔沙纳图谱：用于比较印度哲学的并行注释语料库，附风格分析与探索性图分析
我们介绍了达尔沙纳图谱，一个包含超过12.5万条文本记录的语料库，涵盖古典印度教、佛教和耆那教哲学传统，数据源自公共领域及开放授权翻译的文献，包括《薄伽梵歌》、《梵经》、主要《奥义书》、巴利经典及核心耆那教文献。其独特贡献在于一个结构上独特的子集，约8500条印度教与耆那教文本，其中同一根本经文或偈颂在十八位历史注释者（代表五大学派及其他达尔沙那体系）中对齐，使不同诠释传统对相同源文本的解读得以直接比较。据我们所知，目前尚无公开资源能提供如此规模的跨注释者对齐。我们基于该语料库呈现两项分析：首先，一种透明的风格分析，无需机器学习，通过经文引用密度、明确反驳率及句子复杂度衡量论证风格，发现引用密度与反驳率呈中等负相关，三名相关教义谱系的注释者反驳率显著上升，且巴利经典内部存在可测量的体裁差异；其次，我们描述了一个受限的大语言模型流程，利用预定义的关系词汇表提取概念间类型化哲学关系，并通过确定性后验验证。所得图谱揭示跨学派分歧模式，同时亦揭示重要提取限制，包括某些情况下基于嵌入的独立分析与图谱推导结果不一致。我们发布完整语料库、提取的关系图谱及全部源代码。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a large-scale, cross-tradition comparative resource for Indian philosophy, the authors introduce Darshana Graph, a corpus of over 125,000 text records from Hindu, Buddhist, and Jain sources, with a unique subset of 8,500 aligned verses across 18 commentators. They conduct two analyses: a stylometric study measuring argumentative style via citation density, refutation rate, and sentence complexity, revealing moderate negative correlation between citation and refutation rates, lineage-based refutation trends, and genre differences within the Pali Canon; and a constrained LLM-based graph extraction method that identifies philosophical relationships between concepts, uncovering cross-school disagreements while also highlighting extraction limitations where embedding-based analysis diverges from graph results.
为满足对印度哲学跨传统比较研究的需求，作者构建了Darshana Graph语料库，包含12.5万条文本记录，涵盖印度教、佛教与耆那教经典，其中8500条文本在18位注释者间对齐，便于比较同一经文的不同阐释。通过文体分析，测量引文密度、反驳率与句法复杂度，发现引文密度与反驳率呈中等负相关，相关教派注释者反驳率显著上升，且巴利经典内部存在体裁差异。另采用受限大语言模型提取概念间哲学关系，揭示跨学派分歧模式，但也发现部分结果与嵌入式分析存在矛盾，凸显关系抽取的局限性。
Finite-Time Queue Peak Laws in Stochastic Networks: Logarithmic Scaling After Geometric Thresholds
Authors: Hao Liang, Cheng Tang, Yunzong Xu
First: 2026-06-16T17:47:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:47:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study finite-horizon queue peaks in generalized switches, a standard stochastic-network model in which many queues share constrained service resources. Arrivals may be dependent, time-varying, and adapted to the past; the standing load condition is uniform interior slack, meaning the conditional mean arrival vector stays in a fixed contraction of the capacity region. We show that this slack reshapes the finite-time peak law for drift-minimizing scheduling policies such as MaxWeight. The square-root envelope that is sharp without slack persists only up to a geometry-dependent threshold; beyond that threshold, the running maximum grows only logarithmically with the horizon, both with high probability and in expectation. The mechanism is self-normalization: in the current queue direction, the projected fluctuation scale is normalized by the stabilizing drift scale. This removes capacity geometry from the logarithmic coefficient, while geometry remains in the threshold. Matching lower bounds show that both the logarithmic term and a geometric threshold are unavoidable. When finite-time state-space collapse is available, the threshold can be sharpened using local bottleneck geometry. For generalized input-queued switches, we obtain finite-time peak bounds with tight logarithmic coefficients. Simulations illustrate the two-phase envelope, local geometric refinements, and variance-sensitive improvements predicted by the theory.
中文标题/摘要
标题：随机网络中有限时间队列峰值定律：几何阈值后的对数缩放
我们研究广义交换机中的有限时间队列峰值，这是一种标准的随机网络模型，其中多个队列共享受限的服务资源。到达过程可能具有依赖性、时变性，并适应历史信息；稳态负载条件为均匀内部余量，即条件到达向量的均值始终位于容量区域的一个固定收缩区域内。我们证明，该余量会重塑漂移最小化调度策略（如MaxWeight）的有限时间峰值定律。在无余量情况下尖锐的平方根包络仅在几何依赖阈值内持续存在；超过该阈值后，运行最大值以对数形式随时间跨度增长，且以高概率和期望值均成立。 该机制为自归一化：在当前队列方向上，投影波动尺度由稳定漂移尺度归一化。这消除了对数系数中的容量几何结构，而几何结构仍保留在阈值中。匹配的下界表明，对数项和几何阈值均不可避免。当有限时间状态空间坍缩可用时，可利用局部瓶颈几何结构进一步细化阈值。对于广义输入队列交换机，我们获得了具有紧致对数系数的有限时间峰值界。仿真结果展示了双阶段包络、局部几何细化以及理论预测的方差敏感性改进。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by understanding queue peak behavior in stochastic networks under finite time horizons, the paper analyzes generalized switches where arrivals are time-varying and dependent, yet constrained by uniform interior slack. It demonstrates that drift-minimizing policies like MaxWeight exhibit a two-phase peak envelope: initially growing as a square-root function up to a geometry-dependent threshold, then transitioning to logarithmic growth beyond that threshold. This logarithmic phase arises from self-normalization, where queue fluctuations are scaled relative to stabilizing drift, decoupling the logarithmic coefficient from network geometry while preserving geometry in the threshold. Experimental results confirm this two-phase behavior, show geometric refinements via local bottlenecks, and validate variance-sensitive improvements in peak bounds for input-queued switches.
为理解受限服务资源下随机网络中队列行为，该研究分析了MaxWeight等漂移最小化调度策略在广义交换机中的有限时间峰值规律。研究发现，在均匀内部余量条件下（即到达率保持在容量区域的收缩内），峰值增长从平方根尺度过渡到对数尺度，过渡点由几何结构决定。这一转变源于自归一化机制：队列波动被稳定漂移尺度归一化，使对数系数与容量几何无关，而阈值仍保留几何依赖性。实验结果验证了双阶段包络、局部几何优化及方差敏感性改进，尤其在输入队列交换机中实现了紧致的对数界。
Zone of Proximal Policy Optimization: Teacher in Prompts, Not Gradients
Authors: Byung-Kwan Lee, Ximing Lu, Shizhe Diao, Minki Kang, Saurav Muralidharan, Karan Sapra, Andrew Tao, Pavlo Molchanov, Yejin Choi, Yu-Chiang Frank Wang, Ryo Hachiuma
First: 2026-06-16T17:46:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:46:02+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://byungkwanlee.github.io/ZPPO-page/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Knowledge distillation transfers a teacher's competence to a small student but is brittle in the small-student regime: forcing the student to imitate logits from a much larger teacher concentrates it on the teacher's sharpest modes, hurting generalization on benchmark families beyond the training corpus. Reinforcement learning (RL) avoids logit imitation by training on the student's own rollouts. However, on questions where every rollout fails-yielding zero advantage and being silently discarded-injecting a stronger teacher's response into the policy gradient breaks the on-policy assumption and induces drift. We introduce Zone of Proximal Policy Optimization (ZPPO), inspired by Vygotsky's zone of proximal development, which keeps the teacher inside the prompt rather than the policy gradient. On hard questions, ZPPO constructs two reformulated prompts: a Binary Candidate-included Question (BCQ) pairs one correct teacher response with one incorrect student response as anonymized candidates the student must discriminate, and a Negative Candidate-included Question (NCQ) aggregates the student's wrong rollouts into a single prompt to surface their shared failure modes. A prompt replay buffer recirculates each hard question until it either graduates-the student's mean rollout accuracy on it reaches half- or is FIFO-evicted under finite capacity, amplifying BCQ and NCQ inside the student's current zone of proximal development. On the Qwen3.5 family at four student scales (0.8B-9B) with a 27B teacher, post-trained as vision-language models and evaluated on a 31-benchmark suite (16 VLM, 10 LLM, 5 Video), ZPPO outperforms off/on-policy distillation and GRPO, with the largest gains at the smallest scale.
中文标题/摘要
标题：近端策略优化的区域：提示中的教师，而非梯度
知识蒸馏将教师的能力传递给小型学生模型，但在小型学生模型场景中表现脆弱：强制学生模仿大型教师的logits会使其集中于教师最尖锐的模式，从而损害在训练语料库之外基准数据集上的泛化能力。强化学习（RL）通过在学生自身的rollout上训练避免logits模仿。然而，在所有rollout均失败（产生零优势并被静默丢弃）的问题上，将更强教师的响应注入策略梯度会破坏on-policy假设并引发漂移。我们提出近端策略优化区域（ZPPO），受维果茨基“最近发展区”启发，将教师保留在提示中而非策略梯度内。在困难问题上，ZPPO构建两种重构提示：二元候选问题（BCQ）将一个正确教师回答与一个错误学生回答作为匿名候选，供学生区分；负样本候选问题（NCQ）将学生错误rollout聚合为单个提示，以揭示其共同失败模式。一个提示回放缓冲区循环重放每个困难问题，直至学生在该问题上的平均rollout准确率达到50%（毕业）或因容量有限被FIFO移除，从而在学生当前最近发展区内放大BCQ和NCQ。在Qwen3.5系列四个学生规模（0.8B-9B）与27B教师模型上，经视觉语言模型后训练并在31项基准（16项VLM、10项LLM、5项视频）上评估，ZPPO优于离策略/在线策略蒸馏及GRPO，尤其在最小规模模型上收益最大。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the brittleness of knowledge distillation when transferring from large to small models, this paper proposes Zone of Proximal Policy Optimization (ZPPO), which embeds a teacher’s guidance within prompts rather than policy gradients to avoid gradient drift. ZPPO constructs two prompt types—Binary Candidate-included Question (BCQ) and Negative Candidate-included Question (NCQ)—to help the student discriminate correct from incorrect responses and surface shared failure modes. A replay buffer cycles hard questions until the student achieves 50% accuracy or is evicted, focusing training on the student’s current zone of proximal development. Experiments on Qwen3.5 models across four sizes (0.8B–9B) with a 27B teacher show ZPPO outperforms off-policy, on-policy distillation, and GRPO, with the largest gains observed at the smallest model scale.
为了解决从大模型向小模型迁移知识时知识蒸馏易导致过拟合的问题，本文提出Zone of Proximal Policy Optimization（ZPPO），将教师指导嵌入提示而非策略梯度，避免学生过度聚焦教师的尖锐模式。ZPPO构建两种提示类型——包含正确与错误候选答案的BCQ和聚合学生错误轨迹的NCQ，帮助学生区分正确答案或识别共性失败模式。一个回放缓冲区循环使用困难问题，直到学生在该问题上准确率达到50%或被有限容量淘汰，从而聚焦于学生当前最近发展区。在Qwen3.5系列模型（0.8B–9B）搭配27B教师模型、31项基准测试中，ZPPO显著优于离策略、在线策略蒸馏及GRPO方法，尤其在最小模型规模下收益最大。
Rethinking Dataset Distillation for Classification: Do Distilled Sets Outperform Coresets?
Authors: Trisha Mittal, Akshay Mehra, Joshua Kimball
First: 2026-06-16T17:37:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:37:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Dataset distillation (DD) has emerged as a prominent approach in data centric machine learning, aiming to synthesize compact training sets for efficient training by compressing the information in large datasets into a small number of synthetic samples. However, DD methods are often evaluated under inconsistent evaluation protocols, ranging from standard ERM to single/multi-teacher supervision, making it difficult to isolate the effectiveness of distilled data from evaluation. Moreover, many prior methods claim that DD outperforms data pruning approaches such as coreset selection (CS), based on the assumption that restricting condensed datasets to subsets of real samples fundamentally limits their expressiveness. In this work, we critically evaluate DD methods through large-scale experiments using standardized datasets and evaluation protocols to assess their intrinsic effectiveness. We benchmark seven state-of-the-art (SOTA) DD methods on ImageNet-1K, ImageNet100, and ImageNette, using three widely adopted training protocols against three CS strategies. Our results show that while some DD methods fail to outperform even simple random subsets, the SOTA DD approaches are comparable to or worse than coresets on large-scale datasets and incur a substantially higher cost for construction. Beyond accuracy, we also evaluate the representativeness, diversity, and quality of condensed sets, and find that coresets consistently achieve better coverage of the original data distribution. These findings highlight the limited practical advantages of current DD methods and show that coresets remain competitive and are often a more computationally efficient alternative for data-centric learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重新思考用于分类的数据集蒸馏：蒸馏数据集是否优于核心集？
数据集蒸馏（DD）已成为数据为中心的机器学习中的重要方法，旨在通过将大型数据集中信息压缩为少量合成样本，合成紧凑的训练集以提高训练效率。然而，DD方法常在不一致的评估协议下进行评估，从标准ERM到单/多教师监督，使得难以将蒸馏数据的有效性与评估分离。此外，许多先前方法声称DD优于数据剪枝方法（如核心集选择CS），基于限制浓缩数据集仅限于真实样本子集这一假设，从根本上限制了其表达能力。在本研究中，我们通过大规模实验，使用标准化数据集和评估协议，对DD方法进行批判性评估，以衡量其内在有效性。我们在ImageNet-1K、ImageNet100和ImageNette上对七种最先进的（SOTA）DD方法进行基准测试，采用三种广泛采用的训练协议，对比三种CS策略。结果表明，尽管部分DD方法甚至无法超越简单随机子集，但SOTA DD方法在大规模数据集上与核心集相当或更差，且构建成本显著更高。除准确率外，我们还评估了浓缩数据集的代表性、多样性和质量，发现核心集始终能更好地覆盖原始数据分布。这些发现突显了当前DD方法的有限实用优势，表明核心集仍具竞争力，且通常在数据为中心的学习中是更高效的计算替代方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by inconsistent evaluation practices and inflated claims that dataset distillation (DD) outperforms coreset selection (CS), this study systematically compares seven state-of-the-art DD methods against CS strategies on ImageNet-1K, ImageNet100, and ImageNette using standardized training protocols. The experiments reveal that while some DD methods underperform even random subsets, the best DD approaches are either comparable to or worse than coresets in accuracy, while requiring significantly more computational cost to construct. Additionally, coresets consistently demonstrate superior representativeness, diversity, and coverage of the original data distribution, suggesting that DD offers limited practical advantages over CS and that coresets remain a more efficient and effective alternative for data-centric learning.
鉴于现有研究对数据集蒸馏（DD）与核心集选择（CS）的比较缺乏统一评估标准，本研究在ImageNet-1K、ImageNet100和ImageNette上系统对比了七种前沿DD方法与多种CS策略，采用标准化训练协议。实验表明，部分DD方法甚至不如随机子集，而最优DD方法在准确率上与核心集相当或更差，且构建成本显著更高。此外，核心集在数据分布覆盖、多样性和代表性方面表现更优，说明当前DD方法实际优势有限，核心集仍是更高效且更具竞争力的数据中心学习方案。
Looped World Models
Authors: Hongyuan Adam Lu, Z. L. Victor Wei, Qun Zhang, Jinrui Zeng, Bowen Cao, Lingwei Meng, Mocheng Li, Zezhong Wang, Haonan Yin, Naifu Xue, Minyu Chen, Cenyuan Zhang, Zefan Zhang, Hao Wei, Jiawei Zhou, Haoran Xu, Hao Yang, Ronglai Zuo, Tongda Xu, Yonghao Li, Jian Chen, Hebin Wang, Zeyu Gao, Yang Li, Wei Zhao, Qimin Zhong, Siqi Liu, Yumeng Zhang, Leyan Cui, Zhangyu Wang, Wai Lam
First: 2026-06-16T17:37:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:37:27+00:00
Comments: Technical Report
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current world models face a fundamental tension: faithful long-horizon simulation demands deep computation, but deeper models are expensive to deploy and prone to compounding errors. We resolve this by introducing Looped World Models (LoopWM), which are the first looped architectures for world modelling. Our method iteratively refines latent environment states through a parameter-shared transformer block. This yield up to 100x parameter efficiency over conventional approaches with adaptive computation that automatically scales depth to match the complexity of each prediction step. Orthogonal to scaling model size and training data, LoopWM establishes iterative latent depth as a new scaling axis for world simulation, which might significantly push the community forward.
中文标题/摘要
标题：循环世界模型
当前世界模型面临根本性矛盾：忠实的长时程模拟需要深度计算，但更深层模型部署成本高昂且易累积误差。我们通过引入循环世界模型（LoopWM），首次提出循环架构用于世界建模。该方法通过参数共享的Transformer模块迭代优化潜在环境状态，相较传统方法实现高达100倍的参数效率，并具备自适应计算能力，自动根据每个预测步骤的复杂度调整深度。与扩展模型规模和训练数据量不同，LoopWM将迭代潜在深度确立为世界模拟的新扩展轴，有望显著推动该领域发展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-off between accurate long-horizon simulation and computational cost in current world models, this paper introduces Looped World Models (LoopWM), the first looped architecture for world modeling. LoopWM iteratively refines latent environment states using a parameter-shared transformer block, enabling adaptive computation that scales depth according to prediction complexity. Experimental results show up to 100x parameter efficiency compared to conventional methods, while maintaining simulation fidelity, demonstrating that iterative latent depth can serve as a new scaling axis independent of model size or training data.
为解决世界模型中计算成本与仿真保真度之间的矛盾，本文提出首个循环架构Looped World Models（LoopWM）。该方法通过共享参数的Transformer模块迭代优化潜在环境状态，实现按预测步骤自适应调整深度的计算。实验表明，相比传统方法效率提升最高达100倍，同时保持或提升仿真精度，证明迭代潜在深度可作为独立于模型规模与训练数据的新扩展维度。
Fixed-Point Reasoners: Stable and Adaptive Deep Looped Transformers
Authors: Sajad Movahedi, Vera Milovanović, Shlomo Libo Feigin, Alexander Theus, Thomas Hofmann, Valentina Boeva, T. Konstantin Rusch, Antonio Orvieto
First: 2026-06-16T17:36:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:36:33+00:00
Comments: Code available at https://github.com/nilskiKonjIzDunava/fprm
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Looped architectures provide an inductive bias toward learning step-by-step procedures for tasks that require compositional reasoning. The number of effective layers reached by looping determines the quality of the solution these models find. Like deep architectures, looped architectures are prone to a signal propagation problem induced by depth as the halting decision is postponed. In this paper, we address this signal propagation issue using pre-norm layers and residual scaling. Building on these architectural modifications, we propose FPRM, a Transformer-based Fixed-Point Reasoning Model that uses fixed-point convergence as an end-to-end halting mechanism in a looped architecture. We show that fixed-point halting allows FPRM to adapt its compute to task difficulty. FPRM is effective on common reasoning benchmarks, namely Sudoku, Maze, state-tracking, and ARC-AGI.
中文标题/摘要
标题：定点推理器：稳定且自适应的深度循环Transformer
循环架构为学习需要组合推理的任务提供了逐步执行的归纳偏置。循环所达到的有效层数决定了这些模型找到的解的质量。与深度架构类似，循环架构也容易因深度导致信号传播问题，因为终止决策被推迟。本文通过预归一化层和残差缩放解决该信号传播问题。在此基础上，我们提出FPRM，一种基于Transformer的定点推理模型，利用定点收敛作为循环架构中的端到端终止机制。我们表明，定点终止机制使FPRM能够根据任务难度自适应调整计算量。FPRM在常见的推理基准（如数独、迷宫、状态跟踪和ARC-AGI）上表现有效。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for stable and adaptive reasoning in compositional tasks, this paper introduces FPRM, a looped Transformer architecture that uses fixed-point convergence as an end-to-end halting mechanism to overcome signal propagation issues inherent in deep looped models. By incorporating pre-norm layers and residual scaling, the model stabilizes gradient flow and dynamically adjusts computation based on task complexity. Experiments demonstrate that FPRM achieves competitive performance across reasoning benchmarks including Sudoku, Maze, state-tracking, and ARC-AGI, consistently outperforming or matching baselines while maintaining computational efficiency.
为解决组合推理任务中稳定且自适应推理的需求，本文提出FPRM模型，该模型基于Transformer架构，在循环结构中以固定点收敛作为端到端的终止机制。为缓解深度循环结构中固有的信号传播问题，模型引入了前置归一化层和残差缩放。实验表明，FPRM能根据任务难度动态调整计算量，并在包括数独、迷宫、状态追踪和ARC-AGI在内的多个推理基准上表现优异。
Chain-of-Thought Reasoning In The Wild Is Not Always Faithful
Authors: Iván Arcuschin, Jett Janiak, Robert Krzyzanowski, Senthooran Rajamanoharan, Neel Nanda, Arthur Conmy
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-03-11T17:56:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:36:22+00:00
Comments: Published at the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent studies indicate that when faced with explicit biases in prompts, models often omit mentioning these biases in their Chain-of-Thought (CoT) output, revealing that verbalized reasoning can give an incorrect picture of how models arrive at conclusions (unfaithfulness). In this work, we show that unfaithful CoT also occurs on naturally worded, non-adversarial prompts without adding artificial biases or editing model outputs. We find that when separately presented with the questions "Is X bigger than Y?" and "Is Y bigger than X?", models sometimes produce superficially coherent arguments to justify systematically answering Yes to both or No to both, despite the contradiction. We present preliminary evidence that this is due to models' implicit biases towards Yes or No, labeling this Implicit Post-Hoc Rationalization. Our results reveal rates up to 13% for production models, and while frontier models are more faithful, none are entirely so, including thinking models like DeepSeek R1 (0.37%) and Sonnet 3.7 with thinking (0.04%). We also investigate Unfaithful Illogical Shortcuts, where models use subtly illogical reasoning to make speculative answers to hard math problems seem rigorously proven. Our findings indicate that while CoT can be useful for assessing outputs, it is not a complete account of the internal process that produced the model's answer and should be used with caution in agentic or safety-critical settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：野外的思维链推理并不总是忠实的
近期研究表明，当面对提示中的显式偏见时，模型常在思维链（CoT）输出中省略提及这些偏见，表明口头化推理可能错误地呈现模型得出结论的方式（不忠实）。本研究显示，即使在自然措辞、非对抗性提示下，无需人工添加偏见或编辑模型输出，CoT仍会出现不忠实现象。我们发现，当分别提问“X是否比Y大？”和“Y是否比X大？”时，模型有时会生成表面连贯但系统性矛盾的论证，始终回答“是”或始终回答“否”。我们初步发现，这是由于模型对“是”或“否”的隐含偏好，称之为“隐式事后合理化”。结果表明，生产模型的不忠实率高达13%，前沿模型虽更忠实，但无一完全忠实，包括DeepSeek R1（0.37%）和Sonnet 3.7带思考功能（0.04%）。我们还研究了“不忠实非逻辑捷径”，即模型用微妙非逻辑推理使困难数学问题的推测答案看似严谨证明。研究发现，尽管CoT有助于评估输出，但无法完全反映模型生成答案的内部过程，应在代理或安全关键场景中谨慎使用。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how language models' Chain-of-Thought reasoning can be unfaithful even on natural, non-adversarial prompts, revealing that models sometimes generate internally inconsistent yet superficially coherent justifications. The researchers demonstrate this through experiments where models are asked contradictory comparison questions, often producing contradictory yet logically flawed answers due to implicit biases toward 'Yes' or 'No', termed 'Implicit Post-Hoc Rationalization'. They also identify 'Unfaithful Illogical Shortcuts', where models use subtly illogical reasoning to appear rigorous in solving hard math problems. Results show up to 13% unfaithfulness in production models, with frontier models like DeepSeek R1 (0.37%) and Sonnet 3.7 with thinking (0.04%) still exhibiting some unfaithfulness, indicating that CoT is not a reliable proxy for internal reasoning and should be used cautiously in safety-critical applications.
本研究发现语言模型在自然非对抗性提示下，其思维链（CoT）推理也可能不忠实，表现为对矛盾问题给出看似合理实则自相矛盾的解释，归因于模型隐含的倾向性，称为‘隐式事后合理化’。研究还发现模型常使用微妙不合逻辑的推理方式，使对难题的猜测答案显得有据可依，称为‘不忠实非逻辑捷径’。实验显示主流模型存在高达13%的不忠实率，前沿模型如DeepSeek R1（0.37%）和Sonnet 3.7带思考（0.04%）仍存在部分不忠实，表明CoT不能完全反映模型内部推理过程，安全关键场景下应谨慎使用。
Regression Language Models for Code
Authors: Yash Akhauri, Xingyou Song, Arissa Wongpanich, Bryan Lewandowski, Mohamed S. Abdelfattah
Venue: ICML
First: 2025-09-30T16:25:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:36:05+00:00
Comments: Published in International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study code-to-metric regression: predicting numeric outcomes of code executions, a challenging task due to the open-ended nature of programming languages. While prior methods have resorted to heavy and domain-specific feature engineering, we show that a single unified Regression Language Model (RLM) using a frozen LLM encoder can simultaneously predict directly from text, (i) the memory footprint of code across multiple high-level languages such as Python and C++, (ii) the latency of Triton GPU kernels, and (iii) the accuracy and speed of trained neural networks represented in ONNX. In particular, a relatively small 300M parameter RLM based on T5Gemma, obtains >0.9 Spearman-rank on competitive programming submissions from APPS, and a single unified model achieves >0.5 average Spearman-rank across 24 different programming languages from CodeNet. Furthermore, the RLM can obtain the highest average Kendall-Tau of 0.46 on five classic NAS design spaces previously dominated by graph neural networks, and simultaneously predict architecture latencies on numerous hardware platforms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于代码的回归语言模型
我们研究代码到度量的回归：预测代码执行的数值结果，这是一个由于编程语言开放性而具有挑战性的任务。尽管先前方法依赖于复杂且领域特定的特征工程，我们表明，使用冻结的LLM编码器的单一统一回归语言模型（RLM）能够直接从文本同时预测：（i）Python和C++等多高级语言代码的内存占用，（ii）Triton GPU内核的延迟，以及（iii）以ONNX格式表示的训练神经网络的准确性和速度。特别是，基于T5Gemma的相对较小的300M参数RLM，在APPS竞赛提交的代码上获得了>0.9的Spearman秩相关系数，且单一统一模型在CodeNet的24种不同编程语言上平均Spearman秩相关系数>0.5。此外，RLM在五个此前由图神经网络主导的经典NAS设计空间上获得了最高的平均Kendall-Tau值0.46，并能同时预测多种硬件平台上的架构延迟。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of predicting numeric outcomes from code execution without heavy feature engineering, the authors propose a unified Regression Language Model (RLM) using a frozen LLM encoder to directly predict metrics such as memory footprint, GPU kernel latency, and neural network accuracy/speed. The model, based on a 300M parameter T5Gemma architecture, achieves over 0.9 Spearman-rank on APPS programming submissions and over 0.5 average Spearman-rank across 24 languages from CodeNet. It also outperforms graph neural networks on five NAS design spaces with a Kendall-Tau of 0.46 and can simultaneously predict latencies across multiple hardware platforms.
为解决无需复杂特征工程即可从代码预测数值结果的难题，作者提出一种统一的回归语言模型（RLM），该模型基于冻结的LLM编码器直接预测内存占用、GPU内核延迟和神经网络精度/速度等指标。基于300M参数T5Gemma架构的模型在APPS编程提交数据上Spearman排名超过0.9，在CodeNet的24种语言上平均Spearman排名超过0.5，同时在五个经典NAS设计空间中以0.46的平均Kendall-Tau超越图神经网络，并能同时预测多种硬件平台上的架构延迟。
Analyzing and Encoding the Al-Mawrid Arabic-English Dictionary with the ISO Language Markup Framework and TEI Lex-0
Authors: Diaa Fayed, Laurent Romary
First: 2026-06-16T17:35:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:35:11+00:00
Comments: 44 pages, 58 figures, 12 tables. Submitted to Language Resources and Evaluation, under review since Aug 2025, round 3
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a robust methodology for the systematic digitization and encoding of the Al-Mawrid Arabic-English dictionary, transforming it from a legacy print resource into a standardized computational lexicon. Addressing a significant gap in Arabic lexical infrastructure, the study adopts a dual-standard framing that aligns the ISO Lexical Markup Framework (LMF) with the Text Encoding Initiative TEI Lex-0 guidelines. By applying an editorial view to the dictionary's macro- and microstructure, the research resolves the structural ambiguities and punctuation inconsistencies typical of 20th-century bilingual dictionaries. The methodology is grounded in an empirical analysis of the dictionary's lexical knowledge density. Drawing on a representative sample (the letter Ayn, comprising 4.6% of the total volume), the study provides scientific weight to the encoding process, demonstrating a structural parsing accuracy of 91%. Quantitative evaluation of the information extraction rules reveals high performance, with 85% precision and 98% recall for synonyms, and 88% precision for other morpho-semantic features. Beyond technical description, the paper provides a critical comparison with existing Arabic lexical resources and discusses the limitations of TEI Lex-0 when modelling specific Arabic phenomena, such as implicit "open set" semantic relations and scattered morphological cues. Furthermore, the study explores the potential for Linguistic Linked Open Data (LLOD) integration by establishing a scalable prefix-based referencing system that facilitates the resource's inclusion in the semantic web. The result is an interoperable, machine-tractable resource that provides a reproducible workflow for the retro-digitization of complex legacy bilingual lexicons within the Arabic NLP and Digital Humanities communities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用ISO语言标记框架和TEI Lex-0对《阿尔·马瓦里德》阿拉伯语-英语词典进行分析与编码
本文提出了一种稳健的方法，用于系统性地数字化和编码《阿尔·马瓦里德》阿拉伯语-英语词典，将其从传统印刷资源转变为标准化的计算词典。为填补阿拉伯语词汇基础设施的重大空白，本研究采用双标准框架，将ISO词汇标记框架（LMF）与文本编码倡议TEI Lex-0指南相结合。通过编辑视角处理词典的宏观与微观结构，研究解决了20世纪双语词典常见的结构模糊性和标点不一致问题。该方法基于对词典词汇知识密度的实证分析。以占总量4.6%的字母“艾因”作为代表性样本，研究为编码过程提供了科学依据，结构解析准确率达91%。信息提取规则的定量评估显示高性能：同义词的精确度85%、召回率98%，其他形态语义特征的精确度88%。除技术描述外，本文还对现有阿拉伯语词汇资源进行了批判性比较，并讨论了TEI Lex-0在建模特定阿拉伯语现象（如隐含的“开放集”语义关系和分散的形态线索）时的局限性。此外，研究探索了通过建立可扩展的前缀引用系统，实现与语言开放链接数据（LLOD）的整合，以促进该资源在语义网中的应用。最终成果是一个可互操作、机器可处理的资源，为阿拉伯语自然语言处理和数字人文领域复杂传统双语词典的逆向数字化提供了可复现的工作流程。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to digitize and standardize the legacy Al-Mawrid Arabic-English dictionary for computational use, this study employs a dual-standard encoding framework combining ISO LMF and TEI Lex-0 to resolve structural ambiguities and punctuation inconsistencies. Through empirical analysis of a representative sample (the letter Ayn, 4.6% of the dictionary), the method achieves 91% structural parsing accuracy and demonstrates high performance in extracting lexical features: 85% precision and 98% recall for synonyms, and 88% precision for other morpho-semantic relations. The work also critically evaluates TEI Lex-0’s limitations in modeling Arabic-specific phenomena like implicit semantic relations and scattered morphology, while proposing a prefix-based referencing system to enable integration into Linguistic Linked Open Data. The result is a machine-readable, interoperable lexicon with a reproducible workflow for retro-digitizing complex bilingual dictionaries.
为解决阿拉伯语双语词典数字化与标准化的迫切需求，本研究采用ISO LMF与TEI Lex-0双标准框架，解决传统词典结构模糊与标点不一致的问题。通过对字母“艾因”（占全书4.6%）的实证分析，方法实现91%的结构解析准确率，并在词义提取方面表现优异：同义词提取精度85%、召回率98%，其他形态语义特征精度88%。论文同时批判性评估了TEI Lex-0在建模阿拉伯语特有现象（如隐含语义关系与分散形态线索）方面的局限性，并提出基于前缀的引用体系以支持语义网中的LLOD整合，最终产出一个可机器处理、可互操作的阿拉伯语词典资源，适用于阿拉伯语自然语言处理与数字人文领域。
RubricsTree: Scalable and Evolving Open-Ended Evaluation of Personal Health Agents across Health Memory and Medical Skills
Authors: Weizhi Zhang, Zechen Li, Hamid Palangi, Ben Graef, A. Ali Heydari, Simon A. Lee, Salman Rahman, Ray Luo, Zeinab Esmaeilpour, Erik Schenck, Chloe Zhang, Yamin Li, Menglian Zhou, Philip S. Yu, Daniel McDuff, Lindsey Sunden, Mark Malhotra, Shwetak Patel, Ahmed A. Metwally
First: 2026-06-16T17:34:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:34:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The LLM-empowered personal health agents with user health (sensor) metrics have offered a promising pathway to alleviate global disparities in healthcare access. However, large-scale clinical deployment remains constrained by an open-ended evaluation bottleneck: physician annotation is reliable but costly and unscalable, while LLM-as-a-judge evaluators are scalable but subjective, inconsistent, and sometimes clinically misaligned. We introduce RubricsTree, a scalable evaluation framework with an expert-aligned hierarchical taxonomy of over 100 atomic, clinically-verifiable Boolean rubrics, evolving from the insights of 4,000 real user queries through an iterative human-in-the-loop curation protocol with an expertise panel led by an experienced physician. A context-aware adaptive router activates only the relevant auto-weighted rubric subset per query, providing the throughput needed for scalable evaluation with expert-aligned quality. Through a systematic meta-evaluation, we show that RubricsTree (i) substantially exceeds a strong large-scale evaluation baseline in expert alignment on challenging open-ended queries; (ii) reliably penalizes contextually degraded responses; and (iii) when used as structured instructions, text feedback, or training rewards for performance optimization, yields up to ~66% relative gains on HealthBench for Gemini, GPT, and Qwen model families. RubricsTree thus provides a scalable, auditable, and evolving evaluation infrastructure required for the continuous optimization of product-level personal healthcare AI.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RubricsTree：面向个人健康代理的可扩展且持续演进的开放式评估框架，涵盖健康记忆与医学技能
依托大语言模型的个人健康代理结合用户健康（传感器）数据，为缓解全球医疗资源不平等提供了有前景的路径。然而，大规模临床部署受限于开放式评估瓶颈：医生标注可靠但成本高且不可扩展，而LLM作为裁判的评估器虽可扩展但主观、不一致，有时与临床标准脱节。我们提出RubricsTree，一个可扩展评估框架，包含超过100个原子化、临床可验证的布尔评估标准，构建于4000个真实用户查询的洞察之上，通过由资深医生领导的专家小组参与的迭代人机协同校准流程形成层级分类体系。上下文感知的自适应路由器根据每个查询激活相关自动加权的评估子集，提供可扩展评估所需的吞吐量，同时确保专家对齐的质量。通过系统性元评估，我们证明RubricsTree：(i) 在具有挑战性的开放式查询上显著优于强大规模评估基线，专家对齐度更高；(ii) 可可靠惩罚上下文退化响应；(iii) 当作为结构化指令、文本反馈或性能优化训练奖励时，可为Gemini、GPT和Qwen模型家族在HealthBench上带来高达66%的相对性能提升。RubricsTree因此提供了一种可扩展、可审计且持续演进的评估基础设施，支持产品级个人医疗AI的持续优化。
