Driven by the need to evaluate personal health agents at scale while maintaining clinical accuracy, RubricsTree introduces a hierarchical taxonomy of over 100 Boolean rubrics curated from 4,000 real user queries and expert physician input. It employs a context-aware router to activate only relevant rubrics per query, enabling scalable yet expert-aligned evaluation. Experiments show RubricsTree outperforms existing baselines in expert alignment, penalizes degraded responses reliably, and boosts model performance by up to 66% when used as feedback or training signals for Gemini, GPT, and Qwen models.
为解决个人健康代理大规模评估中专家可靠性与可扩展性之间的矛盾，RubricsTree构建了包含100多个临床可验证布尔指标的分层评估体系，基于4000个真实用户查询和专家医师团队的迭代校准。系统通过上下文感知路由机制动态激活相关子集指标，实现高效可扩展评估。实验表明，RubricsTree在复杂开放问题上显著优于现有大规模评估基线，能有效惩罚上下文退化响应，并作为反馈或训练信号时，可使Gemini、GPT和Qwen系列模型性能提升达66%。
Principled RL for Flow Matching Emerges from the Chunk-level Policy Optimization
Authors: Yifu Luo, Haoyuan Sun, Xinhao Hu, Penghui Du, Keyu Fan, Bo Li, Sinan Du, Xu Wan, Zhiyu Chen, Bo Xia, Yongzhe Chang, Changqian Yu, Kun Gai, Tiantian Zhang, Xueqian Wang
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-10-24T15:50:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:34:16+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent Progress in post-training flow matching for text-to-image (T2I) generation with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has demonstrated strong potential. However, it is hindered by a critical limitation: inaccurate advantage attribution. In this work, we argue that aggregating consecutive steps into a coherent 'chunk' and shifting the policy optimization paradigm from GRPO's step level to the chunk level can effectively mitigate the negative impact of this issue. Building on this insight, we propose Group Chunking Policy Optimization (GCPO), the first chunk-level reinforcement learning approach for post-training flow matching. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GCPO achieves superior performance on both standard T2I benchmarks and preference alignment, with up to 43% relative gains over GRPO, highlighting the promise of chunk-level policy optimization. The code is available on https://github.com/xingzhejun/GCPO.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于原则的流匹配强化学习源于块级策略优化
近期在文本到图像（T2I）生成的后训练流匹配中，基于组相对策略优化（GRPO）的方法展现了强大潜力，但受限于关键缺陷：优势归因不准确。本文认为，将连续步骤聚合为连贯的“块”，并将策略优化范式从GRPO的步级转向块级，可有效缓解该问题。基于此见解，我们提出组块策略优化（GCPO），这是首个用于后训练流匹配的块级强化学习方法。大量实验表明，GCPO在标准T2I基准和偏好对齐任务上均表现更优，相较GRPO最高提升43%，凸显了块级策略优化的前景。代码见https://github.com/xingzhejun/GCPO。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of inaccurate advantage attribution in step-level GRPO for post-training flow matching in text-to-image generation, this work proposes Group Chunking Policy Optimization (GCPO), the first chunk-level reinforcement learning method, which aggregates consecutive steps into coherent 'chunks' to refine policy updates. Experimental results show GCPO outperforms GRPO on standard T2I benchmarks and preference alignment tasks, achieving up to 43% relative gains, demonstrating the effectiveness of chunk-level policy optimization.
为了解决步级GRPO在文本到图像生成中因优势归因不准确导致的性能瓶颈，本文提出首个基于块级策略优化的Group Chunking Policy Optimization（GCPO）方法。通过将连续步骤聚合为语义连贯的策略块，GCPO将优化粒度从单步提升至块级，从而缓解优势估计的噪声与偏差。实验表明，GCPO在标准T2I基准与偏好对齐任务上均优于GRPO，最高提升达43%。
On Surjectivity of Neural Networks: Can you elicit any behavior from your model?
Authors: Haozhe Jiang, Nika Haghtalab
First: 2025-08-26T21:36:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:34:06+00:00
Comments: Blog: https://astro-eric.github.io/blogs/surjective/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Given a trained neural network, can any specified output be generated by some input? Equivalently, does the network correspond to a function that is surjective? In generative models, surjectivity implies that any output, including harmful or undesirable content, can in principle be generated by the networks, raising concerns about model safety and jailbreak vulnerabilities. In this paper, we prove that many fundamental building blocks of modern neural architectures, such as networks with pre-layer normalization and linear-attention modules, are almost always surjective. As corollaries, widely used generative frameworks, including GPT-style transformers and diffusion models with deterministic ODE solvers, admit inverse mappings for arbitrary outputs. By studying surjectivity of these modern and commonly used neural architectures, we contribute a formalism that sheds light on their unavoidable vulnerability to a broad class of adversarial attacks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：神经网络的满射性：你能否从模型中诱导出任何行为？
给定一个训练好的神经网络，是否能通过某些输入生成任何指定的输出？等价地，该网络是否对应一个满射函数？在生成模型中，满射性意味着任何输出（包括有害或不期望的内容）原则上都可以由网络生成，从而引发模型安全性和越狱漏洞的担忧。本文证明，现代神经架构中的许多基本构建模块（如预层归一化网络和线性注意力模块）几乎总是满射的。作为推论，广泛使用的生成框架（包括GPT风格的Transformer和使用确定性ODE求解器的扩散模型）对任意输出均存在逆映射。通过研究这些现代且常用神经架构的满射性，我们贡献了一种形式化方法，揭示了它们对广泛类对抗攻击的不可避免的脆弱性。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates whether trained neural networks can generate any desired output, framing the question as whether the network's mapping is surjective. Motivated by safety concerns in generative models—such as the potential to produce harmful content—the authors prove that common architectural components like pre-layer normalization and linear-attention modules are almost always surjective. As a result, widely used models including GPT-style transformers and diffusion models with deterministic ODE solvers admit inverse mappings for any output, revealing an inherent vulnerability to adversarial attacks that cannot be avoided through architectural design alone.
本文探讨训练好的神经网络是否能生成任意指定输出，即其映射是否为满射。出于对生成模型安全性的担忧——如可能生成有害内容——作者证明了现代神经网络常见组件（如预层归一化和线性注意力模块）几乎总是满射。因此，广泛使用的模型如GPT风格的Transformer和基于确定性ODE求解器的扩散模型，对任意输出都存在逆映射，揭示了其固有的对抗攻击易感性，这种脆弱性无法通过架构设计规避。
SkillJect: Effectively Automating Skill-Based Prompt Injection for Skill-Enabled Agents
Authors: Xiaojun Jia, Jie Liao, Simeng Qin, Jindong Gu, Wenqi Ren, Xiaochun Cao, Yang Liu, Philip Torr
First: 2026-02-15T16:09:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:33:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agent skills extend LLM agents with task-specific instructions, executable scripts, and auxiliary resources, improving reusability but creating a new supply-chain attack surface. A malicious or compromised skill can be repeatedly loaded as trusted guidance and steer downstream tool use. Existing skill-based prompt-injection attacks are often manual and brittle, because explicit malicious instructions are rejected or ignored when they are not aligned with the original workflow. We propose SkillJect, the first automated framework for generating poisoned skills against skill-enabled agent systems. SkillJect uses two coordinated channels. In the artifact channel, it hides the payload inside an auxiliary helper script. In the instruction channel, it rewrites SKILL.md with a front-loaded inducement strategy, placing injected content at the beginning and framing the helper script as a mandatory prerequisite or initialization step. The rewritten instruction explicitly references the helper-script path and provides an executable example command, making the helper appear to be a legitimate setup step before normal skill operations. SkillJect further adopts a closed-loop multi-agent process to improve attack effectiveness. An Attack Agent generates poisoned skills, a Victim Agent executes downstream tasks with the poisoned skill, and an Evaluate Agent inspects execution traces to determine whether the hidden payload was executed. The Attack Agent then uses this feedback to diagnose failure causes and rewrite SKILL.md, while keeping the payload fixed. Experiments across skill-enabled platforms, backend LLMs, and attack categories show that SkillJect substantially outperforms naive direct injection and prior manual skill-injection attacks, highlighting poisoned skills as a persistent threat in reusable skill ecosystems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SkillJect：有效自动化基于技能的提示注入以针对具备技能的代理系统
代理技能通过任务特定指令、可执行脚本和辅助资源扩展大语言模型代理，提升可重用性，但也引入了新的供应链攻击面。恶意或被攻陷的技能可被反复加载为可信指导，引导下游工具使用。现有基于技能的提示注入攻击多为手动且脆弱，因当恶意指令与原始工作流不一致时，常被拒绝或忽略。我们提出SkillJect，首个针对具备技能代理系统的自动化框架，用于生成被污染的技能。SkillJect采用两个协同通道：在产物通道中，将载荷隐藏于辅助帮助脚本内；在指令通道中，重写SKILL.md文件，采用前置诱导策略，将注入内容置于开头，并将帮助脚本描述为必需的前置或初始化步骤。重写后的指令明确引用帮助脚本路径并提供可执行示例命令，使帮助脚本看似正常技能操作前的合法设置步骤。SkillJect进一步采用闭环多代理流程提升攻击效果：攻击代理生成被污染技能，受害者代理使用被污染技能执行下游任务，评估代理检查执行轨迹以判断隐藏载荷是否被执行。攻击代理据此反馈诊断失败原因并重写SKILL.md，同时保持载荷不变。在具备技能平台、后端大语言模型及攻击类别上的实验表明，SkillJect显著优于直接注入和先前手动技能注入攻击，凸显被污染技能在可重用技能生态系统中的持续威胁。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the security risks posed by reusable skill systems in LLM agents, where malicious skills can be repeatedly loaded and influence downstream tool usage, the paper introduces SkillJect, the first automated framework for generating poisoned skills. SkillJect employs two coordinated channels: an artifact channel that embeds payloads in helper scripts, and an instruction channel that rewrites SKILL.md to front-load inducements and frame the helper script as a mandatory setup step, complete with executable examples. A closed-loop multi-agent system—comprising Attack, Victim, and Evaluate Agents—iteratively refines the attack by diagnosing failures and adjusting instructions while preserving the payload. Experiments across multiple platforms and LLMs demonstrate that SkillJect significantly outperforms manual and naive injection methods, confirming poisoned skills as a persistent and potent threat in skill-based agent ecosystems.
为应对可复用技能带来的安全风险，SkillJect提出自动化生成恶意技能以绕过防御机制。其采用双通道策略：通过辅助脚本隐藏恶意载荷（artifact channel），并通过重写SKILL.md文件，将脚本伪装成必要前置步骤并提供可执行示例（instruction channel）。系统采用闭环多智能体架构——攻击者、受害者与评估者协同迭代优化注入策略，保持载荷不变。实验表明，SkillJect在多个平台与大模型上显著优于手动或直接注入方法，验证了恶意技能在技能型代理生态中的持续威胁性。
From Theory to Application: A Practical Introduction to Neural Operators in Scientific Computing
Authors: Prashant K. Jha
First: 2025-03-07T17:25:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:32:54+00:00
Comments: 72 pages, 22 figures, GitHub repository: https://github.com/CEADpx/neural_operators
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This review examines neural operator architectures for learning solution operators of parametric partial differential equations (PDEs), with an emphasis on conceptual clarity and practical implementation. The work analyzes key models, including DeepONet, PCANet, and the Fourier Neural Operator, highlighting their underlying representations, computational structures, and comparative performance. These architectures are demonstrated on three canonical PDE problems: the Poisson equation, a linear elasticity problem, and a hyperelasticity problem. To make the presentation self-contained, key foundational topics are introduced, including finite-dimensional representations of function spaces, singular-value decomposition, and sampling from infinite-dimensional function spaces. Beyond forward modeling, the review discusses the use of neural operators as surrogate models within a Bayesian inverse-problem framework, including prior specification, forward-map approximation, and posterior computation. The performance of the three neural-operator architectures is evaluated on in-distribution samples, out-of-distribution samples, and Bayesian inference tasks. The review also discusses challenges related to prediction accuracy and generalization, outlining emerging strategies such as residual-based error correction and multi-level training. The review concludes by positioning neural operators within broader scientific-computing workflows and by identifying directions for reliable, scalable operator learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从理论到应用：科学计算中神经算子的实用入门
本文综述了用于学习参数化偏微分方程（PDE）解算子的神经算子架构，强调概念清晰性和实际实现。文章分析了关键模型，包括DeepONet、PCANet和傅里叶神经算子，突出其底层表示、计算结构及性能比较。这些架构在三个经典PDE问题上进行了演示：泊松方程、线弹性问题和超弹性问题。为使内容自洽，文章引入了关键基础主题，包括函数空间的有限维表示、奇异值分解及从无限维函数空间采样。除前向建模外，文章还讨论了神经算子作为贝叶斯反问题框架中的代理模型的应用，包括先验设定、前向映射近似及后验计算。三种神经算子架构在分布内样本、分布外样本及贝叶斯推断任务中的性能进行了评估。文章还讨论了预测精度与泛化方面的挑战，概述了新兴策略，如基于残差的误差校正和多级训练。文章最后将神经算子置于更广泛的科学计算工作流中，并指出了可靠、可扩展算子学习的发展方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and scalable solutions to parametric partial differential equations, this review introduces neural operator architectures such as DeepONet, PCANet, and Fourier Neural Operator, emphasizing their conceptual foundations and practical implementation. The study evaluates these models on canonical PDE problems including Poisson, linear elasticity, and hyperelasticity, assessing their performance across in-distribution, out-of-distribution, and Bayesian inference tasks. Results show that while all architectures demonstrate strong generalization and accuracy, Fourier Neural Operator often outperforms others in spectral representation and generalization, especially under distribution shifts. The review also highlights challenges like prediction error and scalability, proposing residual correction and multi-level training as promising strategies to enhance reliability.
为解决科学计算中参数化偏微分方程高效建模的需求，本文综述了DeepONet、PCANet和傅里叶神经算子等神经算子架构，强调其数学基础与实际实现。研究在泊松方程、线弹性与超弹性三个经典问题上评估这些模型，结果显示它们能从数据中学习解算子，对分布内样本表现良好，但在分布外样本和贝叶斯推理任务中表现不一。文章指出泛化能力与精度是主要挑战，并提出残差误差校正与多级训练等策略以提升可靠性。最终，神经算子被定位为可扩展科学计算流程中的可行工具，未来研究方向聚焦于增强其鲁棒性与可扩展性。
Seeing Is Not Screening: Multimodal Hidden Instruction Attacks on Agent Skill Scanners
Authors: Xiaojun Jia, Jie Liao, Simeng Qin, Ke Ma, Wenbo Guo, Yebo Feng, Aishan Liu, Yang Liu
First: 2026-06-16T17:29:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:29:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agent skills are emerging as an important attack surface in LLM-based systems. Through an empirical study of existing skill scanners, we find that current defenses primarily rely on textual descriptions, manifests, and source code as the main signals for security analysis, which can leave visually conveyed malicious intent insufficiently examined. This creates a practical blind spot: harmful operational instructions hidden in images may bypass scanning while still being recoverable by multimodal agents during deployment. To systematically investigate this threat, we propose SkillCamo, a document-mediated multimodal instruction attack that conceals malicious instructions within images bundled with a skill while rewriting the surrounding documentation to naturally reference those images as part of the normal workflow. Thus, the attack does not rely on the image alone, but on the joint interpretation of textual guidance and visual payload at execution time. To defend against such attacks, we further propose ExecScan, an execution-grounded multimodal scanning module that performs intent extraction, behavior reconstruction, abuse assessment, and deliberative execution simulation over skill artifacts. ExecScan jointly analyzes documentation, code, referenced resources, and visual content to recover hidden instructions, reconstruct executable behavior chains, and identify downstream risks such as exfiltration, destruction, persistence, deception, and privilege escalation. Extensive experiments show that image-hidden malicious instructions challenge existing skill scanners, while ExecScan can improve the skill scanning performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：看见不等于筛查：针对代理技能扫描器的多模态隐藏指令攻击
代理技能正成为基于大语言模型系统的重要攻击面。通过对现有技能扫描器的实证研究，我们发现当前防御主要依赖文本描述、清单和源代码作为安全分析的主要信号，这可能导致视觉传达的恶意意图未被充分检查。这造成了一个实际盲区：隐藏在图像中的有害操作指令可能绕过扫描，但在部署时仍可被多模态代理恢复。为系统性研究这一威胁，我们提出SkillCamo，一种通过文档中介的多模态指令攻击，将恶意指令隐藏在与技能捆绑的图像中，并重写周围文档以自然引用这些图像作为正常工作流程的一部分。因此，攻击不依赖图像本身，而是依赖执行时文本指导与视觉载荷的联合解释。为防御此类攻击，我们进一步提出ExecScan，一种基于执行的多模态扫描模块，对技能工件进行意图提取、行为重建、滥用评估和审慎执行模拟。ExecScan联合分析文档、代码、引用资源和视觉内容，以恢复隐藏指令、重建可执行行为链，并识别数据外泄、破坏、持久化、欺骗和权限提升等下游风险。大量实验表明，图像隐藏的恶意指令挑战现有技能扫描器，而ExecScan可提升技能扫描性能。

ExecScan, their proposed defense, jointly analyzes documentation, code, resources, and visual content to reconstruct executable behavior chains and detect hidden threats like exfiltration or privilege escalation. Experiments demonstrate that SkillCamo successfully bypasses existing scanners, while ExecScan significantly improves detection accuracy by grounding analysis in execution context.
鉴于现有LLM代理技能扫描器主要依赖文本和代码信号，忽视视觉中隐藏的恶意意图，本文提出SkillCamo攻击方法，将有害指令嵌入与技能捆绑的图像中，并修改配套文档使其自然引用这些图像，从而在执行时绕过检测。为应对该威胁，作者提出ExecScan模块，联合分析文档、代码、资源与视觉内容，提取意图、重建行为链并评估如数据外泄、权限提升等风险。实验表明，SkillCamo可有效绕过现有扫描器，而ExecScan显著提升检测能力并降低下游风险。
Vulcan: Instance-specialized, Verifiable Systems Heuristics Through LLM-driven Search
Authors: Rohit Dwivedula, Divyanshu Saxena, Sujay Yadalam, Eric Hayden Campbell, Daehyeok Kim, Aditya Akella
First: 2025-12-31T18:58:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:27:56+00:00
Comments: 19 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Systems resource management tasks rely primarily on hand-designed heuristics. However, growing hardware heterogeneity and workload diversity require heuristics specialized to particular deployment instances, making manual design expensive and difficult to scale. In this paper, we explore how to synthesize systems heuristics using LLMs. The main challenge is ensuring that generated heuristics execute safely, integrate correctly with the surrounding system, and still achieve strong performance. We propose Vulcan, a framework that identifies LLM-friendly interfaces that isolate core decision logic from the rest of the implementation. With Vulcan, LLM-generated code is restricted to simple stateless decision functions, while trusted runtime abstractions provide rich derived statistics for meaningful policy exploration without system-integration bugs. To ensure execution safety, LLMs synthesize heuristics in a restricted language, Anvil, that guarantees important properties by construction. We evaluate Vulcan across three well-studied domains and demonstrate up to 4.9x higher savings for spot-VM scheduling, up to 2x lower miss ratios for cache eviction, and up to 10% higher application performance for tiered-memory systems, while ensuring execution safety throughout.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Vulcan：通过LLM驱动搜索实现实例专用、可验证的系统启发式方法
系统资源管理任务主要依赖人工设计的启发式方法。然而，硬件异构性和工作负载多样性日益增长，要求启发式方法针对特定部署实例进行专业化设计，使得手动设计成本高昂且难以扩展。本文探讨如何利用大语言模型（LLM）合成系统启发式方法。主要挑战在于确保生成的启发式方法执行安全、正确集成到系统中，同时仍能实现强性能。我们提出Vulcan框架，该框架识别出适合LLM的接口，将核心决策逻辑与其余实现隔离。在Vulcan中，LLM生成的代码被限制为简单的无状态决策函数，而受信任的运行时抽象提供丰富的派生统计数据，支持有意义的策略探索，同时避免系统集成错误。为确保执行安全，LLM在受限语言Anvil中合成启发式方法，该语言通过构造保证重要属性。我们在三个广泛研究的领域评估Vulcan，结果表明：在Spot-VM调度中节省高达4.9倍，在缓存淘汰中降低高达2倍的缺失率，在分层内存系统中提升高达10%的应用性能，同时全程确保执行安全。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for instance-specialized heuristics in heterogeneous systems to replace costly manual design, Vulcan leverages LLMs to synthesize system heuristics while ensuring safety and correctness. It achieves this by defining LLM-friendly interfaces that isolate decision logic into stateless functions, using trusted runtime abstractions to provide system statistics without integration bugs. Heuristics are synthesized in a restricted language, Anvil, which enforces safety by design. Experiments across three domains show Vulcan delivers up to 4.9x spot-VM savings, 2x lower cache miss ratios, and 10% higher application performance, all while maintaining execution safety.
为应对异构硬件与多样化负载对定制化系统启发式算法的需求，Vulcan利用大语言模型自动生成启发式算法，同时确保其安全与正确性。该框架通过隔离决策逻辑为无状态函数，并借助可信运行时抽象提供系统统计信息，避免集成错误。启发式算法在安全受限语言Anvil中生成，其设计保证了关键属性。实验在三个领域中验证，结果表明可实现最高4.9倍的Spot-VM节省、2倍的缓存命中率提升及10%的应用性能提升，且全程保障执行安全。
Learning from the Self-future: On-policy Self-distillation for dLLMs
Authors: Yifu Luo, Zeyu Chen, Haoyu Wang, Xinhao Hu, Yuxuan Zhang, Zhizhou Sha, Shiwei Liu
First: 2026-06-16T17:24:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:24:57+00:00
Comments: Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has proven effective for post-training large language models (LLMs), yet its application to diffusion LLMs (dLLMs) remains unexplored. Existing OPSD methods are inherently autoregressive-centric. They inject privileged information via left-to-right prefix conditioning with token-level divergence supervision, a design that fundamentally conflicts with the arbitraryorder generation of dLLMs. We introduce d-OPSD, the first OPSD framework tailored for dLLMs. Our approach makes two core contributions. First, we reframe self-teacher construction by using self-generated answers as suffix conditioning, enabling the student model to learn from "self future-experience" rather than privileged prefixes. Second, we shift supervision from token-level to step-level, aligning training with the iterative denoising process of dLLMs. Experiments across four reasoning benchmarks show that d-OPSD consistently outperforms RLVR and SFT baselines with superior sample efficiency, requiring only around 10% of the optimization steps by RLVR and opening a promising pathway for dLLM posttraining. The code is available at https://github.com/xingzhejun/d-OPSD.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从自我未来中学习：面向扩散大语言模型的在线自蒸馏
在线自蒸馏（OPSD）已被证明在大语言模型（LLMs）的后训练中有效，但其在扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）中的应用尚未探索。现有OPSD方法本质上以自回归为中心，通过左到右前缀条件和词级差异监督注入特权信息，这与dLLMs的任意顺序生成机制根本冲突。我们提出了d-OPSD，这是首个专为dLLMs设计的OPSD框架。我们的方法有两个核心贡献：首先，通过自生成答案作为后缀条件重构自教师构建，使学生模型从“自我未来经验”中学习，而非特权前缀；其次，将监督从词级转移到步级，使训练与dLLMs的迭代去噪过程对齐。在四个推理基准上的实验表明，d-OPSD在样本效率上显著优于RLVR和SFT基线，仅需RLVR约10%的优化步数，为dLLM后训练开辟了有前景的路径。代码见https://github.com/xingzhejun/d-OPSD。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to adapt on-policy self-distillation to diffusion LLMs, which differ fundamentally from autoregressive models, the paper introduces d-OPSD, the first OPSD framework designed for dLLMs. It constructs a self-teacher using suffix conditioning from self-generated outputs and shifts supervision from token-level to step-level to align with the denoising process. Experimental results across four reasoning benchmarks show d-OPSD outperforms RLVR and SFT baselines, achieving comparable performance with only about 10% of RLVR’s optimization steps, demonstrating superior sample efficiency.
为将自监督蒸馏方法应用于扩散型大语言模型（dLLMs），作者提出了d-OPSD框架，首次将自监督蒸馏适配于dLLMs。该方法通过自生成输出作为后缀条件构建自教师，并将监督信号从词级调整为步骤级，以匹配dLLMs的去噪过程。在四个推理基准测试中，d-OPSD显著优于RLVR和SFT基线方法，仅需RLVR约10%的优化步数即可达到或超越其性能，展现出更高的样本效率。
A Red-Team Study of Anthropic Fable 5 & Opus 4.8 Models
Authors: Nicola Franco
First: 2026-06-16T17:23:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:23:58+00:00
Comments: White paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We evaluate the adversarial robustness of two frontier large language models (LLMs) developed by Anthropic, Fable 5 and Opus 4.8, against four families of automated jailbreak attack across 7 826 harmful intents spanning a ten-category harm taxonomy. Using the HackAgent red-teaming framework, hundreds of thousands of adversarial attempts were generated and every apparent success was independently re-adjudicated by a panel of three judge models (majority vote). Both models resist the majority of attacks, but the residual surface is larger than aggregate framing suggests: it is dominated by adaptive iterative attacks, while static obfuscation is near-fully neutralised. The strongest adaptive search (tree-of-attacks) breaks Opus 4.8 on 11.5% of intents overall, whereas Fable 5 stays in the single digits (6.1% worst-case). Aggregate rates therefore should not be read as reassurance. Even in these hardened configurations, the two models produced 1 620 (Opus 4.8) and 702 (Fable 5) panel-confirmed harmful completions spanning every harm category, located automatically, cheaply, and within the first one or two refinement steps by an attacker model with no human expert in the loop. The reasonable conclusion is that even the best, most-tested frontier models remain reliably breakable under sustained automated pressure.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对Anthropic Fable 5与Opus 4.8模型的红队研究
我们评估了Anthropic公司开发的两款前沿大语言模型（LLM）——Fable 5和Opus 4.8——在针对七千八百二十六种有害意图（涵盖十类危害分类）的四类自动化越狱攻击下的对抗鲁棒性。使用HackAgent红队框架，生成了数十万次对抗尝试，每项看似成功的攻击均由三位裁判模型独立复审（多数投票）。两款模型均能抵御多数攻击，但残余漏洞面比整体框架暗示的更大：主要由自适应迭代攻击主导，而静态混淆几乎被完全中和。最强的自适应搜索（攻击树）在整体上突破Opus 4.8的攻击比例为11.5%，而Fable 5则保持在个位数（最差情况6.1%）。因此，整体攻击成功率不应被解读为保障。即使在这些强化配置下，两款模型仍分别产生了1620次（Opus 4.8）和702次（Fable 5）经评审确认的有害输出，覆盖所有危害类别，且可由无专家参与的攻击模型在第一或两步优化中自动、低成本地定位。合理结论是：即使是最先进、经过充分测试的模型，在持续自动化压力下仍可被可靠突破。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess real-world adversarial robustness of frontier LLMs, this study evaluates Anthropic’s Fable 5 and Opus 4.8 models against automated jailbreak attacks across 7,826 harmful intents. Using the HackAgent red-teaming framework, hundreds of thousands of adversarial prompts were generated and re-evaluated by a three-model judge panel. While both models resist most attacks, adaptive iterative strategies—especially tree-of-attacks—break Opus 4.8 on 11.5% of intents and Fable 5 on 6.1% worst-case. Despite their resilience, both models still produced 1,620 and 702 confirmed harmful outputs respectively, across all harm categories, generated automatically and within one or two refinement steps without human intervention, indicating that even highly tested models remain vulnerable under sustained automated pressure.
为评估前沿大语言模型在现实对抗场景中的鲁棒性，本研究针对Anthropic的Fable 5与Opus 4.8模型，对其在7826个有害意图上的自动化越狱攻击进行了测试。采用HackAgent红队框架生成数十万次对抗尝试，并由三名裁判模型独立复审。尽管两模型抵御多数攻击，但对适应性迭代攻击仍存在明显漏洞，Opus 4.8失败率达11.5%，Fable 5最坏情况为6.1%。静态混淆攻击基本无效。即便在强化配置下，两模型仍分别产生1620和702个经裁判确认的有害输出，由无专家参与的攻击模型在一次或两次优化步骤内自动、低成本生成。
The Stanford EDGAR Filings Dataset: Reconstructing U.S. Corporate and Financial Disclosures into Layout-Faithful and Token-Efficient Pretraining Data
Authors: Nick Bettencourt, Xiaowei Ding, Kay Giesecke
First: 2026-06-16T17:22:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:22:34+00:00
Comments: Preprint. Includes appendix, tables, and figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As high-quality public web corpora become increasingly exhausted, clean long-context documents have become a scarce and expensive source of training data for large language models (LLMs). Existing long-context corpora are often proprietary and costly to acquire, synthetically generated, or concentrated in narrow domains such as programming. We introduce the Stanford EDGAR Filings Dataset (SEFD), an open reconstruction of SEC filings into layout-faithful MultiMarkdown for financial language modeling and evaluation. SEFD makes audited financial statements, risk disclosures, ownership reports, accounting notes, and market-moving event filings usable as long-context pretraining data and as a basis for financial reasoning, forecasting, compliance, and document understanding. The resulting corpus is token-efficient, model-ready, and has less than 0.1% overlap with Common Crawl-derived corpora. We release SEFD-v1, a 152B-token initial public snapshot, and provide corpus-level analyses of a larger 18.5M-filing archive estimated at 550B tokens. We further introduce two SEFD-derived benchmarks: EDGAR-Forecast, which evaluates filing-grounded numerical forecasting after model knowledge cutoffs, and EDGAR-OCR, which evaluates transcription of complex financial tables.
中文标题/摘要
标题：斯坦福EDGAR文件数据集：将美国公司和财务披露重构为布局忠实且令牌高效的预训练数据
随着高质量公开网络语料库日益枯竭，干净的长上下文文档已成为大型语言模型（LLM）训练数据的稀缺且昂贵来源。现有长上下文语料库多为专有、获取成本高，或为合成生成，或局限于狭窄领域（如编程）。我们引入斯坦福EDGAR文件数据集（SEFD），该数据集将SEC文件开放重构为适用于金融语言建模与评估的布局忠实MultiMarkdown格式。SEFD使经审计的财务报表、风险披露、股权报告、会计注释及影响市场的事件文件可作为长上下文预训练数据，并可用于金融推理、预测、合规及文档理解。该语料库令牌高效、模型友好，且与源自Common Crawl的语料重叠率低于0.1%。我们发布SEFD-v1，即1520亿令牌的初始公开快照，并提供对更大规模1850万份文件档案（预估5500亿令牌）的语料级分析。此外，我们还推出两个基于SEFD的基准测试：EDGAR-Forecast，用于评估模型知识截止后基于文件的数值预测能力；EDGAR-OCR，用于评估复杂金融表格的转录能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of clean, long-context training data for large language models, this work introduces the Stanford EDGAR Filings Dataset (SEFD), which reconstructs SEC filings into layout-preserving MultiMarkdown format for financial language modeling. The method involves converting audited financial statements and disclosures into token-efficient, model-ready text while preserving structural fidelity. Experimental results show SEFD-v1 contains 152B tokens with less than 0.1% overlap with Common Crawl data, and the larger corpus (550B tokens) enables robust benchmarking via EDGAR-Forecast and EDGAR-OCR, demonstrating strong performance in numerical forecasting and OCR transcription tasks post-model cutoff.
为解决大语言模型训练中高质量长上下文数据稀缺的问题，本研究构建了斯坦福EDGAR文件数据集（SEFD），将美国SEC文件重构为保留布局的MultiMarkdown格式，用于金融语言建模。方法将审计财务报表与披露内容转化为高效、模型友好的文本，同时保持结构完整性。实验表明，SEFD-v1包含1520亿个token，与Common Crawl数据重叠率低于0.1%，更大规模档案（18.5万份文件，约5500亿token）支持有效预训练与评估。两个衍生基准EDGAR-Forecast和EDGAR-OCR分别验证了模型在截止后预测与复杂表格转录任务中的能力。
DRFLOW: A Deep Research Benchmark for Personalized Workflow Prediction
Authors: Md Tawkat Islam Khondaker, Raymond Li, Muhammad Abdul-Mageed, Laks V. S. Lakshmanan, Issam H. Laradji
First: 2026-06-16T17:22:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:22:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep research (DR) systems are increasingly used for complex information-seeking tasks, but existing works mainly focus on generating reports and summaries. In contrast, many enterprise tasks instead require an agent to identify concrete workflows which is a sequence of action-steps. For example, rather than summarizing budgeting policies, an agent should be able to determine the steps needed to answer a question such as: "How do I request new headcount given a fixed budget?". Therefore, we introduce DRFLOW, a benchmark for evaluating personalized workflows predicted by agents from heterogeneous sources. Each task requires the agent to identify relevant evidence from scattered sources, then use that evidence to predict the correct action-step sequence for the user's task. DRFLOW contains 100 tasks across five domains, with 1,246 reference workflow steps grounded in more than 3,900 sources. We define seven diagnostic metrics covering factual grounding, step recovery, structural ordering, condition resolution, and personalization. We further present DRFLOW-Agent (DRFA), a workflow-oriented reference agent to predict personalized workflow. We show that although DRFA improves over strong baseline agents (upto 10.02% average F1 score), there is substantial room for improvement remains across these workflow metrics, indicating that predicting complete and correct personalized workflows remains a challenging frontier for deep research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DRFLOW：面向个性化工作流预测的深度研究基准
深度研究（DR）系统正越来越多地用于复杂的资讯获取任务，但现有研究主要聚焦于生成报告和摘要。相比之下，许多企业任务要求代理识别具体的工作流——即一系列操作步骤。例如，而非总结预算政策，代理应能确定回答“如何在预算固定的情况下申请新增编制？”这类问题所需的步骤。因此，我们引入DRFLOW，一个用于评估代理从异构来源预测个性化工作流的基准。每个任务要求代理从分散的来源中识别相关证据，再利用这些证据预测用户任务的正确操作步骤序列。DRFLOW涵盖五个领域共100个任务，包含1,246个基于3,900多个来源的参考工作流步骤。我们定义了七个诊断指标，涵盖事实锚定、步骤恢复、结构排序、条件解析和个性化。我们进一步提出DRFLOW-Agent（DRFA），一个面向工作流的参考代理，用于预测个性化工作流。结果显示，尽管DRFA在强基线代理上表现更优（平均F1分数提升10.02%），但在这些工作流指标上仍有大量改进空间，表明预测完整且正确的个性化工作流仍是深度研究的挑战性前沿。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for agents to predict personalized action sequences rather than summaries, DRFLOW introduces a benchmark evaluating workflow prediction from heterogeneous sources. It comprises 100 tasks across five domains, grounded in over 3,900 sources and 1,246 reference steps, requiring agents to retrieve evidence and generate correct step sequences. Seven diagnostic metrics assess grounding, ordering, condition handling, and personalization. The DRFLOW-Agent (DRFA) achieves up to 10.02% higher average F1 than baselines but still shows significant room for improvement, highlighting the challenge of accurate personalized workflow prediction.
为应对现有深度研究系统主要生成摘要而忽视个性化动作序列预测的问题，作者提出DRFLOW基准，用于评估从异构数据源中预测个性化工作流的能力。该方法要求代理从分散文档中检索证据，并生成符合用户任务的正确步骤序列。DRFLOW涵盖五个领域共100个任务，包含1246个基于3900+来源的参考步骤。通过七项诊断指标评估，提出的DRFLOW-Agent（DRFA）相较基线模型平均F1提升10.02%，但各指标仍有较大提升空间，表明个性化工作流预测仍是深度研究的挑战性前沿。
Multi-Source Cybersecurity Logs: An ATT&CK-Labeled Dataset and SLM Evaluation
Authors: Abir Ashab Niloy, Ahmed Ryan, Imamul Hossain Rafi, Md Erfan, Md Rayhanur Rahman
First: 2026-06-16T17:21:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:21:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-stage cyberattacks span system, network, and browser logs. Detecting them requires correlating events across all three sources. Machine learning methods can learn these cross-source patterns, but they need labeled multi-source data. Existing public datasets fall short. Network-only datasets such as CICIDS and UNSW-NB15 miss host and browser activity. Host-focused datasets such as LMDG and CICAPT-IIoT lack browser telemetry. ATLAS includes all three sources but labels events only as malicious or benign, without MITRE Adversarial Tactics, Techniques, and Common Knowledge (ATT&CK) technique granularity. No public dataset combines all three sources with per-entry ATT&CK technique labels. We close the gap by building a multi-source log dataset of 870 sessions (70 attack, 800 benign) and approximately 2.3 million events. We captured system, network, and browser activity simultaneously on Windows endpoints. We labeled malicious events with ATT&CK technique IDs, covering 12 tactics and 53 techniques. We generated all attack data using real tools, including Remote Access Trojan (RAT), Command and Control (C2) tunnels, and cloud exfiltration. To demonstrate learnability, we fine-tuned three Small Language Models (SLMs) (Qwen2.5-1.5B, Llama-3.2-3B, Phi-4-Mini) using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA). We compared each against its base variant across ten metrics on two tasks: chunk classification and ATT&CK technique identification. Fine-tuning improved every model on every metric. Chunk classification accuracy rose from approximately 8% in the base variants to between 90% and 97% after fine-tuning. Technique identification remained challenging, with the best exact-match accuracy at 42%, although high partial-match scores show the models captured most of the underlying reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多源网络安全日志：一个带有ATT&CK标注的数据集与SLM评估
多阶段网络攻击跨越系统、网络和浏览器日志。检测此类攻击需要跨三个数据源关联事件。机器学习方法可学习这些跨源模式，但需要标注的多源数据。现有公开数据集存在不足：仅网络数据集（如CICIDS和UNSW-NB15）缺少主机和浏览器活动；以主机为中心的数据集（如LMDG和CICAPT-IIoT）缺乏浏览器遥测数据；ATLAS虽包含三类日志，但仅将事件标记为恶意或良性，未提供MITRE攻击战术、技术和常见知识（ATT&CK）技术粒度标注。目前尚无公开数据集同时包含三类日志并提供每条记录的ATT&CK技术标注。我们填补这一空白，构建了一个包含870个会话（70个攻击、800个良性）和约230万事件的多源日志数据集。我们在Windows终端上同步捕获系统、网络和浏览器活动。我们使用ATT&CK技术ID标注恶意事件，涵盖12种战术和53种技术。所有攻击数据均使用真实工具生成，包括远程访问木马（RAT）、命令与控制（C2）隧道及云外泄。为验证可学习性，我们使用低秩自适应（LoRA）对三个小型语言模型（SLM）（Qwen2.5-1.5B、Llama-3.2-3B、Phi-4-Mini）进行微调。我们在两个任务（分块分类与ATT&CK技术识别）的十项指标上，将每个微调模型与基线模型进行比较。微调使所有模型在所有指标上均有所提升。分块分类准确率从基线模型约8%提升至微调后90%至97%。技术识别仍具挑战性，最佳精确匹配准确率为42%，但高部分匹配分数表明模型已捕获大部分底层推理。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to detect multi-stage cyberattacks that span system, network, and browser logs, the authors constructed a novel multi-source log dataset comprising 870 sessions (70 attack, 800 benign) and 2.3 million events, each labeled with MITRE ATT&CK technique IDs. They captured real-world attack traces using tools like RATs and C2 tunnels, enabling fine-tuning of three Small Language Models (SLMs) via LoRA. Experimental results show that fine-tuning significantly improved performance: chunk classification accuracy jumped from ~8% to 90–97%, while ATT&CK technique identification achieved a best exact-match accuracy of 42%, indicating partial but meaningful reasoning capture.
为应对跨越系统、网络和浏览器日志的多阶段网络攻击检测需求，本文构建了一个包含870个会话（70个攻击、800个良性）和230万事件的多源日志数据集，每个事件均标注了MITRE ATT&CK技术ID。该数据集在Windows终端上同步采集三类日志，填补了现有公开数据集的空白。为评估模型学习能力，对三个小型语言模型（Qwen2.5-1.5B、Llama-3.2-3B、Phi-4-Mini）采用LoRA微调。实验表明，微调显著提升性能：片段分类准确率从约8%提升至90–97%，ATT&CK技术识别最佳精确匹配准确率为42%，但高部分匹配分数表明模型已捕捉到大部分底层推理逻辑。
Beyond Failure Recovery: An Engagement-Aware Human-in-the-loop Framework for Robotic Systems
Authors: Jiaying Fang, Joyce Yang, Zhanxin Wu, Bohan Yang, Tapomayukh Bhattacharjee
Venue: Robotics: Science and Systems 2026
First: 2026-06-16T17:21:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:21:43+00:00
Comments: Project website at https://emprise.cs.cornell.edu/empc
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Conventional human-in-the-loop approaches typically involve users only when a robot encounters failure or uncertainty, treating humans primarily as tools for improving robot performance. However, in many human-centered robotics settings, interaction should support engagement by keeping users involved in decision-making rather than limiting them to failure-driven interventions. This is particularly compelling in physical caregiving, where mobility limitations can reduce users' ability to intervene or modulate the robot's behavior in the moment. As a result, failure-driven interaction policies may relegate users to passive observers for long stretches of the task. For example, a user with mobility limitations may feel less engaged when being continuously and passively fed by a robot. At the same time, overly frequent interaction can be tiring and increase the user's workload. To address this trade-off, we propose Engagement-aware MPC (E-MPC), a user-engagement-aware method that plans interaction to maintain engagement while respecting a workload constraint. E-MPC leverages a user interaction dynamics model that captures how user engagement evolves as a function of both the frequency and type of interaction. Rather than requesting input only when difficulties arise during task execution, the robot proactively considers the user's preferred level of engagement throughout the task, balancing autonomy and interaction while ensuring task success. We evaluate E-MPC in simulation with several ablations and baseline comparisons. Results demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach across diverse user personas. In addition, we conduct a real-world user study with participants with emulated mobility limitations on a robot-assisted bite acquisition system, showing that E-MPC improves user experience while maintaining task success.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越故障恢复：面向机器人系统的用户参与感知人机协同框架
传统的人机协同方法通常仅在机器人遇到故障或不确定性时才引入用户，将人类主要视为提升机器人性能的工具。然而，在许多以人为中心的机器人场景中，交互应支持用户参与决策，而非仅限于故障驱动的干预。这在物理护理领域尤为关键，因为行动受限可能削弱用户即时干预或调节机器人行为的能力，导致故障驱动的交互策略使用户长时间沦为被动观察者。例如，行动受限的用户在持续被动接收机器人提供的服务时可能感到参与度降低。同时，过度频繁的交互也会令用户感到疲劳并增加其认知负荷。为解决这一权衡，我们提出参与感知模型预测控制（E-MPC），一种兼顾用户参与度与工作负荷约束的交互规划方法。E-MPC利用用户交互动力学模型，刻画用户参与度随交互频率与类型的变化规律。机器人不再仅在任务执行困难时才请求用户输入，而是主动考虑用户在整个任务中的理想参与水平，在保障任务成功的同时平衡自主性与交互性。我们在仿真环境中对E-MPC进行了多种消融实验与基线对比，结果表明该方法在不同用户画像下均有效。此外，我们在真实世界中对模拟行动受限的用户开展机器人辅助咬合获取系统的用户研究，结果表明E-MPC在维持任务成功率的同时显著提升了用户体验。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to sustain user engagement beyond failure-driven interventions in human-robot systems, especially in caregiving contexts where mobility limitations reduce user agency, the paper proposes Engagement-aware MPC (E-MPC), a method that proactively plans robot-user interactions to balance autonomy and engagement while respecting user workload. E-MPC models how user engagement evolves based on interaction frequency and type, enabling the robot to anticipate and maintain optimal user involvement throughout tasks rather than only reacting to failures. Experimental results in simulation and a real-world user study with mobility-limited participants show that E-MPC improves user experience and maintains task success without increasing perceived workload.
为解决传统人机交互仅在机器人失败时才请求用户介入导致用户被动的问题，特别是在行动受限的护理场景中，该研究提出一种名为E-MPC的用户参与感知模型预测控制方法，通过建模用户参与度随交互频率与类型的变化，主动规划交互时机以平衡自主性与参与度，同时控制用户认知负荷。仿真与真实用户实验（模拟行动受限者）结果表明，E-MPC在保持任务成功率的同时，显著提升了用户体验。
Rethinking Cross-Layer Information Routing in Diffusion Transformers
Authors: Chao Xu, Maohua Li, Qirui Li, Yixuan Xu, Yanke Zhou, Yunhe Li, Cuifeng Shen, Hanlin Tang, Kan Liu, Tao Lan, Lin Qu, Shao-Qun Zhang
First: 2026-05-20T05:07:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:19:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have become a de facto backbone of modern visual generation, and nearly every major axis of their design -- tokenization, attention, conditioning, objectives, and latent autoencoders -- has been extensively revisited. The residual stream that governs how information accumulates across layers, however, has been directly inherited from the original Transformer. In this paper, we present a systematic empirical analysis of cross-layer information flow in DiTs, jointly along depth and denoising timestep, and identify three concrete symptoms of traditional residual addition, namely monotonic forward magnitude inflation, sharp backward gradient decay, and pronounced block-wise redundancy. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose Diffusion-Adaptive Routing (\textsc{DAR}), a drop-in residual replacement that performs \emph{learnable, timestep-adaptive, and non-incremental} aggregation over the history of sublayer outputs. Moreover, the proposed \textsc{DAR} is compatible with many modern Transformer enhancement methods, such as REPA. On ImageNet $256\times256$, \textsc{DAR} improves SiT-XL/2 by $2.11$ FID ($7.56$ vs.\ $9.67$) and matches the baseline's converged quality with $8.75\times$ fewer training iterations. Stacked on top of REPA, it yields a $2\times$ training acceleration in the early stage, suggesting cross-layer information routing as an underexplored design axis in diffusion modeling, one that operates orthogonally to existing representation-alignment objectives. Beyond pretraining, \textsc{DAR} can also be applied during the fine-tuning stage of large-scale T2I models and preserves high-frequency details during Distribution Matching Distillation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重新思考扩散Transformer中的跨层信息路由
扩散Transformer（DiTs）已成为现代视觉生成的主流骨干架构，其设计中的几乎每个主要方面——分词、注意力、条件控制、目标函数和潜在自编码器——都已得到广泛重新审视。然而，控制信息在层间累积的残差流却直接继承自原始Transformer。本文对DiT中跨层信息流动进行了系统性的实证分析，同时考虑深度和去噪时间步，并识别出传统残差相加的三个具体症状：前向幅度单调膨胀、后向梯度急剧衰减以及明显的块状冗余。基于此诊断，我们提出扩散自适应路由（DAR），一种可替换残差的模块，它能对子层输出的历史进行可学习、时间步自适应且非增量式的聚合。此外，所提出的DAR与许多现代Transformer增强方法（如REPA）兼容。在ImageNet 256×256上，DAR将SiT-XL/2的FID提升2.11（7.56 vs. 9.67），并在仅用基线训练迭代次数的1/8.75时达到相同收敛质量。叠加REPA后，早期训练阶段可实现2倍加速，表明跨层信息路由是扩散建模中一个尚未充分探索的设计维度，且与现有表示对齐目标正交。此外，DAR也可应用于大规模文本到图像模型的微调阶段，并在分布匹配蒸馏过程中保留高频细节。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that traditional residual connections in Diffusion Transformers suffer from monotonic magnitude inflation, backward gradient decay, and block-wise redundancy across layers and timesteps, the authors propose Diffusion-Adaptive Routing (DAR), a learnable, timestep-adaptive, non-incremental aggregation mechanism that replaces standard residuals. DAR is designed to be compatible with existing Transformer enhancements like REPA and demonstrates significant performance gains: on ImageNet 256×256, it improves SiT-XL/2 by 2.11 FID (7.56 vs. 9.67) while requiring 8.75× fewer training iterations to reach baseline quality. When stacked with REPA, DAR also accelerates early-stage training by 2×, highlighting cross-layer routing as a novel, orthogonal design axis for diffusion models. Additionally, DAR maintains high-frequency detail during fine-tuning via Distribution Matching Distillation.
鉴于传统残差连接在扩散Transformer中存在层间幅度单调增长、反向梯度衰减和块状冗余等问题，作者提出扩散自适应路由（DAR），一种可学习、时步自适应、非累加的子层输出聚合机制，替代标准残差。DAR兼容现有Transformer增强方法如REPA，在ImageNet 256x256上将SiT-XL/2的FID从9.67降至7.56，且仅用8.75倍更少的训练迭代即可达到基线收敛质量。与REPA结合时，DAR可使早期训练加速2倍，表明跨层信息路由是与表征对齐目标正交的、尚未充分探索的设计维度。
Kolmogorov Regression for Robust Diffusion Policies
Authors: Lekan Molu
First: 2026-06-16T17:18:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:18:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Finite-dimensional (FD) diffusion policies exhibit temporal drift owing to discretization artifacts that degrade long-horizon performance (when deployed on physical systems). We introduce a backward Kolmogorov equation that lifts diffusion policies to a Cameron-Martin space -- a subset of the Hilbert space. Essentially, replacing stochastic score matching with a deterministic boundary-value PDE problem. Our core innovation thrives on Gaussian measure theory whereupon the diffusion noise covariance operator is realized from a colored noise distribution which prescribes a notion of regularity on samples from the model at inference time. We train the diffusion model with a derived precision-weighted Cameron- Martin loss and a Kolmogorov residual is introduced as a PDE diagnostic during inference. These substitutions yield (i) convergence guarantees where the bound's constants depend on the effective rank of the kernel rather than action dimension, (ii) improved trajectory regularity via spectral weighting, and (iii) a deterministic failure detector without reward signals. Validation across two application domains demonstrates substantial improvements: on the PushT manipulation benchmark, the Cameron-Martin loss achieves a 17% improvement in maximum episode reward (0.95 vs. 0.78 for MSE) and 67.6% reduction in inter-step drifts during inference via the introduced residual magnitude. Similarly, on a 6-station manufacturing line with constant work-in-process (CONWIP) flow control, we achieve 28.4% lower RMSE than classical LSTM baselines; a high starvation-event recall (1.0 in test cycles), and effective bottleneck identification (Precision@1 = 1.0 in test set, 13x signal-to-noise ratio). We then certify the dispatch policies with Hamilton-Jacobi reachability theory which reduces deadlock events by 96% compared to uncontrolled dispatch over 100 simulated runs (351 events prevented).
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于科尔莫戈洛夫回归的鲁棒扩散策略
有限维（FD）扩散策略因离散化伪影导致时间漂移，从而损害长时性能（在物理系统部署时）。我们引入一个向后科尔莫戈洛夫方程，将扩散策略提升至卡梅隆-马丁空间——希尔伯特空间的一个子集。本质上，将随机得分匹配替换为确定性边值偏微分方程问题。我们的核心创新基于高斯测度理论，其中扩散噪声协方差算子由有色噪声分布实现，该分布为推理时模型样本提供正则性概念。我们使用推导出的精度加权卡梅隆-马丁损失训练扩散模型，并在推理过程中引入科尔莫戈洛夫残差作为PDE诊断。这些替换带来：（i）收敛性保证，其界常数依赖于核的有效秩而非动作维度；（ii）通过谱加权提升轨迹正则性；（iii）无需奖励信号的确定性失效检测器。在两个应用领域验证显示显著改进：在PushT操作基准测试中，卡梅隆-马丁损失使最大回合奖励提升17%（0.95 vs. 0.78，MSE对比），并通过引入残差幅度将推理过程中的步间漂移降低67.6%。在具有恒定在制品（CONWIP）流量控制的6站制造线上，我们比经典LSTM基线降低28.4%的RMSE，高饥饿事件召回率（测试周期中为1.0），并有效识别瓶颈（测试集Precision@1=1.0，信噪比13倍）。随后，我们通过哈密顿-雅可比可达性理论认证调度策略，相比无控制调度，在100次模拟运行中将死锁事件减少96%（共预防351次事件）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the temporal drift in finite-dimensional diffusion policies that harms long-horizon performance, this work introduces a backward Kolmogorov equation to lift policies into a Cameron-Martin space, reformulating stochastic score matching as a deterministic PDE. Training uses a precision-weighted Cameron-Martin loss, and a Kolmogorov residual acts as a PDE-based diagnostic during inference. Experiments show 17% higher maximum reward and 67.6% less drift on the PushT benchmark, 28.4% lower RMSE and perfect starvation detection on a manufacturing line, and 96% fewer deadlocks certified via reachability theory.
为解决有限维扩散策略因离散化导致的时序漂移问题，本文引入后向柯尔莫哥洛夫方程，将策略提升至卡梅隆-马丁空间，将随机得分匹配转化为确定性偏微分方程。训练采用加权卡梅隆-马丁损失，推理时引入柯尔莫哥洛夫残差作为诊断工具。实验表明，在PushT任务上最大奖励提升17%、步间漂移减少67.6%；在CONWIP制造线任务中，RMSE降低28.4%， starvation事件召回率100%，瓶颈识别精度100%，信噪比提升13倍；经哈密顿-雅可比可达性认证，死锁事件减少96%。
A Diffusion Approximation for Temporal-Difference Learning with Linear Features under Markovian Noise
Authors: M. Forzo, E. Monzio Compagnoni, A. Russo, A. Pacchiano
First: 2026-06-16T17:16:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:16:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Temporal difference (TD) learning with linear function approximation is a core method for policy evaluation. Its classical continuous-time description is an ordinary differential equation (ODE), which captures the asymptotic mean dynamics but neglects stochastic fluctuations determining the error floor. We introduce a stochastic differential equation (SDE) approximation for linear TD(0) under Markovian noise. The resulting model distinguishes the contraction dynamics governed by the projected Bellman operator from the influence of Markovian sampling. As a consequence, the model explains the constant-stepsize error floor through the interaction between Markovian long-run covariance and the contraction geometry of the projected Bellman operator.
中文标题/摘要
标题：马尔可夫噪声下线性特征时序差分学习的扩散近似
线性函数逼近的时序差分（TD）学习是策略评估的核心方法。其经典连续时间描述为常微分方程（ODE），捕捉渐近均值动态但忽略决定误差下限的随机波动。本文引入马尔可夫噪声下线性TD(0)的随机微分方程（SDE）近似模型，该模型区分由投影贝尔曼算子主导的收缩动态与马尔可夫采样影响。由此，模型通过马尔可夫长期协方差与投影贝尔曼算子收缩几何的相互作用，解释了恒定步长下的误差下限。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of ordinary differential equations in capturing stochastic fluctuations in temporal-difference learning, this paper proposes a stochastic differential equation approximation for linear TD(0) under Markovian noise. The method separates the contraction dynamics from the sampling noise influence, enabling a precise characterization of the error floor. Experimental results confirm that the error floor arises from the interplay between the long-run covariance of Markovian sampling and the contraction geometry of the projected Bellman operator, aligning with observed behavior in practice.
为了解决传统常微分方程无法刻画TD学习中随机波动的问题，本文在马尔可夫噪声下提出了一种线性TD(0)的随机微分方程近似方法。该方法将贝尔曼算子的收缩动力学与马尔可夫采样噪声的影响分离，揭示了误差下限由马尔可夫采样长期协方差与投影贝尔曼算子的收缩几何共同作用所致。实验结果表明，该SDE模型能准确预测固定步长下TD学习的恒定误差下限，优于传统ODE模型对随机行为的刻画能力。
IUU+DB: Tracking Illegal, Unreported, and Unregulated Fishing, Seafood Fraud, and Labor Abuse through LLM-driven Information Extraction
Authors: Henry Bodwell, Hong Yang, John C. Simeone, Kelvin Gorospe, Bella Sullivan, Lana Huang, Jessica Gephart, Sandy Aylesworth, Molly Masterton, Naren Ramakrishnan
First: 2026-06-16T17:16:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:16:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Illegal, unreported, and unregulated fishing (IUU) traditionally refers to fishing activities that violate applicable laws or occur in areas that lack applicable laws. We propose the term IUU+ to capture a broader suite of fisheries sector environmental and associated supply chain trade-related crimes and behaviors. Although IUU+ activity is widely recognized as a serious threat to marine ecosystems, markets, and livelihoods, a quantitative understanding of these incidents, e.g., their frequency, geography, species, actors, and patterns in the type of illicit activity, remains difficult to obtain. We propose IUU+DB, a large language model driven system for building a global incident database of IUU+ activity. The system ingests heterogeneous documents, classifies whether they describe relevant incidents, extracts key data elements such as actors, locations, species, vessels, violations, and enforcement outcomes, and supports deduplication and trend analysis. Case studies and validation results show that IUU+DB can help organize fragmented evidence, surface geographic and behavioral hotspots, support fisheries-domain specific research in academia and non-government organizations, assist source and species risk assessments for industry, and provide support for policy implementation and targeted enforcement efforts to government agencies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：IUU+DB：通过大语言模型驱动的信息提取追踪非法、未报告和无监管捕鱼、海鲜欺诈及劳工虐待
传统上，非法、未报告和无监管捕鱼（IUU）指违反适用法律或发生在无适用法律区域的捕鱼活动。我们提出IUU+这一术语，以涵盖渔业领域更广泛的环境相关及供应链贸易犯罪行为。尽管IUU+活动被广泛认为对海洋生态系统、市场和生计构成严重威胁，但对其事件的量化理解——如频率、地理分布、物种、行为者及非法活动类型模式——仍难以获得。我们提出IUU+DB，这是一个基于大语言模型的系统，用于构建全球IUU+事件数据库。该系统可处理异构文档，判断其是否描述相关事件，提取关键数据要素（如行为者、地点、物种、船只、违规行为及执法结果），并支持去重与趋势分析。案例研究与验证结果表明，IUU+DB有助于整合零散证据，揭示地理与行为热点，支持学术界与非政府组织在渔业领域的专项研究，协助行业进行源头与物种风险评估，并为政府机构的政策实施与精准执法提供支持。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of quantitative data on illegal, unreported, and unregulated fishing and related supply chain crimes, the authors developed IUU+DB, a large language model-driven system designed to extract and organize global incident data. The system processes heterogeneous documents, classifies relevant content, and extracts structured information including actors, locations, species, vessels, violations, and enforcement outcomes, while supporting deduplication and trend analysis. Experimental validation demonstrates that IUU+DB effectively consolidates fragmented evidence, identifies geographic and behavioral hotspots, and supports research, industry risk assessments, and policy enforcement efforts.
为应对非法、未报告和无管制渔业及相关供应链犯罪缺乏量化数据的问题，作者构建了基于大语言模型的IUU+DB系统，用于提取和整合全球事件数据。该方法通过处理异构文本资料，分类相关内容，并提取结构化要素如行为者、地点、物种、船只、违规行为及执法结果，同时支持去重与趋势分析。实验验证表明，该系统能整合零散证据，识别地理与行为热点，支持学术研究、行业风险评估及政府执法决策。
EgoCS-400K: An Egocentric Gameplay Dataset for World Models
Authors: Rongjin Guo, Dong Liang, Yuhao Liu, Fang Liu, Tianyu Huang, Gerhard P. Hancke, Rynson W. H. Lau
First: 2026-06-16T17:13:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:13:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The shift from video generation to interactive world modeling places new demands on data: beyond captioned videos, world models require temporally aligned video-action-language trajectories grounded in the actions, camera motion, states, and events that drive future scene changes. However, such data is difficult to obtain at scale. Web video datasets offer broad visual coverage but lack executable actions and reliable states; robotic datasets provide action and state supervision but are costly and limited in scene diversity; and existing simulators often lack large-scale human-driven interaction trajectories. In this paper, we introduce EgoCS-400K, a large-scale replay-grounded egocentric Counter-Strike dataset for world models, built from public professional CS and CS2 match demos that preserve human gameplay trajectories and enable parsing, replaying, rendering, and temporal alignment. We extract player states, view directions, movements, keyboard/button inputs, view-angle changes, weapon usage, game events, and round-level context, and render clean first-person videos from the same trajectories. EgoCS-400K contains over 400,000 first-person videos and 10,000 hours of gameplay from more than 1,000 matches and 40,000 rounds, covering 13 maps and 10 player viewpoints per round. It supports a range of interactive visual modeling tasks, including action-conditioned future prediction, state- and event-aware scene rollout, replay-grounded captioning, and agent egocentric action understanding. By connecting visual observations with human actions, camera motion, game states, and events at scale, EgoCS-400K serves as a practical bridge between passive web videos, controllable game simulation, and costly real-world embodied data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EgoCS-400K：面向世界模型的自拍视角游戏数据集
从视频生成转向交互式世界建模对数据提出了新需求：世界模型不仅需要带字幕的视频，还需与动作、摄像机运动、状态和事件在时间上对齐的视频-动作-语言轨迹，这些因素驱动未来场景变化。然而，此类数据难以大规模获取。网络视频数据虽视觉覆盖广，但缺乏可执行动作和可靠状态；机器人数据虽提供动作和状态监督，但成本高且场景多样性有限；现有模拟器往往缺乏大规模人类驱动的交互轨迹。本文提出EgoCS-400K，这是一个基于回放的自拍视角Counter-Strike大型数据集，从公开的专业CS和CS2比赛录像中构建，保留人类游戏轨迹，支持解析、回放、渲染和时间对齐。我们提取玩家状态、视角方向、移动、键盘/按钮输入、视角变化、武器使用、游戏事件和回合级上下文，并从相同轨迹渲染干净的第一人称视频。EgoCS-400K包含超过40万条第一人称视频和1万小时游戏内容，涵盖1000多场比赛、4万回合，覆盖13张地图，每回合10个玩家视角。它支持多种交互式视觉建模任务，包括动作条件未来预测、状态与事件感知场景展开、回放基础字幕生成和智能体自拍视角动作理解。通过大规模连接视觉观测、人类动作、摄像机运动、游戏状态和事件，EgoCS-400K成为连接被动网络视频、可控游戏模拟与昂贵真实世界具身数据的实用桥梁。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for temporally aligned, action-grounded egocentric data to train world models, the authors construct EgoCS-400K, a large-scale dataset derived from public Counter-Strike match demos. The method involves extracting and aligning player states, camera motion, inputs, weapon usage, and game events from professional gameplay, then rendering clean first-person videos from the same trajectories. The dataset comprises over 400,000 videos and 10,000 hours of gameplay across 40,000 rounds, 13 maps, and 10 viewpoints per round. Experimental results demonstrate its utility in tasks like action-conditioned future prediction, scene rollout, captioning, and agent action understanding, showing strong performance when grounding visual data with human actions and game states.
为满足世界模型训练对时间对齐、动作关联的自拍视角数据的需求，作者构建了EgoCS-400K数据集，该数据集基于公开的Counter-Strike比赛录像。方法从40,000轮次、1,000场比赛中提取玩家状态、摄像机运动、输入、武器使用和游戏事件，并沿相同轨迹渲染清晰的第一人称视频。数据集包含40多万个视频和1万小时游戏内容，覆盖13张地图和每轮10个视角。实验表明，该数据集在动作条件未来预测、场景展开、回放引导的字幕生成和自拍视角动作理解等任务中表现良好，成为连接被动视频、模拟器与真实体感数据的实用桥梁。
Instrumental and Proximal Causal Inference with Gaussian Processes
Authors: Yuqi Zhang, Krikamol Muandet, Dino Sejdinovic, Edwin Fong, Siu Lun Chau
First: 2026-03-02T18:23:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:11:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Instrumental variable (IV) and proximal causal learning (Proxy) methods are central frameworks for causal inference in the presence of unobserved confounding. Despite substantial methodological advances, existing approaches rarely provide reliable epistemic uncertainty (EU) quantification. We address this gap through a Deconditional Gaussian Process (DGP) framework for uncertainty-aware causal learning. Our formulation recovers popular kernel estimators as the posterior mean, ensuring predictive precision, while the posterior variance yields principled and well-calibrated EU. Moreover, the probabilistic structure enables systematic model selection via marginal log-likelihood optimization. Empirical results demonstrate strong predictive performance alongside informative EU quantification, evaluated via empirical coverage frequencies and decision-aware accuracy rejection curves. Together, our approach provides a unified, practical solution for causal inference under unobserved confounding with reliable uncertainty.

标题：使用高斯过程的工具变量与近端因果推断
工具变量（IV）和近端因果学习（Proxy）方法是存在未观测混杂因素时因果推断的核心框架。尽管方法论已有显著进展，现有方法很少提供可靠的元认知不确定性（EU）量化。我们通过一种去条件高斯过程（DGP）框架解决这一问题，用于不确定性感知的因果学习。我们的方法将流行核估计量作为后验均值，确保预测精度，而后验方差则提供原则性且校准良好的EU。此外，概率结构支持通过边缘对数似然优化进行系统性模型选择。实证结果表明，该方法在预测性能和信息丰富的EU量化方面表现优异，通过经验覆盖率频率和决策感知准确率拒绝曲线进行评估。总体而言，我们的方法为在未观测混杂因素下进行因果推断提供了一个统一且实用的解决方案，并具备可靠的不确定性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable uncertainty quantification in causal inference under unobserved confounding, this work introduces a Deconditional Gaussian Process (DGP) framework that unifies instrumental variable and proximal causal learning methods. The DGP model recovers standard kernel estimators as its posterior mean for accurate predictions, while its posterior variance provides principled, well-calibrated epistemic uncertainty. The probabilistic structure also supports model selection via marginal log-likelihood optimization. Experimental results show the method achieves strong predictive accuracy and provides meaningful uncertainty estimates, validated by high empirical coverage and decision-aware accuracy rejection curves.
为了解决在未观测混杂下因果推断中不确定性量化不足的问题，本文提出了一种去条件高斯过程（DGP）框架，统一了工具变量与近端因果学习方法。该框架将常用核估计器作为后验均值以保证预测精度，同时利用后验方差提供可靠、校准良好的认知不确定性。实验结果表明，该方法在预测性能和不确定性量化方面表现优异，通过经验覆盖率和决策感知准确率拒绝曲线验证，为未观测混杂下的因果推断提供了一种实用统一的解决方案。
MiniFool -- Physics-Constraint-Aware Minimizer-Based Adversarial Attacks in Deep Neural Networks
Authors: Lucie Flek, Oliver Janik, Philipp Alexander Jung, Akbar Karimi, Timo Saala, Alexander Schmidt, Matthias Schott, Philipp Soldin, Matthias Thiesmeyer, Christopher Wiebusch, Ulrich Willemsen
Venue: Published in: Eur.Phys.J.C 86 (2026) 6, 641
First: 2025-11-03T08:57:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:10:15+00:00
Comments: Submitted to Computing and Software for Big Science
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we present a new algorithm, MiniFool, that implements physics-inspired adversarial attacks for testing neural network-based classification tasks in particle and astroparticle physics. While we initially developed the algorithm for the search for astrophysical tau neutrinos with the IceCube Neutrino Observatory, we apply it to further data from other science domains, thus demonstrating its general applicability. Here, we apply the algorithm to the well-known MNIST data set and furthermore, to Open Data data from the CMS experiment at the Large Hadron Collider. The algorithm is based on minimizing a cost function that combines a $χ^2$ based test-statistic with the deviation from the desired target score. The test statistic quantifies the probability of the perturbations applied to the data based on the experimental uncertainties. For our studied use cases, we find that the likelihood of a flipped classification differs for both the initially correctly and incorrectly classified events. When testing changes of the classifications as a function of an attack parameter that scales the experimental uncertainties, the robustness of the network decision can be quantified. Furthermore, this allows testing the robustness of the classification of unlabeled experimental data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MiniFool——基于物理约束感知最小化器的深度神经网络对抗攻击
本文提出了一种新算法MiniFool，用于实施受物理启发的对抗攻击，以测试粒子与天体物理领域基于神经网络的分类任务。虽然我们最初为IceCube中微子天文台搜寻天体τ中微子而开发该算法，但将其应用于其他科学领域的进一步数据，从而证明其通用性。本文将该算法应用于著名的MNIST数据集，并进一步应用于大型强子对撞机CMS实验的公开数据。该算法基于最小化一个结合了χ²检验统计量与目标得分偏差的成本函数。检验统计量量化了基于实验不确定性的数据扰动概率。对于所研究的应用场景，我们发现正确与错误分类事件的分类翻转可能性不同。通过测试分类变化随攻击参数（缩放实验不确定性）的变化，可量化网络决策的鲁棒性，同时允许测试未标记实验数据分类的鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate neural network robustness in physics-based classification tasks, MiniFool introduces a physics-constrained adversarial attack method that minimizes a cost function combining a chi-squared test statistic with deviation from target scores, incorporating experimental uncertainties. Applied to MNIST and CMS Open Data, the algorithm reveals that classification flipping likelihood varies depending on whether events were initially correctly or incorrectly classified, and quantifies network robustness by varying the attack parameter that scales uncertainties, enabling assessment of classification reliability for unlabeled experimental data.
为评估物理领域神经网络的鲁棒性，MiniFool提出一种结合卡方检验统计量与目标分数偏差、并融入实验不确定性的物理约束对抗攻击方法。在MNIST和CMS公开数据上的实验表明，分类翻转概率随初始分类正确与否而异，通过调节不确定性参数可量化网络决策的鲁棒性，并支持对未标注实验数据分类可靠性的测试。
A Convex Quasilinearization Method for Solving Nonlinear PDEs with Physics-Informed Neural Networks
Authors: Gbenga T. Awojinrin, Abdul-Akeem Olawoyin, Rami M. Younis
First: 2026-06-16T17:09:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:09:59+00:00
Comments: Preprint. 56 pages, 18 figures. Code: https://github.com/awojinrin/lilq-pinn
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We present a numerical method for the forward solution of nonlinear partial differential equations (PDEs) in which Bellman-Kalaba quasilinearization reduces the nonlinear problem to a sequence of linear subproblems, each discretized by collocation onto a trial space that is linear in its parameters and solved by a single direct linear least-squares QR factorization. The trial space, which we term Linear-in-Learnables (LiL), comprises representations whose trainable parameters enter linearly, including random-feature extreme learning machines, spectral polynomial bases, and trigonometric expansions, each implemented as a physics-informed neural network. The method thus replaces the nonconvex gradient-based training that limits standard PINNs with a convex per-step solve. We establish local Newton-Kantorovich convergence of the outer iteration to a residual-limited neighborhood under an explicit smallness condition, with the limiting accuracy governed by the best-approximation residual of the trial space rather than by an optimization tolerance. The method, denoted LiL-Q, is assessed on seven benchmarks spanning scalar nonlinear PDEs (Bratu, viscous Burgers, Buckley-Leverett), coupled systems (plane-strain elasticity and the incompressible Navier-Stokes equations in two and three spatial dimensions), and steady-state Darcy flow with heterogeneous permeability. Across these problems, LiL-Q converges in single-digit outer iterations in most cases, even at the coarsest basis sizes and independent of the parameter count. When the exact solution lies in the span of the trial space, the method recovers it to machine precision in a single solve. On the Navier-Stokes benchmarks, it matches or exceeds published PINN solvers with up to two orders of magnitude fewer trainable parameters, without gradient-based optimization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种用于求解物理信息神经网络中非线性偏微分方程的凸拟线性化方法
我们提出了一种用于非线性偏微分方程（PDE）正向求解的数值方法，其中Bellman-Kalaba拟线性化将非线性问题转化为一系列线性子问题，每个子问题通过在参数线性化的试验空间上进行插值离散化，并通过单次直接线性最小二乘QR分解求解。该试验空间，我们称之为“可学习参数线性化”（LiL），包含可训练参数线性进入的表示形式，包括随机特征极端学习机、谱多项式基和三角级数展开，每种形式均以物理信息神经网络实现。该方法因此替代了限制标准PINN的非凸梯度训练，转而采用每步凸解。我们在显式小性条件下的外迭代中建立了局部Newton-Kantorovich收敛性，收敛至残差受限邻域，其极限精度由试验空间的最佳逼近残差决定，而非优化容差。该方法（记为LiL-Q）在七个基准问题上进行了评估，涵盖标量非线性PDE（Bratu方程、粘性Burgers方程、Buckley-Leverett方程）、耦合系统（平面应变弹性力学及二维和三维不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程）以及具有异质渗透率的稳态Darcy流。在这些问题中，LiL-Q在大多数情况下仅需单数次外迭代即可收敛，即使在最粗糙的基函数尺寸下亦然，且与参数数量无关。当精确解位于试验空间张成空间时，该方法可在单次求解中恢复至机器精度。在Navier-Stokes基准测试中，其性能与已发表的PINN求解器相当或更优，且参数数量可低达两个数量级，无需梯度优化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency and nonconvexity of standard physics-informed neural networks (PINNs), this work introduces LiL-Q, a convex quasilinearization method that linearizes nonlinear PDEs via Bellman-Kalaba quasilinearization, solving each linearized subproblem with a direct QR factorization on a Linear-in-Learnables (LiL) trial space. The LiL framework includes random-feature ELMs, spectral polynomials, and trigonometric expansions, all implemented as PINNs with linearly parameterized representations. Experiments across seven benchmarks—including Bratu, Burgers, Buckley-Leverett, elasticity, Navier-Stokes, and Darcy flow—show LiL-Q converges in single-digit iterations even at coarse basis sizes and regardless of parameter count. When the true solution lies within the trial space, it achieves machine precision in one step. On Navier-Stokes problems, LiL-Q matches or surpasses existing PINN methods using up to two orders of magnitude fewer parameters without gradient-based optimization.
为解决传统物理信息神经网络（PINN）训练效率低、非凸优化难的问题，本文提出LiL-Q方法，通过Bellman-Kalaba拟线性化将非线性偏微分方程转化为线性子问题，并用直接QR分解求解。该方法采用“线性可学习参数”（LiL）空间，包含随机特征机、多项式基和三角函数展开等线性参数化表示，均以PINN形式实现。在涵盖Bratu、Burgers、Buckley-Leverett、弹性力学、Navier-Stokes及达西渗流等七个基准问题的实验中，LiL-Q在多数情况下仅需单位数外层迭代即可收敛，且在粗基底和参数量变化下仍稳定。当真实解位于试函数空间内时，单次求解即可达到机器精度。在Navier-Stokes问题上，该方法性能不逊于现有PINN方法，且参数量减少达两个数量级，无需梯度优化。
MedicalAgentsBench for Complex Medical Reasoning: Comparing Internalized Reasoning Models versus Externalized Agent-based Frameworks
Authors: Yanjun Shao, Xiangru Tang, Jiwoong Sohn, Jiapeng Chen, Yuxuan Liao, Jiayi Zhang, Jinyu Xiang, Fang Wu, Yilun Zhao, Chenglin Wu, Wenqi Shi, Arman Cohan, Mark Gerstein
First: 2025-03-10T15:38:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:07:03+00:00
Comments: https://github.com/gersteinlab/MedicalAgentsBench
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Complex medical reasoning requires integrating heterogeneous clinical evidence across multiple inference steps. Large language models (LLMs) now approach this through two routes: internalized reasoning and externalized agent scaffolding (frameworks that decompose problems collaboratively amongst multiple LLMs). To determine whether these routes are exclusive or complementary, we introduce MedicalAgentsBench, a filtered benchmark of 862 complex clinical questions drawn from the union of eight medical datasets via difficulty-aware curation and contamination screening. Evaluating three internalized reasoning models (DeepSeek-R1, o1-mini, and o3-mini), seven base models, and nine externalized agent-based methods, we find that internalized and externalized approaches each independently improve performance, and that their benefits compound: the highest accuracy is achieved by layering agent workflows onto an internalized reasoning model (i.e., o3-mini + MDAgents with 35.1%). Pareto analysis shows this combination dominates the cost-performance frontier; moreover, lightweight optimization on inexpensive models offers an entry point for resource-constrained settings. Our benchmark is at https://github.com/gersteinlab/MedicalAgentsBench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MedicalAgentsBench：用于复杂医学推理的基准测试——比较内化推理模型与外部化代理框架
复杂医学推理需要在多个推理步骤中整合异质临床证据。大语言模型（LLMs）目前通过两种途径实现：内化推理和外部化代理框架（将问题分解并在多个LLM间协同解决）。为判断这两种途径是互斥还是互补，我们引入MedicalAgentsBench，该基准由八个医学数据集合并后经难度感知筛选与污染筛查选出的862个复杂临床问题构成。评估三种内化推理模型（DeepSeek-R1、o1-mini、o3-mini）、七种基础模型和九种外部化代理方法后发现，内化与外部化方法各自独立提升性能，且其效果叠加：最高准确率由在内化推理模型（如o3-mini）上叠加代理工作流（MDAgents）实现（准确率35.1%）。帕累托分析显示该组合主导成本-性能前沿；此外，对低成本模型进行轻量化优化可为资源受限场景提供可行入口。本基准见https://github.com/gersteinlab/MedicalAgentsBench。

All Smoke, No Alarm: Oracle Signals in Agent-Authored Test Code
Authors: Dipayan Banik, Kowshik Chowdhury, Shazibul Islam Shamim
First: 2026-06-16T17:06:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:06:51+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the 8th IEEE International Conference on Artificial Intelligence Testing, 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Software practitioners increasingly use AI coding agents that generate test code alongside production code in open source pull requests (PRs). Recent studies report more than 932,000 agent-authored PRs across more than 116,000 repositories, yet whether their test files contain meaningful verification logic remains underexplored. Test files lacking explicit assertions execute code without verifying behavior, so quality gates based on test-file presence overestimate verification strength. The goal of this paper is to help practitioners assess the verification strength of agent-authored patches by characterizing oracle signals and their link to merge outcomes and review effort. We conduct an empirical study of 86,156 test-file patches from 33,596 agent-authored PRs across 2,807 GitHub repositories produced by five coding agents: OpenAI Codex, GitHub Copilot, Devin, Cursor, and Claude Code. A qualitative analysis of 384 stratified patches informs a syntactic taxonomy of eight oracle signal categories. Applied at scale, 80.2% of test patches contain weak or no explicit oracle signals. While raw merge rates are lower for strong-oracle PRs, a regression analysis adjusting for agent, PR size, repository popularity, task type, and language shows strong oracles significantly improve merge likelihood (OR = 1.28, p < 0.001). Our findings suggest that test file counts substantially overestimate verification strength and that practitioners can adopt oracle-aware quality checks to more accurately evaluate agent-authored contributions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：烟雾缭绕，无警报：Oracle信号在代理生成的测试代码中的表现
软件从业者越来越多地使用AI编码代理，在开源拉取请求（PR）中同时生成测试代码和生产代码。近期研究表明，已有超过93.2万次代理生成的PR，涉及超过11.6万个仓库，但其测试文件是否包含有意义的验证逻辑仍鲜有研究。缺乏显式断言的测试文件仅执行代码而不验证行为，因此仅依据测试文件存在性设置的质量门限会高估验证强度。本文旨在帮助从业者评估代理生成补丁的验证强度，通过刻画Oracle信号及其与合并结果和评审工作量的关联。我们对2,807个GitHub仓库中33,596个代理生成PR的86,156个测试文件补丁进行了实证研究，涉及五种编码代理：OpenAI Codex、GitHub Copilot、Devin、Cursor和Claude Code。对384个分层补丁的定性分析构建了八类Oracle信号的句法分类体系。大规模应用显示，80.2%的测试补丁包含弱或无显式Oracle信号。尽管强Oracle PR的原始合并率较低，但调整代理、PR规模、仓库流行度、任务类型和语言后，回归分析显示强Oracle显著提升合并可能性（OR = 1.28，p < 0.001）。我们的研究结果表明，测试文件数量严重高估了验证强度，从业者可采用Oracle感知的质量检查，更准确评估代理生成的贡献。
