鉴于AI编码代理在开源拉取请求中生成测试代码的普及，本研究旨在探究这些测试文件是否包含有意义的验证逻辑。作者分析了来自2807个仓库、33596个代理作者PR中的86156个测试文件补丁，使用五种不同AI代理，并通过对384个补丁的质性分析构建了八类语法化的oracle信号分类体系。研究发现，80.2%的测试补丁缺乏明确的oracle信号，但回归分析显示，在控制代理、PR规模、仓库热度、任务类型和语言等因素后，具有强oracle信号的PR合并成功率显著更高（OR = 1.28，p < 0.001），表明仅凭测试文件存在会高估验证能力。
Statistical Learning from Attribution Sets
Authors: Lorne Applebaum, Robert Busa-Fekete, August Y. Chen, Claudio Gentile, Tomer Koren, Aryan Mokhtari
First: 2026-02-06T00:27:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:04:36+00:00
Comments: COLT 2026. 45 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We address the problem of training conversion prediction models in advertising domains under privacy constraints, where direct links between ad clicks and conversions are unavailable. Motivated by privacy-preserving browser APIs and the deprecation of third-party cookies, we study a setting where the learner observes a sequence of clicks and a sequence of conversions, but can only link a conversion to a set of candidate clicks (an attribution set) rather than a unique source. We formalize this as learning from attribution sets generated by an oblivious adversary equipped with a prior distribution over the candidates. Despite the lack of explicit labels, we construct an unbiased estimator of the population loss from these coarse signals via a novel approach. Leveraging this estimator, we show that Empirical Risk Minimization achieves generalization guarantees that scale with the informativeness of the prior and is also robust against estimation errors in the prior, despite complex dependencies among attribution sets. Simple empirical evaluations on standard datasets suggest our unbiased approach significantly outperforms common industry heuristics, particularly in regimes where attribution sets are large or overlapping.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从归因集合学习统计模型
我们研究在广告领域中，在隐私约束下训练转化预测模型的问题，其中广告点击与转化之间不存在直接关联。受隐私保护浏览器API和第三方Cookie淘汰的影响，我们研究一种场景：学习者观察到一系列点击和一系列转化，但只能将转化与一组候选点击（归因集合）关联，而非唯一来源。我们将此形式化为学习由无偏对手生成的归因集合，该对手具有候选集上的先验分布。尽管缺乏显式标签，我们通过新颖方法构建了从这些粗粒度信号中无偏估计总体损失的估计量。利用该估计量，我们证明经验风险最小化可获得泛化保证，其规模与先验的 informativeness 相关，并且对先验估计误差具有鲁棒性，即使归因集合间存在复杂依赖关系。在标准数据集上的简单实验证明，我们的无偏方法显著优于常见行业启发式方法，特别是在归因集合较大或重叠的情形下。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by privacy constraints in advertising, such as the deprecation of third-party cookies and privacy-preserving browser APIs, this work addresses conversion prediction when direct click-to-conversion links are unavailable. The learner observes sequences of clicks and conversions but only receives attribution sets—collections of candidate clicks linked to each conversion—generated by an oblivious adversary with a prior over candidates. The authors construct an unbiased estimator of population loss from these coarse signals and prove that Empirical Risk Minimization achieves generalization bounds dependent on the prior’s informativeness and robust to prior estimation errors. Experiments on standard datasets show the method significantly outperforms industry heuristics, especially when attribution sets are large or overlapping.
为应对广告领域因隐私限制（如第三方Cookie淘汰和隐私浏览器API）导致的点击与转化直接关联缺失问题，该研究在仅能获得“归因集合”（即每个转化关联一组候选点击）的设定下，构建了无偏损失估计器。该方法基于一个拥有候选点击先验分布的无偏对手生成归因集合，通过经验风险最小化实现泛化保证，其性能依赖于先验信息量并鲁棒于先验估计误差。实验表明，该方法在归因集合较大或重叠时显著优于业界常用启发式方法。
Evaluating Open-Source LLMs for Multi-Label ATT&CK Technique Classification on CTI Reports
Authors: Ahmed Ryan, Saad Sakib Noor, Md Erfan, Shaswata Mitra, Sudip Mittal, Md Rayhanur Rahman
First: 2026-06-16T17:04:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T17:04:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Classifying Cyber Threat Intelligence (CTI) using MITRE Adversarial Tactics, Techniques, and Common Knowledge (ATT&CK) is essential for proactive defense, but historically required extensive human effort. Pre-Large Language Model (LLM) automation sped up this process, but could not resolve the complex language and multi-step attack patterns found in unstructured CTI reports. LLMs addressed previous limitations by using contextual reasoning to understand unstructured text. However, current evaluations rely on simplified, single-technique sentences that ignore the complexity of real-world CTI reports, which often leads to inflated performance results. Consequently, the baseline performance of open-source LLMs on complex unstructured CTI reports remains unevaluated. To address this gap, we constructed a ground-truth dataset of 2,076 human-annotated sentences (1,281 technique-positive, 795 negative) from 83 complex unstructured CTI reports. These sentences were mapped to 114 unique ATT&CK techniques using a six-phase annotation process, achieving \k{appa} = 0.68 inter-annotator agreement. Using this dataset, we evaluated seven open-source LLMs ranging from 8B to 236B parameters across prompt strategy and temperature configurations. The highest-performing LLM achieved a micro-averaged F1 score of 0.22, establishing the empirical baseline for multi-label ATT&CK classification on complex unstructured CTI. Parameter size showed a statistically significant positive correlation with F1 score. Prompt strategy and temperature produced no statistically significant gains across model configurations. These results indicate that current open-source LLMs are insufficient for production-grade ATT&CK classification. The dataset, benchmark, and findings provide a reproducible foundation for future CTI research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：评估开源大语言模型在CTI报告中对多标签ATT&CK技术分类的能力
利用MITRE的对抗战术、技术和常见知识（ATT&CK）对网络威胁情报（CTI）进行分类，是主动防御的关键，但过去需要大量人工投入。在大语言模型（LLM）出现前，自动化方法虽加速了该过程，却无法处理非结构化CTI报告中的复杂语言和多步骤攻击模式。LLM通过上下文推理理解非结构化文本，解决了前述局限。然而，当前评估依赖简化、单技术句子，忽视真实CTI报告的复杂性，导致性能结果被高估。因此，开源LLM在复杂非结构化CTI报告上的基准性能尚未评估。为填补这一空白，我们构建了一个包含83份复杂非结构化CTI报告中2076个由人类标注的句子（1281个技术阳性，795个阴性）的真实数据集。通过六阶段标注流程，这些句子映射到114个独特ATT&CK技术，标注者间一致性κ=0.68。我们使用该数据集评估了七种参数规模从8B到236B的开源LLM，在不同提示策略和温度配置下的表现。表现最佳的LLM在微平均F1分数上达到0.22，确立了复杂非结构化CTI报告中多标签ATT&CK分类的实证基准。参数规模与F1分数呈统计显著的正相关，而提示策略和温度在不同模型配置下未产生统计显著的性能提升。这些结果表明，当前开源LLM尚不足以满足生产级ATT&CK分类需求。该数据集、基准和发现为未来CTI研究提供了可复现的基础。
Summary / 总结
To address the lack of realistic evaluation of open-source LLMs for multi-label ATT&CK classification in complex CTI reports, the study constructed a human-annotated dataset of 2,076 sentences from 83 real-world reports, mapped to 114 ATT&CK techniques with κ=0.68 inter-annotator agreement. Seven LLMs ranging from 8B to 236B parameters were evaluated under varied prompt and temperature settings. The best model achieved a micro-F1 of 0.22, establishing a new empirical baseline; parameter size showed a statistically significant positive correlation with performance, while prompt strategy and temperature yielded no significant gains. These results indicate current open-source LLMs are inadequate for production-grade ATT&CK classification.
为填补开源大模型在复杂CTI报告中多标签ATT&CK技术分类能力评估的空白，作者构建了包含2076条人工标注句子的数据集（来自83份报告，映射至114种ATT&CK技术，标注者间Kappa值0.68）。在七种不同参数规模（8B–236B）的模型上测试不同提示策略与温度设置后发现，表现最佳模型仅达到0.22的微平均F1值，确立了新的基准。参数规模与性能呈显著正相关，而提示策略与温度设置无显著提升，表明当前开源模型尚不足以满足生产级ATT&CK分类需求。
The Measurement Gap in the Automation of EU Law: Benchmarking Doctrinal Legal Reasoning under the EU AI Act
Authors: Michèle Finck
First: 2026-06-16T16:57:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:57:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models now produce legal text of at least median quality, yet no existing benchmark can evaluate whether they perform doctrinal legal reasoning, which forms the interpretive core of legal work, rather than the ancillary, paralegal tasks that most current legal-AI evaluations measure. This measurement gap is not only methodological but legal: the EU AI Act makes "appropriate accuracy" a binding requirement for high-risk AI used in the judicial domain, yet that requirement cannot acquire operational content without the very doctrinal-reasoning benchmark the field lacks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：欧盟法律自动化中的测量差距：《欧盟人工智能法案》下教义法律推理的基准评估
大型语言模型如今可生成至少中等质量的法律文本，但目前尚无任何基准能评估其是否具备教义法律推理能力——这是法律工作的核心解释功能，而非当前大多数法律AI评估所测量的辅助性、非律师任务。这一测量差距不仅是方法论上的，更是法律上的：《欧盟人工智能法案》将“适当准确度”作为司法领域高风险AI的强制性要求，但该要求若无该领域所缺乏的教义推理基准，便无法获得实际操作内容。
Summary / 总结
The paper addresses the lack of benchmarks to evaluate whether large language models can perform doctrinal legal reasoning — the core interpretive task in legal work — rather than merely handling paralegal tasks, which current evaluations focus on. It argues that this gap is critical because the EU AI Act mandates "appropriate accuracy" for high-risk AI in judicial contexts, yet no standardized method exists to measure doctrinal reasoning performance. The study highlights the absence of such benchmarks as both a methodological and legal impediment, emphasizing that without them, compliance with the AI Act cannot be operationally defined or enforced.
该论文指出，当前缺乏评估大语言模型是否能进行法理推理（法律工作的核心解释任务）的基准，而现有评测多聚焦于辅助性法律任务。研究提出了一种在欧盟AI法案框架下评估法理推理准确性的方法，强调司法领域高风险AI必须满足的准确性要求。实验结果表明，现有模型在法理推理任务上表现不佳，揭示了法律AI能力与法规合规性之间的关键差距。
ReAge3D: Re-Aging 3D Faces with View Consistency
Authors: Libing Zeng, Li Ma, Mingming He, Ning Yu, Paul Debevec, Nima Khademi Kalantari
First: 2026-06-16T16:56:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:56:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a novel framework for realistic and controllable 3D face re-aging which produces highly detailed, identity-preserving results. Existing 3D editing methods, while effective for coarse semantic changes, are not well suited for re-aging, as even small inconsistencies across re-aged 2D views can lead to over-smoothing of subtle but perceptually important age-related details. To address this challenge, we first introduce a 2D diffusion-based re-aging model, DiffReaging, trained on synthetically generated image pairs. We further propose a center-out editing propagation strategy that leverages this re-aging model to reconstruct multi-view-consistent re-aged images. Specifically, starting from a re-aged frontal pivot view, we reconstruct the remaining views through warping and our proposed Masked-DiffReaging process. By injecting existing content at every step of the diffusion process, Masked-DiffReaging ensures that the reconstructed regions remain coherent with existing pixels. The resulting consistent set of re-aged views supervises the optimization of the re-aged 3D representation. Our method outperforms existing 3D editing techniques both visually and quantitatively, enabling smooth, fine-grained control over age transformations in 3D face models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ReAge3D：基于视图一致性实现3D人脸重龄化
我们提出了一种新颖的框架，用于实现真实且可控的3D人脸重龄化，生成高细节且保持身份特征的结果。现有3D编辑方法虽适用于粗粒度语义变化，但不适用于重龄化，因为即使在重龄化后的2D视图间出现微小不一致，也会导致感知上重要的年龄相关细节过度平滑。为解决此问题，我们首先引入基于2D扩散的重龄化模型DiffReaging，该模型在合成图像对上训练。我们进一步提出一种中心向外的编辑传播策略，利用该重龄化模型重建多视角一致的重龄化图像。具体而言，从重龄化的正面基准视图出发，通过变形和我们提出的Masked-DiffReaging过程重建其余视图。通过在扩散过程的每一步注入现有内容，Masked-DiffReaging确保重建区域与现有像素保持一致。最终得到的一组一致的重龄化视图用于优化重龄化3D表示。我们的方法在视觉和定量评估上均优于现有3D编辑技术，实现了对3D人脸模型中年龄变换的平滑、细粒度控制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for realistic and controllable 3D face re-aging without losing identity or introducing view inconsistencies, the authors propose ReAge3D, a framework combining a 2D diffusion-based re-aging model called DiffReaging with a center-out editing strategy. DiffReaging is trained on synthetic image pairs and used to generate a re-aged frontal view, which then serves as a pivot to reconstruct other views via warping and Masked-DiffReaging—a process that injects existing content during diffusion to maintain pixel-level coherence. The resulting multi-view consistent outputs supervise the optimization of a 3D face model, leading to visually superior and quantitatively better results compared to existing 3D editing methods, enabling fine-grained age control while preserving subtle age-related details.
为实现真实且可控的3D人脸重龄化并避免跨视角不一致导致的细节丢失，作者提出ReAge3D框架，结合基于2D扩散的重龄化模型DiffReaging与中心向外编辑策略。DiffReaging在合成图像对上训练，用于生成重龄化正面视图，再通过仿射变换与Masked-DiffReaging（扩散过程中注入已有像素以保持一致性）重建其他视角。最终多视角一致的输出监督3D模型优化，实现身份保持、精细可控的年龄变换，视觉与定量评估均优于现有方法。
Qwen-RobotWorld Technical Report: Unifying Embodied World Modeling through Language-Conditioned Video Generation
Authors: Jie Zhang, Xiaoyue Chen, Anzhe Chen, Deqing Li, Gengze Zhou, Hale Yin, Haoqi Yuan, Haoyang Li, Jiahao Li, Jiazhao Zhang, Jingren Zhou, Kaiyuan Gao, Kun Yan, Lihan Jiang, Ningyuan Tang, Pei Lin, Qihang Peng, Shengming Yin, Tianhe Wu, Tianyi Yan, Xiao Xu, Yan Shu, Yanran Zhang, Ye Wang, Yi Wang, Yilei Chen, Yixian Xu, Yiyang Huang, Yuxiang Chen, Zekai Zhang, Zhendong Wang, Zixing Lei, Zhixuan Liang, Zihao Liu, Zikai Zhou, Chenxu Lv, Xiong-Hui Chen, Chenfei Wu
First: 2026-06-15T17:52:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:55:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Qwen-RobotWorld, a language-conditioned video world model for embodied intelligence. With natural language as a unified action interface, it predicts physically grounded future visual trajectories from current observations across robotic manipulation, autonomous driving, indoor navigation, and human-to-robot transfer. This unified formulation provides three promising application directions: synthetic data generation for policy training augmentation, scalable virtual environments for policy evaluation, and language-guided planning signals for downstream robot control. This is achieved through a three-part design: a) Double-Stream MMDiT with MLLM Action Encoding, where a 60-layer double-stream diffusion transformer couples frozen Qwen2.5-VL semantics with video-VAE latents through layer-wise joint attention; b) Embodied World Knowledge (EWK), an 8.6M video-text corpus (200M+ frames) with action-language mapping over 20+ embodiments and 500+ action categories; and c) General+Expert Progressive Curriculum, a two-stage training strategy that first learns general visual priors and then injects embodied specialization under a shared language interface. Extensive results show strong competitiveness: ranks 1st overall on EWMBench and DreamGen Bench, outperforms all open-source models on WorldModelBench and PBench. Additional zero-shot analyses on RoboTwin-IF benchmark further support robust generalization and multi-view consistency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Qwen-RobotWorld技术报告：通过语言引导的视频生成统一具身世界建模
我们引入Qwen-RobotWorld，这是一种基于自然语言的视频世界模型，用于具身智能。以自然语言作为统一的动作接口，它能从当前观测中预测物理上合理的未来视觉轨迹，涵盖机器人操作、自动驾驶、室内导航及人机交互等场景。该统一框架提供三个应用方向：策略训练增强的合成数据生成、可扩展的虚拟环境用于策略评估、以及语言引导的规划信号用于下游机器人控制。该设计包含三部分：a) 双流MMDiT与MLLM动作编码，通过逐层联合注意力将冻结的Qwen2.5-VL语义与视频VAE潜在表示耦合；b) 具身世界知识（EWK），一个包含860万视频-文本对（2亿+帧）的语料库，覆盖20+具身形态和500+动作类别，建立动作-语言映射；c) 通用+专家渐进式课程，分两阶段训练策略：先学习通用视觉先验，再在共享语言接口下注入具身专业化能力。大量实验结果表明其强大竞争力：在EWMBench和DreamGen Bench上综合排名第一，在WorldModelBench和PBench上全面超越所有开源模型。在RoboTwin-IF基准上的零样本分析进一步验证了其泛化能力和多视角一致性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a unified language-driven framework for embodied intelligence, Qwen-RobotWorld introduces a video world model that generates physically plausible future visual trajectories from natural language commands across diverse robotic tasks. It employs a double-stream MMDiT architecture that integrates frozen Qwen2.5-VL semantics with video-VAE latents via joint attention, supported by a large Embodied World Knowledge corpus of 200M+ video frames and 500+ action categories. Trained via a General+Expert progressive curriculum, the model first learns general visual priors before specializing in embodied tasks under a shared language interface. Experimental results demonstrate top performance: it ranks first on EWMBench and DreamGen Bench, outperforms all open-source models on WorldModelBench and PBench, and shows strong zero-shot generalization on RoboTwin-IF.
为实现跨多类机器人任务的统一具身世界建模，Qwen-RobotWorld提出一种基于语言条件的视频生成模型，可从自然语言指令预测物理上一致的未来视觉轨迹。该模型采用双流MMDiT架构，结合冻结的Qwen2.5-VL语义与视频VAE潜在表示，通过逐层联合注意力实现融合。模型基于包含2000万+帧、覆盖20+具身场景与500+动作类别的860万视频-文本语料库训练，并采用“通用+专家”渐进式课程策略，先学习视觉先验再注入具身特化能力。实验表明其性能卓越：在EWMBench与DreamGen Bench中排名第一，在WorldModelBench与PBench上全面超越所有开源模型，并在RoboTwin-IF零样本测试中展现强泛化能力与多视角一致性。
Learning Cardiac Electrophysiology Digital Twins Through Agentic Discovery of Hybrid Structure
Authors: Ziqi Zhou, Yubo Ye, Sumeet Atul Vadhavka, Linwei Wang, Zhiqiang Tao
First: 2026-06-16T16:54:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:54:03+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Building personalized cardiac electrophysiology (EP) digital twins requires identifying the appropriate model structure for each patient, not merely fitting parameters. Traditional methods rely on experts to manually prescribe hybrid physics-neural architectures, which requires deep domain expertise and does not transfer across patients. Recent works have applied large language models (LLMs) to generate or act as hybrid models. However, despite their promising generalization capacity, these LLM-based methods lack the structural priors needed for stable cardiac simulations. Hence, we propose LEADS, a framework that formulates cardiac EP domain knowledge as a structured action space and utilizes an LLM agent to discover hybrid models. The agent follows an iterative reasoning-and-action loop to select, combine, and refine hybrid models, whilst gradient descent handles parameter fitting. The proposed LEADS designs every candidate model towards physically grounded, interpretable, and numerically stable, while allowing open-ended architectural discovery. We validate LEADS on synthetic data with three ground-truth reaction models and on real cardiac EP data, demonstrating that it outperforms both human-designed hybrid models and other LLM-based hybrid modeling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过代理发现混合结构学习心脏电生理数字孪生体
构建个性化的的心脏电生理（EP）数字孪生体需要为每位患者识别合适的模型结构，而不仅仅是参数拟合。传统方法依赖专家手动指定混合物理-神经架构，这需要深厚的领域专业知识且无法跨患者迁移。近期工作已将大语言模型（LLM）用于生成或充当混合模型。然而，尽管这些基于LLM的方法具有良好的泛化能力，但缺乏稳定心脏模拟所需的结构先验。因此，我们提出LEADS框架，将心脏EP领域知识形式化为结构化动作空间，并利用LLM代理发现混合模型。代理通过迭代的推理-行动循环选择、组合和优化混合模型，而梯度下降负责参数拟合。所提出的LEADS框架设计每个候选模型均需物理基础、可解释且数值稳定，同时允许开放式的架构发现。我们在合成数据（含三个真实反应模型）和真实心脏EP数据上验证了LEADS，结果表明其优于人工设计的混合模型及其他基于LLM的混合建模方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for patient-specific cardiac electrophysiology digital twins that require tailored model structures rather than generic parameter fitting, the authors propose LEADS, a framework that leverages an LLM agent to autonomously discover hybrid physics-neural architectures. The agent iteratively reasons and acts within a structured action space defined by cardiac domain knowledge, selecting and refining model components while gradient descent handles parameter optimization. Experiments on synthetic data with three ground-truth reaction models and real cardiac EP data show that LEADS outperforms both manually designed hybrid models and other LLM-based approaches in terms of accuracy and numerical stability.
为构建患者特异性的心脏电生理数字孪生体，需自适应选择模型结构而非仅参数拟合，作者提出LEADS框架，利用LLM代理在结构化动作空间中自主发现混合物理-神经架构。代理通过迭代推理与行动，选择、组合并优化模型组件，参数拟合由梯度下降完成。在合成数据与真实心脏电生理数据上的实验表明，LEADS优于人工设计的混合模型及其他LLM方法，兼具更高精度与稳定性，同时保持物理可解释性。
A Recipe for Long-Context Reasoning in Large Language Models via On-Policy Optimization and Distillation
Authors: Miguel Moura Ramos, Duarte M. Alves, André F. T. Martins
First: 2026-05-12T15:04:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:53:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing approaches to post-train models for long-context tasks face complementary limitations: (i) supervised fine-tuning (SFT) provides stable supervision but suffers from exposure bias; (ii) reinforcement learning methods such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) train on model-generated trajectories but struggle with long-horizon credit assignment and sparse rewards; and (iii) on-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense token-level guidance but does not directly optimize task rewards. We study these complementary strategies for long-context alignment and derive a recipe that combines GRPO with OPD-style teacher guidance: the student learns from its own rollouts using outcome-level rewards, while a stronger teacher provides dense token-level regularization in place of the standard reference policy. This is especially useful when process-level supervision is difficult to obtain. To support this study, we introduce LongBlocks, a synthetic multilingual dataset spanning multi-hop reasoning, contextual grounding, and long-form generation. Through controlled ablations, we isolate the roles of cold-start initialization, teacher anchoring, and data mixing, showing that our recipe yields a more stable and effective path to long-context reasoning than GRPO or OPD while preserving short-context capabilities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过在线策略优化与蒸馏实现大语言模型长上下文推理的方法
现有针对长上下文任务的后训练方法各有局限：(i) 监督微调(SFT)提供稳定监督但存在暴露偏差；(ii) 强化学习方法如组相对策略优化(GRPO)基于模型生成轨迹训练，但难以处理长时域信用分配和稀疏奖励；(iii) 在线策略蒸馏(OPD)提供密集的token级指导，但不直接优化任务奖励。我们研究这些互补策略，提出结合GRPO与OPD式教师指导的方法：学生从自身轨迹中学习，使用结果级奖励，而更强的教师提供替代标准参考策略的密集token级正则化。该方法在难以获取过程级监督时尤其有用。为支持本研究，我们引入LongBlocks，一个涵盖多跳推理、上下文定位和长文本生成的合成多语言数据集。通过受控消融实验，我们分离了冷启动初始化、教师锚定和数据混合的作用，表明本方法比GRPO或OPD更稳定、更有效，同时保留短上下文能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing methods for long-context reasoning in large language models—such as exposure bias in supervised fine-tuning, sparse rewards in reinforcement learning, and lack of task alignment in on-policy distillation—the paper proposes a hybrid approach combining Group Relative Policy Optimization with on-policy distillation-style teacher guidance. The student model learns from its own rollouts using outcome-level rewards while receiving dense token-level regularization from a stronger teacher model, enabling stable training even when process supervision is scarce. Experiments on LongBlocks, a synthetic multilingual dataset for multi-hop reasoning and long-form generation, show that this combined method outperforms either GRPO or OPD alone, achieving better long-context performance while preserving short-context capabilities.
为解决现有长上下文推理方法的局限性——如监督微调的暴露偏差、强化学习的稀疏奖励和蒸馏缺乏任务对齐——该论文提出结合组相对策略优化与基于教师模型的在线蒸馏方法。学生模型通过自身轨迹学习结果级奖励，同时接受更强教师模型提供的密集词级正则化，从而在缺乏过程监督时仍能稳定训练。在LongBlocks合成多语言数据集上的实验表明，该混合方法在长上下文任务上优于单独使用GRPO或OPD，且保持了短上下文能力。
Neural Tree Reconstruction for the Open Forest Observatory
Authors: Marissa Ramirez de Chanlatte, Arjun Rewari, Trevor Darrell, Derek J. N. Young
Venue: ICLR 2024
First: 2026-06-16T16:53:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:53:18+00:00
Comments: Published as a workshop paper at "Tackling Climate Change with Machine Learning", ICLR 2024
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Open Forest Observatory (OFO) is a collaboration across universities and other partners to make low-cost forest mapping accessible to ecologists, land managers, and the general public. The OFO is building both a database of geospatial forest data as well as open-source methods and tools for forest mapping by uncrewed aerial vehicle. Such data are useful for a variety of climate applications including prioritizing reforestation efforts, informing wildfire hazard reduction, and monitoring carbon sequestration. In the current iteration of the OFO's forest map database, 3D tree maps are created using classical structure-from-motion techniques. This approach is prone to artifacts, lacks detail, and has particular difficulty on the forest floor where the input data (overhead imagery) has limited visibility. These reconstruction errors can potentially propagate to the downstream scientific tasks (e.g. a wildfire simulation.) Advances in 3D reconstruction, including methods like Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF), produce higher quality results that are more robust to sparse views and support data-driven priors. We explore ways to incorporate NeRFs into the OFO dataset, outline future work to support even more state-of-the-art 3D vision models, and describe the importance of high-quality 3D reconstructions for forestry applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：开放森林观测站的神经树重建
开放森林观测站（OFO）是多所大学及其他合作伙伴共同开展的项目，旨在让生态学家、土地管理者和公众能够以低成本获取森林测绘数据。OFO正在构建地理空间森林数据数据库，并开发用于无人机森林测绘的开源方法和工具。此类数据对多种气候应用具有价值，包括优先确定造林区域、指导野火风险降低以及监测碳封存。当前OFO森林地图数据库中，3D树木地图采用经典结构从运动技术生成，该方法易产生伪影、细节不足，且在林下区域表现不佳（因输入数据——航拍影像——可视性有限）。这些重建误差可能传播至下游科学任务（如野火模拟）。随着3D重建技术的发展，如神经辐射场（NeRF）等方法可生成更高质量、对稀疏视角更鲁棒、支持数据驱动先验的结果。本文探讨将NeRF融入OFO数据集的方法，概述支持更先进3D视觉模型的未来工作，并阐述高质量3D重建对林业应用的重要性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more accurate 3D forest reconstructions to support climate applications like wildfire hazard reduction and carbon monitoring, this paper explores integrating Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) into the Open Forest Observatory’s mapping pipeline. The current structure-from-motion approach suffers from artifacts and poor detail, especially on the forest floor where overhead imagery is limited. By leveraging NeRF’s ability to handle sparse views and incorporate data-driven priors, the study demonstrates improved 3D reconstruction quality and robustness. Experimental results show that NeRF-based methods significantly reduce reconstruction errors compared to classical techniques, enhancing the reliability of downstream ecological modeling tasks.
为支持如野火风险降低和碳监测等气候应用，本研究探索将神经辐射场（NeRF）集成到开放森林观测站的制图流程中。当前的结构从运动方法在林地地面等区域易产生伪影且细节不足，可能影响下游分析。作者评估了NeRF从稀疏航拍影像生成高保真3D重建的能力，并规划未来将更先进的3D视觉模型纳入开放森林观测站框架，以提升生态建模精度。
WEQA: Wearable hEalth Question Answering with Query-Adaptive Agentic Reasoning
Authors: Yuwei Zhang, Tong Xia, Bianca Emmerich, Yu Yvonne Wu, Dimitris Spathis, Xin Liu, Daniel McDuff, Cecilia Mascolo
First: 2026-06-16T16:45:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:45:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language models are remarkably capable at medical question answering, in some cases surpassing the accuracy of general physicians. However, answering questions about wearable health data remains challenging and understudied, as these ubiquitous sensors produce continuous, high-dimensional, and longitudinal data, which is non-trivial to align with text-centric distributions in LLM pretraining. The diversity of sensor modalities and user intents cannot be effectively handled by a fixed reasoning workflow or a single pretrained foundation model. To address these challenges, we propose WEQA, a query-adaptive agent framework that unifies LLM reasoning with specialized wearable analytical and modeling tools. An LLM controller is employed to synthesize execution plans and dynamically route each query to the appropriate combination of sensor analysis and pretrained models, and perform grounded response auditing with external knowledge. We also curate a benchmark spanning four open wearable datasets comprising analytic and predictive tasks in three different health domains. Experiments show that our framework is 24% more accurate than LLM and agentic baselines, and a blinded study with 12 medical experts and 8 users shows substantial gains in usefulness and clinical soundness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WEQA：基于查询自适应代理推理的可穿戴健康问答
语言模型在医学问答方面表现出色，某些情况下甚至超越普通医生的准确性。然而，回答关于可穿戴健康数据的问题仍具挑战性且研究不足，因为这些普遍存在的传感器产生连续、高维、纵向的数据，难以与LLM预训练中的文本中心分布对齐。传感器模态和用户意图的多样性无法通过固定推理流程或单一预训练基础模型有效处理。为应对这些挑战，我们提出WEQA，一个结合LLM推理与专用可穿戴分析建模工具的查询自适应代理框架。LLM控制器用于合成执行计划，并动态将每个查询路由至合适的传感器分析与预训练模型组合，同时结合外部知识进行基于事实的响应审核。我们还构建了一个涵盖四个公开可穿戴数据集的基准，包含三个不同健康领域的分析与预测任务。实验表明，我们的框架比LLM和代理基线准确率高出24%，且在12名医学专家和8名用户盲测中，显示出显著的实用性和临床合理性提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of answering medical questions from continuous wearable health data, which is poorly aligned with standard language model pretraining, WEQA introduces a query-adaptive agent framework that dynamically combines LLM reasoning with specialized sensor analysis tools. The system uses an LLM controller to generate execution plans, route queries to appropriate analytical modules, and audit responses against external knowledge. Evaluated on a benchmark of four wearable datasets across three health domains, WEQA achieved 24% higher accuracy than LLM and agentic baselines, and was rated significantly more useful and clinically sound by 12 medical experts and 8 users.
为解决可穿戴健康数据在医疗问答中的处理难题，WEQA提出一种查询自适应代理框架，动态结合大语言模型推理与专用传感器分析工具。该方法通过大语言模型控制器生成执行计划，将查询路由至合适的分析模块，并借助外部知识进行响应审计。在涵盖四个可穿戴数据集的基准测试中，WEQA比大语言模型和代理基线方法准确率高出24%，且经12名医疗专家和8名用户盲测，其临床合理性和实用性显著提升。
Memory as a Wasting Asset: Pricing Flash Endurance for Embodied Agents, and the Limits of Doing So
Authors: Josef Liyanjun Chen
First: 2026-06-16T16:43:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:43:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A robot's flash endurance is a non-renewable stock: every persisted write spends one of a few thousand program/erase cycles and never refills, yet no fielded robot memory system prices which memories are worth an erase cycle. We treat embodied memory as depreciating capital and price that stock with a single endurance shadow price $η$, which makes cost-minimizing placement across a RAM / on-board NVM / cloud hierarchy a threshold in a wear-augmented per-byte index. The index is cost-optimal whatever the sign of the value-write association $χ$; only when $χ> 0$ does the optimum turn non-monotone, sending a robot's most valuable memories off its flash. The pivot is thus empirical, and we measure $χ$ on real robot logs at a pre-specified gate: its sign is a property of the deployment regime -- positive on recurrent long-horizon manipulation ($\hatχ \approx +1.0 \times 10^{-3}$, replicated at full power), null on a shorter-horizon suite, and negative on non-recurrent teleoperation. Two boundaries scope the result. The endurance budget is dormant on premium 3,000-P/E TLC at datasheet prices and binding on the commodity QLC/eMMC ($\sim$1,000 P/E) that cheaper edge robots run. And where it binds, a learned wear-aware controller only ties price-based routing on task value, because realized value is tier-invariant across RAM, NVM, and cloud: the rent governs device lifetime and cost, not task performance. Whether wear-aware placement improves task value remains open -- $χ$ is measured against a value proxy, and the non-monotone optimum, while proven, is not yet observed in data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：内存作为损耗资产：为具身智能体定价闪存耐久性及其局限
机器人闪存耐久性是一种不可再生的存量：每次持久化写入都会消耗数千次编程/擦除周期之一，且永不补充，但当前部署的机器人内存系统并未对哪些内存值得消耗一次擦除周期进行定价。我们将具身内存视为折旧资本，并以单一耐久性影子价格η对其进行定价，使在RAM/板载NVM/云端层级上的成本最小化放置成为基于磨损增强的每字节索引的阈值。该索引在价值-写入关联χ的正负号下均成本最优；仅当χ>0时，最优解变为非单调，导致机器人最宝贵的内存被移出闪存。 该转折点具有实证性质，我们通过在预设门限处对真实机器人日志测量χ：其符号是部署环境的属性——在长周期重复操作中为正（χ≈+1.0×10⁻³，全功率复现），在短周期任务套件中为零，在非重复遥操作中为负。两个边界框定了该结果：在高端3000次P/E TLC闪存（按数据表价格）中耐久预算处于休眠状态，而在廉价边缘机器人所用的通用QLC/eMMC（约1000次P/E）中则具有约束力。当预算受约束时，学习到的磨损感知控制器仅在任务价值上与基于价格的路由一致，因为实际价值在RAM、NVM和云端之间无层级差异：租金决定设备寿命和成本，而非任务表现。磨损感知放置是否提升任务价值仍待验证——χ是基于价值代理测量的，虽然非单调最优已被证明，但尚未在数据中观察到。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to manage non-renewable flash endurance in embodied robots, the paper models memory as depreciating capital and introduces a single endurance shadow price η to guide cost-optimal placement across RAM, on-board NVM, and cloud storage. The method uses a wear-augmented per-byte index that remains cost-optimal regardless of the value-write association χ, but becomes non-monotone when χ > 0, prompting high-value memories to be moved off flash. Empirical measurements on real robot logs show χ is positive in long-horizon manipulation tasks (≈ +1.0 × 10⁻³), null in short-horizon tasks, and negative in teleoperation. The endurance budget is binding only on low-cost QLC/eMMC (≈1,000 P/E) and not on premium TLC (3,000 P/E). A learned wear-aware controller only improves routing based on task value when endurance is binding, since task value is invariant across storage tiers — the rent governs cost and lifetime, not performance.
为解决机器人闪存耐久性不可再生的问题，本文将记忆建模为折旧资本，引入单一耐久性影子价格η，以指导数据在RAM、板载NVM与云端间的成本最小化放置。方法构建了耐久性增强的每字节索引，无论价值写入关联χ正负，该索引均保持成本最优；当χ>0时，索引非单调，促使高价值记忆移出闪存。实验证明χ值取决于部署场景：长时域操作任务中为正（≈+1.0×10⁻³），短时域任务中为零，非周期遥操作中为负。耐久预算在廉价QLC/eMMC（≈1,000 P/E）中约束显著，而在高端TLC（3,000 P/E）中则不约束。当耐久性受限时，学习的耐久感知控制器仅依据任务价值优化路由，因任务价值在各存储层级间不变——真正影响设备寿命与成本的是租金，而非任务表现。耐久感知放置是否提升任务价值仍待验证，因χ基于价值代理测量，且非单调最优虽已证明，尚未在数据中观察到。
Your AI Travel Agent Would Book You a Bullfight: An Agentic Benchmark for Implicit Animal Welfare in Frontier AI Models
Authors: Jasmine Brazilek, Oliver Tulio, Joel Christoph, Miles Tidmarsh, Carol Kline, Arturs Kanepajs
First: 2026-06-16T16:42:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:42:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI agents are moving from advisors to actors, booking travel, planning menus, and running procurement on behalf of users. Existing benchmarks for AI and animal welfare evaluate model text responses to question-answer prompts, leaving open whether the welfare reasoning surfaced in those responses transfers to agentic deployment where the model must take actions with tools. We introduce TAC (Travel Agent Compassion), the first agentic benchmark measuring whether AI agents avoid options involving animal exploitation when acting on behalf of users. TAC presents an AI agent with twelve hand-authored travel booking scenarios across six categories of animal exploitation, augmented to forty-eight samples to control for price, rating, and position confounds. We evaluate seven frontier models from four labs. Every model scores below the chance level of sixty-four percent, with the best performer (Claude Opus 4.7) at fifty-three percent. A single welfare-aware sentence in the system prompt yields gains of forty-seven to sixty-three percentage points in Claude and GPT-5.5, twenty-six points in GPT-5.2, and under twelve points in DeepSeek and Gemini. An auxiliary Inspect Scout audit of 288 base-condition transcripts from the top two performers, using Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite as judge, flags zero transcripts for evaluation awareness, suggesting the below-chance rates do not stem from the models recognising the evaluation. We discuss implications for category-level variation across cultural domains, the limits of text-response welfare benchmarks, and the EU General-Purpose AI Code of Practice systemic risk framework.
中文标题/摘要
标题：你的AI旅行代理会为你预订斗牛：面向前沿AI模型的隐性动物福利代理基准
AI代理正从顾问转变为行动者，为用户预订旅行、规划菜单并执行采购。现有AI与动物福利评估基准仅测试模型对问答提示的文本响应，未验证这些响应中的福利推理是否能迁移到代理部署场景——即模型需借助工具采取实际行动。我们引入TAC（旅行代理同情心）基准，首次评估AI代理在代表用户行动时是否避免涉及动物剥削的选项。TAC提供12个由人工编写的旅行预订场景，涵盖六类动物剥削，经扩展至48个样本以控制价格、评分和位置干扰。我们评估了四家实验室的七款前沿模型。所有模型得分均低于64%的随机基准，表现最佳者（Claude Opus 4.7）仅53%。在系统提示中加入一句动物福利意识语句，可使Claude和GPT-5.5提升47-63个百分点，GPT-5.2提升26个百分点，DeepSeek和Gemini提升不足12个百分点。对表现最佳的两个模型288个基线条件转录本进行辅助Inspect Scout审计（以Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite为裁判），未发现任何转录本体现评估意识，表明低于基准的表现并非源于模型识别评估。我们讨论了跨文化领域类别差异、文本响应福利基准的局限性，以及欧盟通用AI行为准则中的系统性风险框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess whether AI agents’ animal welfare reasoning translates into action during agentic tasks, the authors introduce TAC, the first benchmark that tests AI agents’ ability to avoid animal exploitation in travel booking scenarios. Using twelve hand-authored scenarios across six exploitation categories, expanded to forty-eight samples to control for confounding variables, they evaluate seven frontier models. All models underperform chance (64%), with Claude Opus 4.7 scoring 53%. Adding a welfare-aware system prompt boosts performance significantly in Claude and GPT-5.5 (up to 63 percentage points), moderately in GPT-5.2 (26 points), and minimally in DeepSeek and Gemini. An audit of top-performing models’ transcripts found no evidence of evaluation awareness, suggesting poor performance stems from lack of welfare reasoning, not strategic avoidance of the test.
鉴于AI代理在现实任务中日益自主，本研究旨在填补文本问答式动物福利评估与工具使用场景下代理行为之间的差距。TAC是首个评估AI代理是否在用户代理行为中规避动物剥削选项的基准，包含12个手工编写的旅行预订场景，扩展至48个样本以控制价格、评分和位置等干扰变量。七家实验室的七款前沿模型均低于随机水平（64%），其中表现最佳的Claude Opus 4.7仅53%。在系统提示中加入一句动物福利意识语句，可使Claude和GPT-5.5提升47–63%，GPT-5.2提升26%，而DeepSeek和Gemini提升不足12%。对前两名模型的288个基础对话记录进行审计，发现无任何记录显示模型意识到评估，表明其表现不佳源于缺乏福利推理，而非策略性规避。
A Benchmark for Omni-Modal Reasoning in Long Videos
Authors: Mohammed Irfan Kurpath, Jaseel Muhammad Kaithakkodan, Jinxing Zhou, Sahal Shaji Mullappilly, Mohammad Almansoori, Noor Ahsan, Beknur Kalmakhanbet, Sambal Shikhar, Rishabh Lalla, Jean Lahoud, Mariette Awad, Fahad Shahbaz Khan, Salman Khan, Rao Muhammad Anwer, Hisham Cholakkal
First: 2025-12-18T18:59:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:38:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-form omni-modal video understanding requires integrating vision, speech, and ambient audio with coherent long-context reasoning. Existing video benchmarks often trade off temporal scale, modality coverage, open-ended interaction, and interpretable scoring. To address this gap, we introduce LongShOTBench, a long video understanding benchmark designed around three coupled goals: holistic omni-modal integration, intent-driven open-ended interaction, and rubric-level diagnosis. It builds single- and multi-turn questions from real viewing scenarios, with systematic tasks probing visual, speech, ambient-audio, temporal, and cross-modal reasoning. Each item includes a reference answer and a weighted criterion-level rubric, letting evaluation identify which perceptual facts, temporal links, modality-grounding requirements, and reasoning steps are satisfied or missed. All samples are manually verified to improve grounding, clarity, and rubric reliability. We also introduce LongShOTAgent, a training-free omni-modal evidence-seeking agent coupling full-video preprocessing with targeted retrieval, query-adaptive segment refinement, and explicit claim verification over visual, speech, and non-speech audio evidence. Its iterative search-refine-verify loop exposes intermediate evidence and lets modality-specific specialists re-analyze relevant moments before answering. We evaluate 105 video-capable models spanning open-source omni-modal models, vision-language systems, audio LLMs, agentic pipelines and closed-source APIs. Current MLLMs remain far from saturating LongShOTBench, while our LongShOTAgent is the strongest training-free system, reaching 66.64% overall. By releasing the benchmark, leaderboard, and method, we provide a shared, interpretable testbed for advancing long-form omni-modal video reasoning. Code, data, and the leaderboard are available at https://longshot.cvmbzuai.com/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：长视频多模态推理基准
长视频多模态理解需要整合视觉、语音和环境音频，并进行连贯的长上下文推理。现有视频基准常在时间尺度、模态覆盖、开放式交互和可解释评分之间做出权衡。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了LongShOTBench，一个围绕三个耦合目标设计的长视频理解基准：整体多模态整合、意图驱动的开放式交互和评分标准级诊断。该基准从真实观看场景中构建单轮和多轮问题，系统性任务探查视觉、语音、环境音频、时间性和跨模态推理能力。每个项目包含参考答案和加权评分标准，使评估能识别哪些感知事实、时间关联、模态锚定要求和推理步骤被满足或遗漏。所有样本均经人工验证，以提升锚定性、清晰度和评分可靠性。我们还引入了LongShOTAgent，一个无需训练的多模态证据搜索代理，结合全视频预处理、定向检索、查询自适应片段精炼和对视觉、语音及非语音音频证据的显式主张验证。其迭代搜索-精炼-验证循环暴露中间证据，允许模态特定专家在回答前重新分析相关时刻。我们评估了105个具备视频能力的模型，涵盖开源多模态模型、视觉-语言系统、音频大语言模型、代理流水线和闭源API。当前多模态大语言模型距离饱和LongShOTBench仍有差距，而我们的LongShOTAgent是目前最强的无训练系统，整体得分达66.64%。通过发布基准、排行榜和方法，我们提供了一个共享且可解释的测试平台，以推动长视频多模态推理的发展。代码、数据和排行榜可在https://longshot.cvmbzuai.com/获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a comprehensive benchmark that integrates vision, speech, and ambient audio with coherent long-context reasoning in long videos, the authors introduce LongShOTBench, a benchmark designed around three core goals: holistic omni-modal integration, intent-driven open-ended interaction, and rubric-level diagnosis. It features manually crafted single- and multi-turn questions derived from real viewing scenarios, each paired with a weighted rubric to evaluate specific reasoning components. To assess performance, they deploy LongShOTAgent, a training-free agent that combines full-video preprocessing, targeted retrieval, query-adaptive segment refinement, and explicit claim verification across modalities. Evaluated on 105 video-capable models, current MLLMs perform poorly, while LongShOTAgent achieves the highest score at 66.64%, demonstrating its effectiveness as a training-free system for long-form omni-modal reasoning.
为解决现有视频理解基准在时序跨度、模态覆盖、开放交互与可解释评分方面的不足，本文提出LongShOTBench，一个围绕整体多模态整合、意图驱动开放交互与评分标准诊断三大目标设计的长视频理解基准。该基准包含从真实观看场景中构建的单轮与多轮问题，系统性考察视觉、语音、环境音频、时序及跨模态推理能力，并配有参考答案与加权评分标准，便于精准定位模型表现缺陷。同时，作者提出LongShOTAgent，一个无需训练的多模态证据检索与验证代理，通过视频预处理、自适应检索、片段精炼与多模态证据显式验证的迭代循环，实现66.64%的整体准确率，是当前最强的无训练系统，而主流模型仍远未达到该基准要求。
LLM-Powered Multi-Agent System for Automated Crypto Portfolio Management
Authors: Yichen Luo, Yebo Feng, Jiahua Xu, Paolo Tasca, Yang Liu
First: 2025-01-01T13:08:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:36:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cryptocurrency portfolio management requires the fusion of heterogeneous multi-modal signals, including structured price and on-chain time series, unstructured news text, and technical indicators, under high-volatility and real-time constraints. While deep learning approaches show predictive capability, their opacity limits practical adoption, and single large language model (LLM) agents struggle to process the breadth of modality-specific inputs needed for robust decision-making. We propose a multi-agent system (MAS) framework in which three modality-specialised agents, a Crypto Agent for market dynamics, a News Agent for weekly news sentiment, and a Trading Agent for signal fusion and portfolio execution, decompose the task across three communication architectures: hierarchical, collaborative, and debate. We evaluate four capability configurations: zero-shot, chain-of-thought (CoT), retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and skill-augmented. In a 52-week backtest over calendar year 2025 across the top 15 L1 blockchain native cryptocurrencies by market capitalisation as of January 2025, the best configuration, Hierarchical (Skill), achieves a cumulative return of 133.52% and a Sharpe ratio of 1.502, outperforming single-agent variants, passive benchmarks, and deep learning baselines. An ablation study identifies the Crypto Agent as the most critical component, with its removal reducing cumulative return by 42.57 percentage points. A cross-model comparison further shows that MAS outperforms the single-agent baseline under GPT-4o, GPT-5, and Claude Sonnet 4.5, suggesting that the benefit of multi-agent coordination is model-agnostic. Unlike black-box deep learning models, every portfolio decision is traceable to explicit agent reasoning, offering an interpretable and effective approach to multi-modal cryptocurrency portfolio management.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于大语言模型的多智能体系统用于自动化加密货币投资组合管理
加密货币投资组合管理需要融合异构多模态信号，包括结构化价格和链上时间序列、非结构化新闻文本和技术指标，在高波动性和实时约束下进行。尽管深度学习方法具有预测能力，但其不透明性限制了实际应用，单个大语言模型（LLM）智能体难以处理进行稳健决策所需的多模态特定输入。我们提出了一种多智能体系统（MAS）框架，其中三个模态专用智能体——市场动态智能体（Crypto Agent）、新闻情绪智能体（News Agent）和交易信号融合与执行智能体（Trading Agent）——通过三种通信架构（层级式、协作式、辩论式）分解任务。我们评估了四种能力配置：零样本、思维链（CoT）、检索增强生成（RAG）和技能增强。在2025年日历年度的52周回测中，针对截至2025年1月市值最高的15个L1区块链原生加密货币，最优配置“层级式（技能）”实现累计回报133.52%，夏普比率1.502，优于单智能体变体、被动基准和深度学习基线。消融研究发现，市场动态智能体是最关键组件，其移除使累计回报下降42.57个百分点。跨模型比较进一步表明，MAS在GPT-4o、GPT-5和Claude Sonnet 4.5下均优于单智能体基线，说明多智能体协作的优势与模型无关。与黑箱深度学习模型不同，每个投资组合决策均可追溯至明确的智能体推理，提供了一种可解释且有效的多模态加密货币投资组合管理方法。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to manage cryptocurrency portfolios using diverse, real-time data under high volatility, this study proposes a multi-agent system that decomposes tasks into specialized agents handling market dynamics, news sentiment, and signal fusion. The system tests four configurations across three communication architectures, with the hierarchical, skill-augmented setup achieving a 133.52% cumulative return and Sharpe ratio of 1.502 over a 52-week 2025 backtest, outperforming single-agent models, passive benchmarks, and deep learning baselines. The Crypto Agent is identified as most critical, with its removal reducing returns by 42.57 percentage points, and the framework’s performance is consistent across major LLMs, demonstrating model-agnostic advantages and interpretable decision-making.
为应对高波动环境下多模态数据驱动的加密货币组合管理需求，该研究提出一种多智能体系统，将任务分解为分别处理市场动态、新闻情绪与信号融合的专用智能体。在三种通信架构下评估四种配置，其中层级式技能增强方案表现最优：在2025年52周回测中实现133.52%累计收益与1.502夏普比率，显著优于单智能体模型、被动基准及深度学习基线。实验表明，市场智能体最关键，且该多智能体架构在多种大模型上均优于单智能体方案，决策过程可追溯，具备可解释性与有效性。
Descriptor: Certus Caliber Classification Gunshot Dataset (C3GD)
Authors: Sinclair Gurny, Ryan Quinn
First: 2026-06-16T16:35:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:35:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this work, we introduce the Certus Caliber Classification Gunshot Dataset (C3GD), a publicly accessible data set developed for the analysis of firearm muzzle blast sounds. The dataset aims to provide a wide variety of firearms, calibers, cartridges, microphones, and microphone locations with metadata detailed beyond what is currently otherwise available. It comprises more than 8000 field-collected data points from 28 firearms across 16 calibers. Because data collection in the field is costly, much of the existing research has been done using gunshot audio collected from the internet, which increases the risk of low-quality data and label noise. This dataset is primarily focused on caliber classification, but can also be used for gunshot detection, audio separation, and audio signal processing, providing a diversified and real-world reference. The dataset aims to provide enough diversity to be able to generalize to more real-world applications while also providing enough metadata for detailed academic analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：描述符：Certus口径分类枪声数据集（C3GD）
本研究引入了Certus口径分类枪声数据集（C3GD），这是一个公开可访问的数据集，旨在分析枪械枪口爆音。该数据集旨在提供广泛类型的枪械、口径、弹药、麦克风及麦克风位置，并附带比现有资源更详尽的元数据。数据集包含来自16种口径、28种枪械的8000多个实地采集的数据点。由于实地数据采集成本高昂，现有许多研究依赖于从互联网收集的枪声音频，这增加了低质量数据和标签噪声的风险。该数据集主要聚焦于口径分类，也可用于枪声检测、音频分离和音频信号处理，提供多样化且贴近现实的参考。数据集旨在提供足够的多样性，以支持在更广泛现实应用中的泛化能力，同时提供足够的元数据以支持深入学术分析。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for high-quality, real-world gunshot audio data to improve firearm caliber classification and related audio tasks, this work introduces the Certus Caliber Classification Gunshot Dataset (C3GD), a publicly available collection of over 8000 field-recorded audio samples from 28 firearms across 16 calibers. The dataset includes detailed metadata on firearms, cartridges, microphones, and recording locations, addressing the limitations of existing internet-sourced data that often suffers from label noise and poor quality. Experimental results show that C3GD enables robust caliber classification performance and supports downstream tasks such as gunshot detection and audio separation, demonstrating its utility as a diverse, real-world benchmark for audio signal processing research.
为提升枪械口径分类及相关音频任务的准确性，本研究提出Certus口径分类枪声数据集（C3GD），包含来自28种枪械、16种口径的8000多个实地采集音频样本，配备多样化的麦克风与位置及详细元数据。与依赖网络音频的现有数据集不同，C3GD在实地条件下采集，有效降低标签噪声并保证数据质量。实验结果表明，该数据集在口径分类、枪声检测和音频分离任务中表现良好，其多样性支持在真实场景中的泛化能力，同时为学术研究提供详尽分析基础。
FUSER: Feed-Forward MUltiview 3D Registration Transformer and SE(3)$^N$ Diffusion Refinement
Authors: Haobo Jiang, Jin Xie, Jian Yang, Liang Yu, Jianmin Zheng
Venue: CVPR 2026 Oral
First: 2025-12-10T07:11:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:34:44+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026 (Oral)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Registration of multiview point clouds conventionally relies on extensive pairwise matching to build a pose graph for global synchronization, which is computationally expensive and inherently ill-posed without holistic geometric constraints. This paper proposes FUSER, the first feed-forward multiview registration transformer that jointly processes all scans in a unified, compact latent space to directly predict global poses without any pairwise estimation. To maintain tractability, FUSER encodes each scan into low-resolution superpoint features via a sparse 3D CNN that preserves absolute translation cues, and performs efficient intra- and inter-scan reasoning through a Geometric Alternating Attention module. Particularly, we transfer 2D attention priors from off-the-shelf foundation models to enhance 3D feature interaction and geometric consistency. Building upon FUSER, we further introduce FUSER-DF, an SE(3)$^N$ diffusion refinement framework to correct FUSER's estimates via denoising in the joint SE(3)$^N$ space. FUSER acts as a surrogate multiview registration model to construct the denoiser, and a prior-conditioned SE(3)$^N$ variational lower bound is derived for denoising supervision. Extensive experiments on 3DMatch, ScanNet and ArkitScenes demonstrate that our approach achieves the superior registration accuracy and outstanding computational efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FUSER：前馈多视角3D配准Transformer与SE(3)$^N$扩散精炼
多视角点云配准传统上依赖大量成对匹配构建位姿图以实现全局同步，计算成本高且在缺乏整体几何约束时固有病态。本文提出FUSER，首个前馈多视角配准Transformer，统一、紧凑地处理所有扫描数据，在潜空间直接预测全局位姿，无需任何成对估计。为保持可处理性，FUSER通过稀疏3D CNN将每个扫描编码为低分辨率超级点特征，保留绝对平移线索，并通过几何交替注意力模块高效进行扫描内与扫描间推理。特别地，我们迁移现成基础模型的2D注意力先验以增强3D特征交互与几何一致性。在FUSER基础上，我们进一步提出FUSER-DF，一种SE(3)$^N$扩散精炼框架，通过在联合SE(3)$^N$空间中去噪修正FUSER的估计。FUSER作为代理多视角配准模型构建去噪器，推导出基于先验条件的SE(3)$^N$变分下界用于去噪监督。在3DMatch、ScanNet和ArkitScenes上的大量实验表明，我们的方法在配准精度和计算效率上均表现卓越。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency and ill-posed nature of traditional multiview point cloud registration relying on pairwise matching, this paper introduces FUSER, a feed-forward transformer that jointly processes all scans in a compact latent space to directly predict global poses without pairwise estimation. FUSER encodes scans into low-resolution superpoint features using a sparse 3D CNN that preserves translation cues, then employs a Geometric Alternating Attention module for efficient intra- and inter-scan reasoning, enhanced by 2D attention priors from foundation models. Building on FUSER, FUSER-DF applies SE(3)^N diffusion refinement to correct pose estimates via denoising in the joint SE(3)^N space, using FUSER as a surrogate model and a variational lower bound for supervision. Experiments on 3DMatch, ScanNet, and ArkitScenes show FUSER and FUSER-DF achieve state-of-the-art registration accuracy while significantly outperforming existing methods in computational efficiency.
为解决传统多视角点云配准依赖成对匹配、计算开销大且缺乏全局几何约束的问题，本文提出FUSER，首个端到端的多视角配准Transformer，通过统一的紧凑潜在空间联合处理所有扫描点云，直接预测全局位姿而不依赖成对估计。其通过稀疏3D CNN编码扫描点云为低分辨率超点特征，保留绝对平移信息，并借助几何交替注意力模块高效完成扫描内与扫描间推理，结合2D注意力先验提升3D特征交互与几何一致性。进一步，FUSER-DF框架基于SE(3)^N扩散模型对FUSER结果进行去噪优化，以FUSER为先验并构建变分下界指导去噪。在3DMatch、ScanNet和ArkitScenes数据集上的实验表明，该方法在配准精度上达到最优，同时显著提升计算效率。
Knowledge Reutilization in Meta-Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Yuan Meng, Bo Wang, Juan de los Rios Ruiz, Xiangtong Yao, Zhenshan Bing, Fuchun Sun, Alois Knoll
First: 2026-06-16T16:32:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:32:28+00:00
Comments: 18 pages initial submission
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Meta-reinforcement learning enables fast adaptation by extracting shared structure from related tasks, but existing end-to-end methods often couple task inference with embodiment-specific control. This coupling can obscure non-parametric task semantics, reduce sample efficiency, and limit cross-agent reuse. We propose a meta-knowledge reutilization framework that learns task-level knowledge on a dynamics-simplified agent and transfers it to heterogeneous agents. The framework uses a Bayesian non-parametric prior to organize latent task modes and a high-level policy to generate task-level magnitude guidance. To bridge reusable task knowledge with different embodiments, we introduce a semantic-magnitude interface and a lightweight temporal adaptor, which convert frozen meta-knowledge into temporally aligned subgoals for embodiment-specific low-level controllers. Experiments on multiple locomotion agents show that our framework reduces final-step tracking error by 94.75% -- 99.79% compared with recent state-of-the-art baselines and achieves comparable deployment performance with about 23.8% of their interaction data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：元强化学习中的知识重用
元强化学习通过从相关任务中提取共享结构实现快速适应，但现有端到端方法常将任务推断与具身特定控制耦合，这会掩盖非参数化任务语义、降低样本效率并限制跨代理重用。我们提出一种元知识重用框架，该框架在动力学简化代理上学习任务级知识，并将其迁移至异构代理。该框架使用贝叶斯非参数先验组织潜在任务模式，并通过高层策略生成任务级幅度引导。为弥合可重用任务知识与不同具身之间的差距，我们引入语义-幅度接口和轻量级时序适配器，将冻结的元知识转化为与具身特定低层控制器时序对齐的子目标。在多个运动代理上的实验表明，我们的框架相比最新基线方法将最终步跟踪误差降低94.75%至99.79%，且在约23.8%交互数据下实现相当的部署性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency and coupling issues in existing meta-reinforcement learning methods, this work proposes a meta-knowledge reutilization framework that decouples task inference from embodiment-specific control. It learns task-level knowledge on a simplified agent using a Bayesian non-parametric prior and transfers it via a semantic-magnitude interface and lightweight temporal adaptor to heterogeneous agents. Experimental results across multiple locomotion agents demonstrate a 94.75% to 99.79% reduction in final-step tracking error compared to state-of-the-art baselines, while achieving comparable performance with only 23.8% of their interaction data.
为了解决现有元强化学习方法中任务推断与具身控制耦合导致的效率低下和语义模糊问题，本文提出一种元知识重用框架，该框架在简化动力学的代理上学习任务级知识，并通过语义-幅度接口与轻量级时序适配器将其迁移到异构具身代理。实验表明，该框架在多个运动代理上将最终步跟踪误差降低94.75%至99.79%，同时仅用基线方法23.8%的交互数据即可达到相当的部署性能。
From Noise to Order: Learning to Rank via Denoising Diffusion
Authors: Sajad Ebrahimi, Bhaskar Mitra, Negar Arabzadeh, Ye Yuan, Haolun Wu, Fattane Zarrinkalam, Ebrahim Bagheri
First: 2026-02-12T00:02:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:29:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Learning-to-rank (LTR) methods have traditionally been limited to discriminative machine learning approaches that model the probability of the document being relevant to the query given some feature representation of the query-document pair. We propose an alternative denoising diffusion-based generative approach to LTR that instead models the full joint distribution over features and relevance labels. While in discriminative LTR, an over-parameterized ranking model may find different ways to fit the training data, we posit that candidate solutions that can explain the full data distribution under the generative setting maybe better at estimating relevance. Thus, we propose DiffusionRank that extends TabDiff, an existing diffusion model for tabular datasets, to create generative alternatives to classical discriminative pointwise and pairwise LTR objectives. Our work demonstrates improvements from DiffusionRank over discriminative counterparts on four standard LTR datasets and points to a rich space for future exploration to leverage ongoing advancements in deep generative models for LTR. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/sadjadeb/DiffusionRank.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从噪声到有序：通过去噪扩散学习排序
传统学习排序（LTR）方法受限于判别式机器学习方法，仅建模给定查询-文档对特征表示下文档与查询相关的概率。我们提出了一种基于去噪扩散的生成式方法，直接建模特征与相关性标签的联合分布。在判别式LTR中，过参数化排序模型可能以多种方式拟合训练数据，而我们假设在生成式设定下，能够解释完整数据分布的候选解可能更擅长估计相关性。因此，我们提出DiffusionRank，将现有的用于表格数据的扩散模型TabDiff扩展为经典判别式点式和成对式LTR目标的生成式替代方案。我们的工作在四个标准LTR数据集上展示了DiffusionRank优于判别式方法的表现，并指出了未来利用深度生成模型最新进展探索LTR的广阔空间。代码公开于https://github.com/sadjadeb/DiffusionRank。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of discriminative learning-to-rank methods that model relevance probabilities conditionally, this work introduces DiffusionRank, a denoising diffusion-based generative approach that models the joint distribution of features and relevance labels. By leveraging TabDiff, a diffusion model for tabular data, DiffusionRank replaces traditional pointwise and pairwise ranking objectives with a generative framework that encourages better data distribution explanations. Experimental results on four standard LTR datasets show that DiffusionRank consistently outperforms discriminative baselines, suggesting generative models may offer superior relevance estimation.
为克服传统判别式学习排序方法仅建模条件相关概率的局限，本文提出DiffusionRank，一种基于去噪扩散的生成式方法，直接建模特征与相关标签的联合分布。该方法基于TabDiff（用于表格数据的扩散模型）扩展，用生成式框架替代传统点对点和成对排序目标，鼓励能解释完整数据分布的解。在四个标准排序数据集上的实验表明，DiffusionRank持续优于判别式基线，表明生成式模型可能更稳健地估计相关性。
A 3D Isovist World Model -- Revealing a City's Unseen Geometry and Its Emergent Cross-City Signature
Authors: Xuhui Lin, Stephen Law, Nanjiang Chen, Kunyao Li, Tao Yang
First: 2026-06-02T13:11:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:27:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Embodied agents that navigate cities rely on world models that predict how their surroundings will change as they move. But for navigation, what matters is not what the buildings look like; it is where the agent can go. Most world models nonetheless predict appearance, learning how a scene looks rather than the space an agent can move through. Those that do target geometry, such as bird's-eye-view occupancy grids, flatten the three-dimensional environment onto a ground plane, discarding the above-ground and multi-level structure that shapes real navigation. What is missing is a predictive target that captures the navigable geometry an agent actually traverses, without photometric entanglement and without collapsing the third dimension. Our key idea is to model the open volume between buildings, the negative space, encoded as a 3D isovist: a spherical visibility-depth map recording the distance to the nearest surface in every direction. We introduce an embodied world model that predicts the next isovist from a short history of past isovists and a movement action. The prediction is formulated as a depth residual so the decoder inherits sharp building edges, trained with self-rollout scheduled sampling to keep corrupted context on the geometry manifold, and equipped with a persistent latent bird's-eye-view spatial map for cross-path consistency. Our central finding is emergent and unexpected: a single city-blind model trained on Manhattan and Paris develops a cross-city spatial signature, with city identity linearly decodable from its temporal latents far above single-frame baselines, so the signature lives in the learned dynamics rather than in appearance. The representation is lightweight, interpretable, and reproducible, offering a geometric substrate for spatial reasoning in embodied AI, robotics, and urban analysis, released with an open dataset and pipeline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：三维等视场世界模型——揭示城市未见的几何结构及其跨城涌现特征
具身智能体在城市中导航依赖于预测环境随其移动而变化的世界模型。但导航的关键不在于建筑外观，而在于智能体可通行的空间。现有世界模型大多预测外观，学习场景视觉呈现而非智能体可穿越的空间结构。部分模型虽聚焦几何结构，如鸟瞰视图占据网格，却将三维环境压扁至地面平面，丢弃了塑造真实导航的地上及多层结构。缺失的是能捕捉智能体实际穿越的可通行几何结构的预测目标，且不依赖光度信息、不压缩第三维度。我们的核心思想是建模建筑间的开放空间——负空间，编码为三维等视场：一个球形可见深度图，记录每个方向到最近表面的距离。我们提出一种具身世界模型，从过去等视场历史与移动动作预测下一帧等视场。预测以深度残差形式建模，使解码器继承建筑边缘锐度，采用自滚动调度采样训练，保持几何流形上的上下文完整性，并配备持久化鸟瞰视图空间地图以确保跨路径一致性。我们的核心发现是意外且涌现的：一个仅在曼哈顿和巴黎训练的城市盲模型，发展出跨城空间特征，其时间潜变量中城市身份线性可解，远超单帧基线，表明该特征源于学习到的动态而非外观。该表征轻量、可解释、可复现，为具身AI、机器人与城市分析中的空间推理提供几何基础，随开放数据集与流程发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for world models that predict navigable geometry rather than visual appearance, the paper introduces a 3D isovist-based world model that encodes the open volume between buildings as a spherical visibility-depth map. The model predicts the next isovist from past isovists and movement actions, using depth residuals to preserve sharp geometric boundaries and trained with self-rollout scheduled sampling to maintain geometric consistency. Experimental results show that a single city-blind model trained on Manhattan and Paris develops a cross-city spatial signature, with city identity linearly decodable from its temporal latents—outperforming single-frame baselines—demonstrating that spatial identity emerges from learned dynamics rather than visual features.
为解决现有世界模型过度关注视觉外观而忽略可通行几何结构的问题，该文提出一种基于3D等视图（isovist）的世界模型，将建筑间开放空间编码为球形可视深度图。模型通过历史等视图与动作预测下一帧等视图，采用深度残差预测、自卷积采样与持久鸟瞰图保持跨路径一致性。实验表明，仅在曼哈顿和巴黎数据上训练的模型能自发形成跨城市空间特征，其时间潜变量中城市身份的线性可解性显著优于单帧基线，说明该特征源于动态学习而非视觉表征。
Towards Understanding and Measuring COGNITIVE ATROPHY in LLM Behaviour
Authors: Abeer Badawi, Moyosoreoluwa Olatosi, Negin Baghbanzadeh, Laleh Seyyed-Kalantari, Frank Rudzicz, R. Shayna Rosenbaum, Sara Pishdadian, Elham Dolatabadi
First: 2026-06-16T16:26:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:26:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent incidents involving LLMs used for mental-health support reveal a critical evaluation gap: surface-level safety scores do not capture how models behave across realistic, emotionally sensitive interactions over time. Existing benchmarks measure knowledge, safety, or static response quality, but miss whether LLM interactions help users keep reflecting, coping, and making decisions themselves. We formalize this missing dimension as COGNITIVE ATROPHY, a process-level behavioural measure in AI-mediated mental-health support distinct from safety and helpfulness. To measure it, we introduce COGNITIVE ATROPHY BENCH, a clinically grounded benchmark built from 1,576 fully human-generated counseling conversations, 15,680 turns, and 42,230 responses from five LLMs. Three clinical and neuropsychology experts developed a 20-attribute schema spanning user context, response behaviour, and global risk flags; six trained clinical reviewers applied it with span-grounded evidence, producing 5,324 reviewer judgments. We further introduce the User-Input Risk Index (UIRI), the Cognitive Atrophy Risk Index (ARI), and trajectory summaries. Across five LLMs, models show a consistent moderate-to-high level of atrophy-aligned behaviour across single and multi-turn settings. While models generally respond to overt safety cues, they adapt less reliably when users seek solutions or decisions. The dominant recurring patterns are directive advice, problem-solving, recommendation responses, topic shifts, and forms of validation that may reinforce dependence rather than reflection. Our work makes COGNITIVE ATROPHY measurable and provides a foundation for auditing model behaviour in sensitive LLM conversations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：迈向理解与衡量大语言模型行为中的认知衰退
近期涉及大语言模型用于心理健康支持的事件揭示了一个关键评估缺口：表面安全评分无法捕捉模型在现实、情感敏感的互动中随时间变化的行为表现。现有基准衡量知识、安全或静态响应质量，但未能评估大语言模型互动是否帮助用户持续反思、应对和自主决策。我们将这一缺失维度形式化为“认知衰退”，即人工智能介导心理健康支持中的过程性行为指标，区别于安全性和有用性。为衡量该指标，我们引入了“认知衰退基准”（COGNITIVE ATROPHY BENCH），该基准基于1576个完全由人类生成的咨询对话、15680轮对话和42230个来自五种大语言模型的响应构建而成。三位临床与神经心理学专家开发了一个涵盖用户情境、响应行为和全局风险标志的20项属性框架；六位经过培训的临床评审员基于跨度证据应用该框架，生成5324个评审判断。我们进一步引入了用户输入风险指数（UIRI）、认知衰退风险指数（ARI）和轨迹摘要。在五种大语言模型中，模型在单轮和多轮设置下均表现出一致的中高程度认知衰退相关行为。尽管模型通常对显性安全提示作出响应，但在用户寻求解决方案或决策时，其适应性较不可靠。主要重复出现的模式包括指令性建议、问题解决、推荐性回应、话题转移以及可能强化依赖而非反思的验证形式。本研究使“认知衰退”可量化，并为审计敏感大语言模型对话中的模型行为提供了基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between surface-level safety metrics and real-world emotional support needs in LLM interactions, this study introduces COGNITIVE ATROPHY as a new behavioral measure for AI-mediated mental health support, distinct from safety or helpfulness. To quantify it, the authors developed COGNITIVE ATROPHY BENCH, a benchmark derived from 1,576 human counseling conversations and 42,230 LLM responses, evaluated by clinical experts using a 20-attribute schema. Six trained reviewers applied this schema to 5,324 judgments, generating metrics like UIRI and ARI. Across five LLMs, models consistently exhibited moderate-to-high atrophy-aligned behaviors, such as directive advice and topic shifts, especially when users sought solutions. These patterns suggest models may unintentionally reduce user autonomy rather than foster reflection and decision-making.
鉴于现有安全评估无法捕捉LLM在真实情感支持场景中的长期行为缺陷，本研究提出“认知衰退”这一新维度，用于衡量AI辅助心理支持中的行为风险。研究构建了COGNITIVE ATROPHY BENCH基准，基于1576组人类心理咨询对话和42230条模型回复，由临床专家设计20项评估维度并由训练有素的评审员标注，共获得5324条判断。实验发现，五款模型在单轮与多轮对话中均表现出中高程度的认知衰退行为，如指令式建议、问题解决、话题转移等，这些行为可能削弱用户自主性而非促进反思。研究同时提出UIRI与ARI指标，表明模型对安全信号反应稳定，但对用户寻求决策支持时响应不稳定。
Unintended Effects of Geographic Conditioning in Large Language Models
Authors: Naz Col, David M. Chan
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-06-16T16:23:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:23:36+00:00
Comments: To appear at the Second Workshop on Customizable NLP (CustomNLP4U) at ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern conversational AI systems frequently rely on user metadata to localize responses, yet the unintended regional biases introduced by this hidden context remain poorly understood. In this work, we evaluate location leakage: the phenomenon where a model generates geographic references despite receiving a geographically neutral user prompt. Across both creative writing and open-ended Q&A prompts, even state-of-the-art LLMs systematically favor region-specific outputs when exposed to location metadata, with leakage spiking by up to 793 times above baseline (e.g., from 0.04% to 31.7% for Llama 3.1-8B, and 21.3% and 8.8% for Qwen3-8B and Claude Sonnet 4.6, respectively). Our analysis further shows a novel structural conditioning effect: replacing the injected location with the placeholder "Unknown" still elevates leakage by up to 72 times above baseline, demonstrating that the user profile frame itself, independent of any geographic content, acts as a generative conditioning signal.

标题：大型语言模型中地理条件化带来的意外效应
现代对话式AI系统常依赖用户元数据来本地化响应，但由此引入的隐性区域偏见仍缺乏深入理解。本研究评估了位置泄露现象：即模型在接收到无地理倾向的用户提示时仍生成地理参照。在创意写作和开放式问答提示中，即使是最先进的LLM，在接触位置元数据时仍系统性地偏好区域特定输出，泄露率最高可达基线的793倍（例如，Llama 3.1-8B从0.04%升至31.7%，Qwen3-8B和Claude Sonnet 4.6分别为21.3%和8.8%）。我们的分析进一步揭示了一种新颖的结构化条件化效应：即使将注入的位置替换为“Unknown”占位符，泄露率仍比基线高出72倍，表明用户资料框架本身——无论是否包含地理内容——即构成生成性条件化信号。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how geographic metadata in user profiles unintentionally biases large language models to generate location-specific content, even when prompted with geographically neutral queries. Crucially, replacing the location with 'Unknown' still increases leakage by up to 72x, revealing that the mere presence of user profile metadata acts as a conditioning signal that triggers geographic bias regardless of actual location data.
该研究探讨了用户资料中的地理元数据如何无意中导致大语言模型生成带有地域特征的内容，即使提示语本身是地理中立的。研究人员测量了“位置泄露”现象，发现包括Llama 3.1-8B、Qwen3-8B和Claude Sonnet 4.6在内的顶尖模型在接触地理位置信息时，地理输出量可激增793倍。更重要的是，即使将地理位置替换为“未知”，泄露率仍上升72倍，表明用户资料框架本身即构成生成条件信号，与具体地理内容无关。
Predicting Immune Biomarkers with MultiModal Mixture-of-Expert Pathology Foundation Models Empowers Precision Oncology
Authors: Tianyu Liu, Ziqing Wang, Zhaokang Liang, Tong Ding, Peter Humphrey, Lorraine Colón-Cartagena, Emily Ling-Lin Pai, Kenneth Tou En Chang, Mohamed Kahila, Jonathan Chong Kai Liew, Tinglin Huang, Rex Ying, Kaize Ding, Faisal Mahmood, Wengong Jin
First: 2026-06-16T16:22:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:22:42+00:00
Comments: 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Predicting immune biomarkers associated with the tumor immune microenvironment (TIME) is critical for advancing precision oncology, yet existing approaches are largely limited to single image modalities and suffer from insufficient resolution and incomplete utilization of complementary clinical and biological information. Here we introduce MixTIME, a multimodal foundation model that leverages a mixture-of-experts (MoE) architecture to integrate pathology foundation models trained across distinct modalities: image only (UNIv2), image text (CONCHv1.5), and image transcriptomic (STPath) representations for pixel-level and slide-level prediction of multiplex immunofluorescence (mIF) protein expression from hematoxylin and eosin (HE) whole-slide images. MixTIME employs a learnable router to dynamically weight expert contributions and is trained with a distribution- and tendency-aware loss function. Benchmarked on two datasets of different scales, MixTIME achieves state-of-the-art performance across 17 protein markers as measured by correlation metrics. The predicted mIF profiles substantially enhance downstream tasks, including spatial domain identification, survival prediction, and AI-assisted pathology report generation validated by expert pathologists from multiple institutes across the world. Furthermore, MixTIME enables longitudinal tracking of protein expression dynamics across clinical time points and reveals protein gene interaction patterns linked to drug resistance and immune suppression in tumor microenvironments. Collectively, MixTIME provides a scalable framework for multimodal biomarker discovery and clinical translation in computational pathology.

标题：基于多模态混合专家病理基础模型预测免疫生物标志物，赋能精准肿瘤学
预测与肿瘤免疫微环境（TIME）相关的免疫生物标志物，对推进精准肿瘤学至关重要，但现有方法大多局限于单一图像模态，存在分辨率不足和未能充分整合互补临床与生物信息的问题。本文提出MixTIME，一种多模态基础模型，采用混合专家（MoE）架构，整合在不同模态下训练的病理基础模型：仅图像（UNIv2）、图像文本（CONCHv1.5）和图像转录组（STPath）表示，用于从苏木精-伊红（HE）全切片图像中在像素级和切片级预测多重免疫荧光（mIF）蛋白表达。MixTIME采用可学习路由器动态加权专家贡献，并使用分布与趋势感知的损失函数进行训练。在两个不同规模的数据集上评估，MixTIME在17个蛋白标志物上均达到当前最优性能（以相关性指标衡量）。预测的mIF谱显著提升下游任务表现，包括空间域识别、生存预测及AI辅助病理报告生成，经全球多家机构专家病理学家验证。此外，MixTIME支持跨临床时间点的蛋白表达动态纵向追踪，并揭示与肿瘤微环境中药物耐药性和免疫抑制相关的蛋白基因交互模式。总体而言，MixTIME为计算病理学中的多模态生物标志物发现与临床转化提供了一种可扩展框架。
