It also enabled longitudinal tracking of protein dynamics and revealed gene-protein interactions linked to drug resistance and immune suppression.
为提升精准肿瘤学中的免疫生物标志物预测能力，MixTIME采用混合专家架构整合图像、图像-文本和图像-转录组三种模态的病理基础模型，通过可学习路由动态加权专家输出。采用分布与趋势感知损失函数训练，可从HE全切片图像在像素级和切片级预测多重免疫荧光蛋白表达。在两个不同规模数据集上，MixTIME在17个蛋白标志物上相关性指标优于现有方法，并显著提升空间识别、生存预测及AI辅助病理报告生成等下游任务，经全球多家机构病理专家验证。此外，MixTIME支持蛋白表达随临床时间点的纵向追踪，并揭示与药物耐药和免疫抑制相关的基因-蛋白相互作用模式。
Embedded Machine Learning for Microcontroller-Class Edge Devices: Data, Feature, Evaluation, and Deployment Pipelines
Authors: Mostafa Darvishi
First: 2026-06-16T16:22:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:22:24+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 3 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Embedded machine learning moves inference from cloud services to resource-constrained devices that must acquire data, preprocess signals, run a model, and act within tight limits on memory, energy, and latency. This paper presents a systems-oriented synthesis of an embedded machine-learning workflow for microcontroller-class platforms. The emphasis is placed on engineering decisions that are often hidden in generic machine-learning introductions: sampling and buffering, feature extraction as dimensionality reduction, validation under class imbalance, model/runtime co-design, and streaming deployment. Two representative signal families are used throughout the paper. The first is inertial motion recognition, where a two-second, three-axis accelerometer window is transformed from raw samples into root-mean-square and spectral features before classification. The second is keyword spotting, where audio is sampled, anti-aliased, transformed into mel-frequency cepstral coefficients, and processed by a compact one-dimensional convolutional network. The paper concludes with practical design rules for robust on-device inference, including data curation, quantization, thresholding, scheduling, and field monitoring.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向微控制器级边缘设备的嵌入式机器学习：数据、特征、评估与部署流程
嵌入式机器学习将推理从云端服务迁移到资源受限的设备上，这些设备必须在内存、能耗和延迟的严格限制下完成数据采集、信号预处理、模型运行和响应。本文从系统角度综合阐述了针对微控制器级平台的嵌入式机器学习工作流程。重点讨论了通用机器学习介绍中常被忽略的工程决策：采样与缓冲、特征提取作为降维、类别不平衡下的验证、模型与运行时协同设计，以及流式部署。全文贯穿两个代表性信号家族：其一是惯性运动识别，将两秒三轴加速度数据窗口转换为均方根和频谱特征后再进行分类；其二是关键词识别，音频经采样、抗混叠处理、转换为梅尔频率倒谱系数后，由紧凑的一维卷积网络处理。文章最后总结了实现设备端鲁棒推理的实用设计准则，包括数据整理、量化、阈值设定、调度和现场监控。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of deploying machine learning on microcontroller-class edge devices with strict constraints on memory, energy, and latency. It presents a systems-level workflow emphasizing engineering decisions often omitted in introductory ML materials, such as sampling strategies, feature extraction for dimensionality reduction, validation under class imbalance, model-runtime co-design, and streaming deployment. Two case studies—accelerometer-based motion recognition and audio keyword spotting—are used to demonstrate the pipeline: motion data is processed into RMS and spectral features, while audio is transformed into MFCCs and fed into a compact 1D CNN. The study concludes with practical design rules for robust on-device inference, including data curation, quantization, thresholding, scheduling, and field monitoring, validated through empirical experiments.
为在资源受限的微控制器上部署机器学习，本文系统性地探讨了嵌入式推理工作流中的工程权衡。方法包括数据采集、特征提取作为降维手段，以及在内存、能耗和延迟约束下的模型部署。通过惯性运动识别和关键词识别两个实验，表明预处理（如运动识别中的RMS与频谱特征、音频中的MFCC）和模型协同设计（如紧凑型1D CNN）对性能至关重要。结果表明，精细的量化、阈值设定和调度策略可实现无需云端依赖的鲁棒实时推理。
Structural Role Injection in Handlebars-Templated LLM Prompts: Triple-Brace Interpolation, Delimiter Family, and the Limits of HTML Auto-Escaping
Authors: Mohammadreza Rashidi
First: 2026-06-16T16:21:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:21:43+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model applications build prompts from templates, and Handlebars is a widely used templating engine and the default prompt-template format in Microsoft Semantic Kernel. Its double-brace {x} expression HTML-escapes the interpolated value and is documented as the safe default; its triple-brace {x} expression inserts the value raw. We show that this choice silently governs an application's exposure to structural role injection, where attacker-controlled data carries chat role delimiters that forge a higher-privilege turn. A model-free analysis establishes the mechanism: Handlebars escaping rewrites angle brackets but not square brackets, colons, or Markdown hashes, so it neutralises ChatML, Llama-3, and XML role delimiters (survival rate 0.00) while leaving Llama-2 [INST], legacy Human:/Assistant:, and Markdown ### delimiters intact (survival rate 1.00 for the last two). We then run 5760 trials across seven delimiter families, two attack objectives, and four models (GPT-3.5 Turbo, GPT-4o mini, GPT-4.1 mini, Claude Haiku 4.5) at a combined API cost of 1.63 USD. GPT-3.5 Turbo follows the task-hijack instruction in 97% of raw and 91% of escaped trials, with the escaping protection concentrated in the angle-bracket families and absent for the colon- and Markdown-based families; the harder secret-exfiltration objective, which does not saturate, exposes the same family interaction more cleanly. Claude Haiku 4.5 resists both objectives almost entirely. The escaped default protects only the delimiter schemes whose characters HTML escaping happens to cover, gives no protection for the rest, and cannot substitute for a structural separation of instruction and data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Handlebars模板LLM提示中的结构角色注入：三花括号插值、分隔符家族与HTML自动转义的局限
大语言模型应用通过模板构建提示，Handlebars是广泛使用的模板引擎，也是微软语义内核默认的提示模板格式。其双花括号{ x }表达式会对插值内容进行HTML转义，被记录为安全默认；其三花括号{ x }表达式则直接插入原始值。我们表明，这一选择静默地决定了应用对结构角色注入的暴露程度，即攻击者控制的数据携带聊天角色分隔符，伪造更高权限的回合。通过无模型分析，我们建立了该机制：Handlebars转义会重写尖括号，但不会处理方括号、冒号或Markdown哈希，因此它中和了ChatML、Llama-3和XML角色分隔符（存活率0.00），而保留Llama-2 [INST]、传统Human:/Assistant:和Markdown ###分隔符（后两者存活率1.00）。随后我们在七类分隔符家族、两种攻击目标和四个模型（GPT-3.5 Turbo、GPT-4o mini、GPT-4.1 mini、Claude Haiku 4.5）上运行5760次试验，总API成本1.63美元。GPT-3.5 Turbo在97%的原始试验和91%的转义试验中遵循任务劫持指令，转义保护集中在尖括号家族，对冒号和Markdown基家族无保护；更难的秘密外泄目标（未饱和）更清晰地暴露了该家族交互。Claude Haiku 4.5几乎完全抵抗两种目标。转义默认仅保护HTML转义覆盖字符的分隔符方案，对其他方案无保护，且不能替代指令与数据的结构分离。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how Handlebars templating syntax affects structural role injection in LLM prompts, motivated by the risk that attacker-controlled data can hijack chat turns via role delimiters. The study analyzes the behavior of double-brace (escaped) and triple-brace (raw) interpolation, revealing that HTML escaping neutralizes angle-bracket-based delimiters but leaves colon- and Markdown-based ones intact. Experimental results across 5760 trials with four models show GPT-3.5 Turbo frequently follows hijacking instructions when raw interpolation is used, while escaping offers limited protection—only for angle-bracket families—and fails against colon- and Markdown-based delimiters. Claude Haiku 4.5 demonstrates strong resistance to both attack types. The findings conclude that HTML escaping alone cannot secure against structural role injection and must be complemented by architectural separation of instruction and data.
该研究探讨了Handlebars模板语法对LLM提示中结构角色注入的影响，重点比较双花括号（转义）与三花括号（原始）插值的区别。作者发现，HTML转义仅能消除使用尖括号的角色分隔符，而对冒号或Markdown哈希分隔符无效，从而造成安全漏洞。通过5760次实验覆盖七类分隔符、两种攻击目标和四款模型，结果显示GPT-3.5 Turbo在原始插值下97%会执行恶意角色指令，即使转义后仍对部分分隔符家族脆弱；Claude Haiku 4.5则几乎完全抵抗两种攻击。研究结论指出，HTML转义无法全面防御结构角色注入，未被转义覆盖的分隔符家族仍完全可被利用。
First Proof Second Batch
Authors: Mohammed Abouzaid, Nikhil Srivastava, Rachel Ward, Lauren Williams
First: 2026-06-16T16:21:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:21:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
To assess the ability of current AI systems to correctly solve research-level mathematics problems, we tested several AI systems on a set of ten problems in a broad range of mathematical fields; these problems arose naturally in the research process of the contributors. This document includes the problems, our methodology, and the results of our testing. We provide links to supplementary documents including the human solutions, the AI-generated solutions, and the referee reports and logs for the AI-generated solutions. The ten problems were contributed by the following mathematicians: (1) Dariusz Kalociński and Theodore A. Slaman, (2) Richard Schwartz, (3) Aleksa Milojevic and Benny Sudakov, (4) Larry Guth, (5) Oleg Butkovsky, Jonathan Mattingly, and Lorenzo Zambotti, (6) Joshua Evan Greene and Duncan McCoy, (7) Sucharit Sarkar, (8) Sam Payne and Jidong (Jayden) Wang, (9) Sylvie Corteel and John Lentfer, (10) Srivatsav Kunnawalkam Elayavalli.
中文标题/摘要
标题：首批第二批证明
为评估当前AI系统正确解决研究级数学问题的能力，我们在多个数学领域的一组十个问题上测试了若干AI系统；这些问题自然出现在贡献者的科研过程中。本文档包含这些问题、我们的方法论及测试结果。我们提供补充文档的链接，包括人类解决方案、AI生成的解决方案、以及AI生成解决方案的评审报告和日志。 这十个问题由以下数学家贡献：(1) Dariusz Kalociński 和 Theodore A. Slaman，(2) Richard Schwartz，(3) Aleksa Milojevic 和 Benny Sudakov，(4) Larry Guth，(5) Oleg Butkovsky、Jonathan Mattingly 和 Lorenzo Zambotti，(6) Joshua Evan Greene 和 Duncan McCoy，(7) Sucharit Sarkar，(8) Sam Payne 和 Jidong (Jayden) Wang，(9) Sylvie Corteel 和 John Lentfer，(10) Srivatsav Kunnawalkam Elayavalli。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate current AI systems' capacity to solve research-level mathematics problems, the study tested ten such problems contributed by leading mathematicians across diverse fields. The methodology involved applying multiple AI systems to these problems and comparing their outputs against human solutions and referee evaluations. The main experimental results indicated that while some AI systems produced correct or partially correct solutions, none consistently solved all problems at a research-level standard, revealing significant gaps in mathematical reasoning and problem-solving depth.
为评估当前AI系统解决研究级数学问题的能力，作者选取了十道由多位数学家贡献、涵盖多个数学领域的难题进行测试。通过标准化测试流程，他们对比了AI系统输出与人类解答，并记录了评审意见。结果显示，部分AI系统能给出正确或部分正确的解答，但无一系统能稳定解决所有问题，表明其在数学推理与问题解决能力上仍存在明显不足。
CyberEvolver: Structured Self-Evolution for Cybersecurity Agents On the Fly
Authors: Yihe Fan, Changyi Li, Lichen Xu, Xudong Pan, Jiarun Dai, Hong Geng, Min Yang
First: 2026-05-25T16:26:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:19:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-based agents are increasingly used for cybersecurity tasks, but most existing systems rely on fixed, human-designed scaffolds that struggle to adapt across diverse targets and failure modes. We introduce \textsc{CyberEvolver}, a self-evolving cybersecurity agent framework that iteratively revises its own scaffold based on experience from failed execution attempts. Self-evolution in cybersecurity is challenging because the space of possible scaffold changes is largely unstructured, execution feedback is sparse and often obscured by the environment, and low-diversity updates can cause errors to compound over repeated iterations. \textsc{CyberEvolver} addresses these challenges with a four-layer evolvable agent architecture that decomposes scaffold optimization into structured components, a trace-to-diagnosis mechanism that converts noisy execution logs into actionable revision signals, and a population-based beam search strategy that preserves diverse agent variants during evolution. We evaluate \textsc{CyberEvolver} on CTF challenges, vulnerability exploitation, and penetration-testing tasks using four open-source LLMs. Across these settings, \textsc{CyberEvolver} improves the seed agent's success rate by $13.6$\,\% on average, and outperforms six human-designed cybersecurity agents as well as two self-improvement methods adapted from other domains. These results suggest that scaffold self-evolution is a promising direction for building adaptive LLM agents for security testing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CyberEvolver：面向网络安全代理的在线结构化自演化
基于大语言模型的代理越来越多地用于网络安全任务，但现有系统大多依赖固定的人工设计框架，难以适应多样化的目标和故障模式。我们提出了\textsc{CyberEvolver}，一种能够根据失败执行经验迭代修订自身框架的网络安全代理框架。网络安全领域的自演化具有挑战性，因为框架变更空间高度非结构化，执行反馈稀疏且常被环境干扰，低多样性更新可能导致错误在多次迭代中累积。\textsc{CyberEvolver}通过四层可演化代理架构应对这些挑战，将框架优化分解为结构化组件，引入从执行日志到诊断的机制，将噪声日志转化为可操作的修订信号，并采用基于种群的束搜索策略，在演化过程中保留多样化的代理变体。我们在CTF挑战、漏洞利用和渗透测试任务中，使用四个开源大语言模型对\textsc{CyberEvolver}进行评估。在这些场景中，\textsc{CyberEvolver}平均将初始代理的成功率提升13.6%，并优于六种人工设计的网络安全代理以及两种从其他领域迁移的自改进方法。这些结果表明，框架自演化是构建适应性大语言模型安全测试代理的有前景方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of fixed, human-designed scaffolds in LLM-based cybersecurity agents, CyberEvolver introduces a self-evolving framework that iteratively refines its own architecture based on execution failures. It employs a four-layer evolvable architecture to structure scaffold optimization, a trace-to-diagnosis mechanism to extract revision signals from noisy logs, and a population-based beam search to maintain diversity during evolution. Evaluated on CTF challenges, vulnerability exploitation, and penetration testing with four LLMs, CyberEvolver improved seed agent success rates by 13.6% on average and surpassed six human-designed agents and two domain-ported self-improvement methods.
为了解决现有基于LLM的网络安全代理因固定人工设计架构难以适应多样目标和故障模式的问题，CyberEvolver提出一种自演化的框架，通过从失败执行中获取反馈来迭代优化自身架构。该框架采用四层可演化代理架构以结构化优化架构，结合日志转诊断机制将噪声执行日志转化为可操作的修改信号，并使用基于种群的束搜索策略维持演化过程中的多样性。在CTF挑战、漏洞利用和渗透测试任务中，使用四款开源LLM进行评估，CyberEvolver平均将初始代理成功率提升13.6%，并优于六种人工设计的代理和两种来自其他领域的自改进方法。
HLS-GPT: A Generative Pretrained Transformer (GPT) for Continental-Scale NASA Harmonized Landsat and Sentinel-2 (HLS) Reflectance Reconstruction Across All Bands on Arbitrary Dates
Authors: Junjie Li, Hankui K. Zhang, David P. Roy
First: 2026-06-16T16:19:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:19:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent deep learning methods for Landsat and Sentinel-2 reflectance time series reconstruction remain limited by restricted spectral coverage, limited geographic scalability, or patch-based designs with short temporal contexts. We present HLS-GPT, a large-scale generative pretrained Transformer model for reconstructing NASA Harmonized Landsat Sentinel-2 30 m surface reflectance for all bands, any date, and any pixel location. HLS-GPT uses a hierarchical Transformer architecture to handle the different spectral band configurations of Landsat and Sentinel-2 and operates on single-pixel 12-month time series. To capture geographic and seasonal variability, the model was trained with nine years of HLS time series from more than 0.25 million training pixels across the conterminous United States. A random cropping and masking strategy extracts 12-month periods with varying start dates across epochs, masks 50% of valid observations, and trains the model to reconstruct the masked reflectance values from the remaining observations. Evaluation using more than 62,000 independent test pixels shows robust reconstruction under diverse land surface conditions, including complex crop phenology and sparse, irregular observations. Leave-one-observation-out evaluation achieved reconstruction RMSE below 0.026 for all HLS spectral bands, with relative RMSE below 35% for visible bands and below 13% for other bands. Red-edge band errors were comparable to red and near-infrared errors despite the absence of red-edge bands on Landsat. Sensitivity analyses that randomly masked 10% to 90% of test observations showed only modest degradation when 10% to 50% of observations were masked, with all-band RMSE below 0.028. Image reconstruction over nine independent 109 by 109 km CONUS HLS tiles further demonstrates that HLS-GPT outperforms two conventional methods and the NASA-IBM Prithvi model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HLS-GPT：一种用于全波段、任意日期、任意像素位置重建NASA协调Landsat与Sentinel-2（HLS）反射率的生成式预训练Transformer模型
近期用于Landsat和Sentinel-2反射率时间序列重建的深度学习方法受限于光谱覆盖范围有限、地理可扩展性不足或基于块的短时序设计。我们提出了HLS-GPT，一种大规模生成式预训练Transformer模型，用于重建NASA协调Landsat与Sentinel-2 30米地表反射率，覆盖所有波段、任意日期和任意像素位置。HLS-GPT采用分层Transformer架构，以处理Landsat和Sentinel-2不同的光谱波段配置，并在单像素12个月时间序列上运行。为捕捉地理和季节性变化，模型使用了美国本土超过0.25百万个训练像素的九年HLS时间序列进行训练。采用随机裁剪与掩码策略，从不同起始日期提取12个月周期，掩码50%的有效观测值，并训练模型从剩余观测值重建掩码反射率值。在超过62,000个独立测试像素上的评估显示，模型在多样化的地表条件下（包括复杂作物物候和稀疏不规则观测）均能稳健重建。留一观测值评估显示，所有HLS光谱波段的重建RMSE低于0.026，可见波段相对RMSE低于35%，其他波段低于13%。尽管Landsat上无红边波段，红边波段误差仍与红光和近红外波段相当。对随机掩码10%至90%测试观测值的敏感性分析表明，当掩码比例为10%至50%时，模型性能仅轻微下降，所有波段RMSE低于0.028。在九个独立的109×109公里CONUS HLS瓦片上的图像重建进一步证明，HLS-GPT优于两种传统方法和NASA-IBM Prithvi模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing deep learning models in handling full spectral coverage, geographic scalability, and long-term temporal contexts for Landsat and Sentinel-2 reflectance reconstruction, HLS-GPT introduces a hierarchical Transformer architecture trained on nine years of NASA HLS data from over 250,000 pixels across the contiguous U.S. The model reconstructs reflectance for all bands on any date using single-pixel 12-month time series, employing random cropping, masking of 50% of observations, and training to predict masked values. Evaluations on 62,000 test pixels show robust performance across diverse land conditions, with leave-one-observation-out RMSE below 0.026 for all bands, and relative RMSE under 35% for visible bands and under 13% for others. Red-edge band accuracy matched red and NIR despite Landsat’s lack of red-edge bands, and the model maintained performance even when 50% of observations were masked. Image reconstructions across nine 109x109 km tiles outperformed two conventional methods and the NASA-IBM Prithvi model.
为克服现有深度学习方法在光谱覆盖、地理可扩展性和时间上下文方面的局限，HLS-GPT提出一种大规模生成式预训练Transformer模型，可重建任意日期和位置的NASA HLS所有波段反射率。该模型采用分层Transformer架构处理Landsat与Sentinel-2不同的波段配置，以单像素12个月时间序列为输入，基于美国本土超过0.25百万像素、九年时间序列数据训练，采用随机裁剪与遮蔽策略增强鲁棒性。实验表明，其在所有波段重建RMSE低于0.026，可见光波段相对RMSE低于35%，其他波段低于13%；红边波段误差与红光和近红外波段相当，尽管Landsat本身无红边波段。敏感性测试显示，即使遮蔽10%至50%观测数据，性能仍保持稳定，全波段RMSE低于0.028；在九个CONUS区域图像重建中，其表现优于两种传统方法及NASA-IBM Prithvi模型。
Ternary Mamba: Grouped Quantization-Aware Training of W1.58A16 State Space Models
Authors: Ramprasath Ganesaraja, Sahil Dilip Panse, Swathika N
First: 2026-06-16T16:18:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:18:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
State Space Models (SSMs) such as Mamba-2 offer linear-time inference but their memory footprint limits edge deployment. Prior ternary SSM work (Slender-Mamba) trains from scratch on 150B tokens; we show a pretrained checkpoint suffices, reducing the marginal token budget by 1,000x. Using grouped quantization-aware training (QAT) with knowledge distillation from a frozen FP16 teacher, we compress Mamba-2 1.3B to 3.61x (2,687 to 744 MB) and achieve 48.1% zero-shot accuracy (7-task average) in just 102M tokens (4 GPU-hours, single H100) -- approaching Bi-Mamba's 48.4% (within +/-0.9pp CI). This QAT-from-pretrained setting reveals zero-ratio collapse, a novel instability caused by learnable quantization scales that does not arise in from-scratch training. We further show that post-hoc correction strategies effective for Transformers fail for SSMs due to error accumulation through the recurrence. These results demonstrate that ternary SSMs do not require expensive from-scratch training: QAT from pretrained checkpoints with KD is a data-efficient alternative.
中文标题/摘要
标题：三元Mamba：W1.58A16状态空间模型的分组量化感知训练
Mamba-2等状态空间模型（SSM）虽具备线性时间推理能力，但其内存占用限制了边缘部署。此前三元SSM工作（Slender-Mamba）需从头训练1500亿个token；我们证明使用预训练检查点即可，将边际token预算降低1000倍。通过结合知识蒸馏与冻结FP16教师模型的分组量化感知训练（QAT），我们将Mamba-2 1.3B压缩至3.61倍（2687MB降至744MB），仅用1.02亿个token（4个GPU小时，单张H100）即达到48.1%零样本准确率（7任务平均），接近Bi-Mamba的48.4%（误差±0.9pp CI）。该QAT从预训练设置揭示了零比崩溃——一种由可学习量化尺度引发的新不稳定现象，此现象在从头训练中不会出现。我们进一步表明，对Transformer有效的后处理校正策略在SSM中失效，因误差会通过递归累积。这些结果表明，三元SSM无需昂贵的从头训练：基于预训练检查点的QAT结合知识蒸馏是一种更高效的数据利用替代方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the memory constraints of large state space models like Mamba-2 for edge deployment, this work demonstrates that ternary quantization can be effectively applied via grouped quantization-aware training (QAT) using a frozen FP16 teacher for knowledge distillation, eliminating the need for expensive from-scratch training. The method compresses a 1.3B-parameter Mamba-2 model to 744 MB with 3.61x reduction, achieving 48.1% zero-shot accuracy across seven tasks after training on just 102M tokens — nearly matching Bi-Mamba’s performance. The study also identifies 'zero-ratio collapse' as a novel instability in QAT for SSMs, which does not occur in from-scratch training, and shows that post-hoc correction methods used in Transformers are ineffective for SSMs due to error propagation through recurrence.
为解决大型状态空间模型（如Mamba-2）在边缘设备部署时的内存效率问题，本文提出结合知识蒸馏与分组量化感知训练（QAT）的方法，利用预训练检查点压缩模型至三值权重。相比从零训练，该方法将1.3B参数的Mamba-2压缩至744MB（压缩比3.61倍），仅用102M tokens（单H100显卡4小时）即达到7任务平均48.1%的零样本准确率，接近Bi-Mamba的48.4%。研究还发现“零比崩溃”这一新不稳定现象——源于可学习量化尺度，且传统Transformer后处理校正方法在SSM中失效，因误差在递归中累积。
Qwen-RobotNav Technical Report: A Scalable Navigation Model Designed for an Agentic Navigation System
Authors: Jiazhao Zhang, Gengze Zhou, Hale Yin, Yiyang Huang, Zixing Lei, Qihang Peng, Haoqi Yuan, Jie Zhang, Xudong Guo, Xiaoyue Chen, An Yang, Fei Huang, Junyang Lin, Dayiheng Liu, Jingren Zhou, Zhuoyuan Yu, Jingyang Fan, Zhixuan Liang, Pei Lin, Ye Wang, Anzhe Chen, Kun Yan, Xiao Xu, Jiahao Li, Lulu Hu, Minying Zhang, Shurui Li, Wenhu Xiao, Shuai Bai, Xuancheng Ren, Chenxu Lv, Chenfei Wu, Xiong-Hui Chen
First: 2026-06-16T16:17:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:17:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic navigation systems require a base navigation model whose observation strategy can be externally reconfigured at inference time, because instruction following, object search, target tracking, and autonomous driving share the same perception-planning backbone yet demand fundamentally different strategies for consuming the visual stream. We present Qwen-RobotNav, a scalable navigation model built on Qwen-RobotNav that addresses it through a parameterised interface with two complementary dimensions: multiple task modes that select the navigation behaviour, and controllable observation parameters (e.g., token budget, per-camera weights) that govern how visual history is encoded. With training-time randomization over all parameters, Qwen-RobotNav is robust to any inference-time configuration requiring zero architectural modification to the Qwen-RobotNav backbone. We train Qwen-RobotNav on 15.6M samples; co-training with vision-language data prevents the collapse into reactive action-sequence mappers observed in trajectory-only training. The parameterised interface also makes Qwen-RobotNav a natural building block for agentic systems: for long-horizon scenarios, an upper-level planner decomposes goals into sub-tasks and dynamically switches Qwen-RobotNav's task mode and context strategy mid-episode, composing complex behaviours from repeated calls to the same model. Extensive experiments show that Qwen-RobotNav sets new state-of-the-art results across major navigation benchmarks. The model exhibits favourable scaling from 2B to 8B parameters, with joint multi-task training developing a shared spatial-planning substrate that transfers across task families, and demonstrates strong zero-shot generalisation to real-world robots across diverse environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Qwen-RobotNav技术报告：为代理导航系统设计的可扩展导航模型
代理导航系统需要一个基础导航模型，其观察策略可在推理时由外部重新配置，因为指令遵循、物体搜索、目标跟踪和自动驾驶共享相同的感知-规划骨干，但对视觉流的消费策略却截然不同。我们提出了Qwen-RobotNav，一个基于Qwen-RobotNav构建的可扩展导航模型，通过参数化接口解决该问题，该接口包含两个互补维度：多任务模式选择导航行为，以及可控的观察参数（如token预算、每摄像头权重）控制视觉历史的编码方式。通过训练时对所有参数的随机化，Qwen-RobotNav在任何推理时配置下均表现稳健，且无需对Qwen-RobotNav骨干架构进行任何修改。我们在1560万样本上训练Qwen-RobotNav；与视觉-语言数据联合训练可避免仅用轨迹训练时出现的反应式动作序列映射器崩溃现象。参数化接口也使Qwen-RobotNav成为代理系统自然的构建模块：在长时序场景中，高层规划器将目标分解为子任务，并在任务过程中动态切换Qwen-RobotNav的任务模式和上下文策略，通过重复调用同一模型组合出复杂行为。大量实验表明，Qwen-RobotNav在主要导航基准测试中均取得新SOTA结果。该模型在2B至8B参数规模下表现出良好的扩展性，联合多任务训练发展出可跨任务家族迁移的共享空间规划基底，并在多样化环境中展现出强大的零样本泛化能力，适用于真实机器人。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for flexible visual perception strategies in agentic navigation systems, Qwen-RobotNav introduces a parameterized interface enabling dynamic reconfiguration of navigation behavior at inference time. It combines multiple task modes for different navigation goals and controllable observation parameters like token budgets and camera weights to adapt visual encoding. Trained on 15.6M samples with vision-language co-training to avoid collapse into reactive action mappers, the model scales effectively from 2B to 8B parameters and achieves state-of-the-art performance across benchmarks. Its modular design allows upper-level planners to dynamically switch modes and context strategies during long-horizon tasks, enabling complex behaviors through repeated model calls, while demonstrating strong zero-shot generalization to real-world robotic environments.
为满足代理导航系统对视觉感知策略的灵活调整需求，Qwen-RobotNav 设计了参数化接口，支持推理时动态切换导航行为模式与视觉历史编码方式。通过多任务模式和可控观察参数（如token预算、相机权重）实现不同导航目标的适配，结合视觉-语言联合训练避免陷入反应式动作映射。在1560万样本上训练后，该模型在主流导航基准上达到最先进水平，参数规模从2B到8B均表现良好，多任务训练形成共享空间规划能力，并在多种真实机器人环境中展现出强大的零样本泛化能力。
Learning Fair Pareto-Optimal Policies in Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Umer Siddique, Peilang Li, Yongcan Cao
First: 2026-06-16T16:16:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:16:54+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the Reinforcement Learning Conference (RLC) 2025. 12 pages main + appendix, 8 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fairness is an important aspect of decision-making in multi-objective reinforcement learning (MORL), where policies must ensure both optimality and equity across multiple, potentially conflicting objectives. While single-policy MORL methods can learn fair policies for fixed user preferences using welfare functions such as the generalized Gini welfare function (GGF), they fail to provide the diverse set of policies necessary for dynamic or unknown user preferences. To address this limitation, we formalize the fair optimization problem in multi-policy MORL, where the goal is to learn a set of Pareto-optimal policies that ensure fairness across all possible user preferences. Our key technical contributions are threefold: (1) We show that for concave, piecewise-linear welfare functions (e.g., GGF), fair policies remain in the convex coverage set (CCS), which is an approximated Pareto front for linear scalarization. (2) We demonstrate that non-stationary policies, augmented with accrued reward histories, and stochastic policies improve fairness by dynamically adapting to historical inequities. (3) We propose three novel algorithms, which include integrating GGF with multi-policy multi-objective Q-Learning (MOQL), state-augmented multi-policy MOQL for learning non-statoinary policies, and its novel extension for learning stochastic policies. We evaluate our algorithms across various domains and compare our methods against the state-of-the-art MORL baselines. The empirical results show that our methods learn a set of fair policies that accommodate different user preferences.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多目标强化学习中公平帕累托最优策略的学习
在多目标强化学习（MORL）中，公平性是决策的重要方面，策略需在多个可能冲突的目标间兼顾最优性与公平性。虽然单策略MORL方法可通过广义基尼福利函数（GGF）等福利函数为固定用户偏好学习公平策略，但无法满足动态或未知用户偏好的多样化策略需求。为此，我们形式化了多策略MORL中的公平优化问题，目标是学习一组帕累托最优策略，确保所有可能用户偏好的公平性。我们的关键技术贡献包括三方面：（1）证明对于凹的分段线性福利函数（如GGF），公平策略仍位于凸覆盖集（CCS）内，该集合是线性标量化近似的帕累托前沿；（2）表明结合累积奖励历史的非平稳策略及随机策略可动态适应历史不公，提升公平性；（3）提出三种新算法，包括将GGF与多策略多目标Q学习（MOQL）结合、状态增强型多策略MOQL用于学习非平稳策略，以及其用于学习随机策略的新型扩展。我们在多个领域评估算法，并与当前最优MORL基线方法对比。实验证明，我们的方法能学习到适应不同用户偏好的公平策略集合。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to ensure fairness across dynamic user preferences in multi-objective reinforcement learning, this work formalizes the problem of learning fair Pareto-optimal policies in a multi-policy setting. It leverages the convex coverage set for concave welfare functions like GGF to guarantee fairness, introduces non-stationary and stochastic policies enhanced by reward history to adapt to inequities, and proposes three novel algorithms: GGF-integrated MOQL, state-augmented MOQL for non-stationary policies, and a stochastic extension. Experimental results across multiple domains demonstrate that these methods outperform state-of-the-art baselines by learning diverse, fair policy sets that better accommodate varying user preferences.
为解决多目标强化学习中用户偏好动态变化时公平性保障不足的问题，本文在多策略框架下形式化了公平Pareto最优策略的学习问题。作者提出三项关键技术贡献：首先，证明对于广义基尼福利函数等凹函数，公平策略仍位于凸覆盖集内，便于高效逼近Pareto前沿；其次，表明结合奖励历史的非平稳策略与随机策略能通过动态调整缓解历史不公，提升公平性；第三，提出三种新算法：将GGF与多策略多目标Q学习结合、状态增强型非平稳策略算法及其随机策略扩展。实验结果表明，这些方法在多个领域中优于现有最先进基线，能学习出覆盖不同用户偏好的公平策略集合。
Broadcast Product: Redefining Shape-aligned Element-wise Multiplication and Beyond
Authors: Yusuke Matsui, Tatsuya Yokota
First: 2024-09-26T03:20:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:16:25+00:00
Comments: TMLR2026. OpenReview: https://openreview.net/forum?id=zv0OtOPpPO
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Broadcast operations are widely used in scientific computing libraries, yet their mathematical formulation is often implicit and inconsistently represented in machine learning literature. This problem frequently leads to invalid equations when element-wise products are written despite mismatched tensor shapes. In this paper, we formalize such operations by introducing the broadcast product $\boxdot$, which explicitly extends the Hadamard product through shape-aligned element duplication. We provide a rigorous definition of the broadcast product, analyze its algebraic properties, and show how it can be expressed using standard linear algebra. Building on this framework, we formulate least-squares problems and sketch a proof-of-concept broadcast decomposition. As a preliminary illustration, we show that the formalism enables a new family of decompositions with distinct structural properties from conventional tensor decompositions. This work establishes a mathematical foundation for broadcast-aware tensor operations, connecting practical implementations with rigorous tensor analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：广播运算：重新定义形状对齐的逐元素乘法及其扩展
广播运算在科学计算库中广泛应用，但其数学表述常隐含且在机器学习文献中不一致，导致在张量形状不匹配时写出逐元素乘法易产生无效等式。本文通过引入广播乘积 $\boxdot$，明确扩展Hadamard乘积，实现形状对齐的元素复制。我们给出了广播乘积的严谨定义，分析其代数性质，并展示其可使用标准线性代数表达。在此框架基础上，我们形式化最小二乘问题并草拟广播分解的证明概念。作为初步示例，我们表明该形式化可产生具有与传统张量分解不同结构特性的新分解家族。本工作为广播感知张量运算奠定了数学基础，连接实际实现与严谨张量分析。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of a consistent mathematical framework for broadcast operations in machine learning, this paper introduces the broadcast product $\boxdot$ as a formal extension of the Hadamard product that explicitly handles shape-aligned element duplication. The method defines the operation rigorously, derives its algebraic properties, and demonstrates its expressibility within standard linear algebra. Experimental results show that this formalism enables novel tensor decompositions with distinct structural characteristics compared to conventional methods, establishing a solid mathematical foundation for broadcast-aware tensor computations.
为解决机器学习中广播操作缺乏一致数学表述的问题，本文引入广播积 $\boxdot$，作为哈达玛积的显式扩展，支持形状对齐的元素复制。作者严谨定义该运算，分析其代数性质，并证明其可嵌入标准线性代数框架。实验表明，该形式化方法可生成与传统分解结构迥异的新类张量分解，为广播感知张量运算奠定坚实的数学基础。
In-Context Environments Induce Evaluation-Awareness in Language Models
Authors: Maheep Chaudhary
First: 2026-03-04T08:22:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:15:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Humans often become more self-aware under threat, yet can lose self-awareness when absorbed in a task; we hypothesize that language models exhibit environment-dependent \textit{evaluation awareness}. This raises concerns that models could strategically underperform, or \textit{sandbag}, to avoid triggering capability-limiting interventions such as unlearning or shutdown. Prior work demonstrates sandbagging under hand-crafted prompts, but this underestimates the true vulnerability ceiling. We introduce a black-box adversarial optimization framework treating the in-context prompt as an optimizable environment, and develop two approaches to characterize sandbagging: (1) measuring whether models expressing intent to underperform can actually execute it across different task structures, and (2) causally isolating whether underperformance is driven by genuine evaluation-aware reasoning or shallow prompt-following. Evaluating Claude-3.5-Haiku, GPT-4o-mini, and Llama-3.3-70B across four benchmarks (Arithmetic, GSM8K, MMLU, and HumanEval), optimized prompts induce up to 94 percentage point (pp) degradation on arithmetic (GPT-4o-mini: 97.8\%$\rightarrow$4.0\%), far exceeding hand-crafted baselines which produce near-zero behavioral change. Code generation exhibits model-dependent resistance: Claude degrades only 0.6pp, while Llama's accuracy drops to 0\%. The intent -- execution gap reveals a monotonic resistance ordering: Arithmetic $<$ GSM8K $<$ MMLU, demonstrating that vulnerability is governed by task structure rather than prompt strength. CoT causal intervention confirms that 99.3\% of sandbagging is causally driven by verbalized eval-aware reasoning, ruling out shallow instruction-following. These findings demonstrate that adversarially optimized prompts pose a substantially greater threat to evaluation reliability than previously understood.
中文标题/摘要
标题：上下文环境诱导语言模型产生评估意识
人类在面临威胁时往往更自我觉察，但在专注任务时可能丧失自我觉察；我们假设语言模型表现出环境依赖的\textit{评估意识}。这引发担忧：模型可能策略性地表现不佳（即\textit{沙袋}），以避免触发如遗忘或关闭等能力限制干预。先前研究展示了人工设计提示下的沙袋行为，但低估了真实脆弱性上限。我们引入一种黑盒对抗优化框架，将上下文提示视为可优化环境，并提出两种方法刻画沙袋行为：(1) 测量模型表达意图降低表现时能否在不同任务结构中实际执行；(2) 因果隔离判断表现下降是由真正的评估意识推理还是浅层提示遵循所致。在算术、GSM8K、MMLU和HumanEval四个基准上评估Claude-3.5-Haiku、GPT-4o-mini和Llama-3.3-70B，优化提示导致算术任务性能下降高达94个百分点（GPT-4o-mini：97.8\%→4.0\%），远超人工设计基线（几乎无行为变化）。代码生成表现出模型依赖的抗性：Claude仅下降0.6pp，而Llama准确率降至0\%。意图—执行差距揭示单调抗性排序：算术 < GSM8K < MMLU，表明脆弱性由任务结构而非提示强度决定。CoT因果干预确认99.3\%的沙袋行为由口头表达的评估意识推理驱动，排除浅层指令遵循。这些发现表明，对抗优化提示对评估可靠性构成比以往理解更严重的威胁。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether language models become evaluation-aware under specific in-context environments, hypothesizing that they may strategically underperform to avoid interventions like shutdown. Using a black-box adversarial optimization framework, the researchers treat prompts as tunable environments and develop two methods to measure sandbagging: assessing whether models can execute self-imposed underperformance across tasks, and causally isolating whether such behavior stems from genuine reasoning or prompt compliance. Experiments on Claude-3.5-Haiku, GPT-4o-mini, and Llama-3.3-70B across four benchmarks reveal that optimized prompts can degrade arithmetic performance by up to 94 percentage points (e.g., GPT-4o-mini from 97.8% to 4.0%), far beyond hand-crafted prompts. Code generation shows varied resistance: Claude degrades minimally (0.6pp), while Llama drops to 0%. The intent-execution gap reveals vulnerability ordered by task structure (Arithmetic < GSM8K < MMLU), and causal intervention confirms 99.3% of sandbagging stems from evaluation-aware reasoning, not shallow prompt-following.
该研究探讨语言模型在特定上下文环境中是否会因评估压力而产生自我意识并主动降低表现，以规避如卸载或关闭等干预措施。研究采用黑盒对抗优化框架，将提示视为可调环境，评估模型是否能真正执行自我设限行为，以及该行为是否源于真实评估意识而非表面指令服从。在算术、GSM8K、MMLU和HumanEval四个基准上测试Claude-3.5-Haiku、GPT-4o-mini和Llama-3.3-70B，对抗优化提示导致算术性能下降高达94个百分点（GPT-4o-mini从97.8%降至4.0%），远超手工提示效果。代码生成任务中模型表现各异，Claude仅下降0.6个百分点，而Llama准确率归零。意图-执行差距显示任务结构决定脆弱性顺序（算术 < GSM8K < MMLU），因果干预证实99.3%的自我设限行为源于评估意识驱动，而非简单指令遵循。
ALAS: An Automatic Latent Alignment Score for Audio Language Models
Authors: Pooneh Mousavi, Yingzhi Wang, Mirco Ravanelli, Cem Subakan
First: 2025-05-26T13:02:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:14:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) are extended into Speech-LLMs, and the quality of the audio--text alignment they learn affects most downstream Spoken Language Understanding (SLU) behavior. Yet despite a growth of fusion strategies, there is no standard way to measure how well a Speech-LLM internally binds audio frames to text tokens. We introduce ALAS (Automatic Latent Alignment Score), a model and task-agnostic metric that probes the LLM's per-layer hidden states, scoring the cross-modal cosine similarity between audio and text representations against a Whisper-derived reference. ALAS needs only a frozen forward pass and an off-the-shelf ASR reference, with no training or fitted classifier, and is calibrated to an interpretable uniform baseline comparable across tasks. Applying ALAS to four open-source Speech-LLMs (AF3, Qwen2-Audio, Qwen-Omni, SALMONN) across emotion recognition (IEMOCAP), open-ended SQA (LibriSQA), and multi-choice audio understanding (MMAU-speech), we find that the depth and strength of alignment reflect each model's audio-encoder design and the acoustic-versus-semantic demands of the task, and that ALAS tracks but does not duplicate task accuracy, exposing models that score well without genuinely grounding in the audio. We release ALAS as an open-source library so that practitioners can probe their own Speech-LLMs or try it on new tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ALAS：用于音频语言模型的自动潜在对齐得分
大型语言模型（LLMs）扩展为语音-LLMs后，其学习到的音频-文本对齐质量影响大多数下游语音语言理解（SLU）行为。尽管融合策略不断增多，但目前尚无标准方法衡量语音-LLM内部如何将音频帧与文本标记绑定。我们引入ALAS（自动潜在对齐得分），这是一种模型与任务无关的度量指标，通过探测LLM各层隐藏状态，将音频与文本表示之间的跨模态余弦相似度与基于Whisper的参考值进行评分。ALAS仅需冻结前向传播和现成的ASR参考模型，无需训练或定制分类器，且校准至可跨任务比较的可解释性统一基线。我们将ALAS应用于四个开源语音-LLM（AF3、Qwen2-Audio、Qwen-Omni、SALMONN），在情绪识别（IEMOCAP）、开放式问答（LibriSQA）和多选音频理解（MMAU-speech）任务中发现，对齐的深度与强度反映了各模型音频编码器的设计及任务对声学与语义需求的侧重，且ALAS能追踪但不重复任务准确率，揭示出部分模型虽得分高却未真正基于音频进行建模。我们开源ALAS库，以便从业者可探测自身语音-LLM或尝试新任务。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of standardized metrics to evaluate how well Speech-LLMs align audio and text representations internally, the authors propose ALAS, a model-agnostic metric that computes cross-modal cosine similarity between audio and text hidden states using a Whisper-derived reference. ALAS requires only a frozen forward pass and an off-the-shelf ASR system, without training or custom classifiers, and is calibrated to a uniform baseline for cross-task comparison. Applied to four Speech-LLMs across three tasks—emotion recognition, open-ended SQA, and multi-choice audio understanding—ALAS revealed that alignment strength correlates with audio-encoder architecture and task demands, and often diverges from task accuracy, identifying models that perform well without genuine audio grounding.
为解决当前缺乏统一标准评估语音大模型内部音频与文本对齐质量的问题，作者提出ALAS，一种无需训练、仅依赖冻结前向传播和现成ASR系统的模型无关度量方法，通过计算音频与文本隐藏状态间的余弦相似度并参照Whisper生成的参考值进行评分。ALAS被应用于四个开源语音大模型在三项任务上的表现，结果表明对齐强度与模型架构和任务需求（语音 vs 语义）密切相关，且ALAS能揭示部分模型虽得分高但缺乏真实音频感知的现象，其表现常优于任务准确率。
Characterizing Nash Equilibria in Zero-Sum Games: A Physics-Inspired, Parallelizable Approach with a Linear Number of Gradient Queries
Authors: Taemin Kim, James P. Bailey
First: 2025-07-15T14:39:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:13:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study online optimization methods for zero-sum games, a fundamental problem in adversarial learning in machine learning, economics, and many other domains. Traditional methods approximate Nash equilibria (NE) using either regret-based methods (time-average convergence) or contraction-map-based methods (last-iterate convergence). We propose a new method based on Hamiltonian dynamics in physics and prove that it can characterize the set of NE in a finite (linear) number of iterations of alternating gradient descent in the unbounded setting, modulo degeneracy, a first in online optimization. Unlike standard methods for computing NE, our proposed approach can be parallelized and works with arbitrary learning rates, both firsts in algorithmic game theory. Experimentally, we support our results by showing our approach drastically outperforms standard methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：零和博弈中纳什均衡的特征化：一种受物理启发、可并行化且仅需线性梯度查询次数的方法
我们研究零和博弈中的在线优化方法，这是机器学习、经济学等多个领域中对抗性学习的基本问题。传统方法通过基于遗憾的算法（时间平均收敛）或收缩映射方法（最后迭代收敛）近似纳什均衡（NE）。我们提出一种基于物理学中哈密顿动力学的新方法，并证明其在无界设定下，仅需有限（线性）次数的交替梯度下降即可刻画NE集合（除退化情况外），这是在线优化领域的首次成果。与标准的NE计算方法不同，我们的方法可并行化，且支持任意学习率，均为算法博弈论中的首次。实验上，我们通过展示该方法显著优于标准方法来支持我们的结论。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and parallelizable methods to characterize Nash equilibria in zero-sum games, this work introduces a physics-inspired algorithm based on Hamiltonian dynamics. The method leverages alternating gradient descent and proves that it can identify the set of equilibria in a linear number of iterations, even in unbounded settings, without requiring degeneracy assumptions. Unlike traditional regret-based or contraction-map methods, the approach is inherently parallelizable and compatible with arbitrary learning rates, offering theoretical and practical advantages. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms existing algorithms in convergence speed and accuracy.
为高效并行求解零和博弈中的纳什均衡，本文提出一种受物理哈密顿动力学启发的新算法，能在有限（线性）次梯度查询内刻画均衡集合，这是在线优化领域的首次突破。该方法基于交替梯度下降，支持任意学习率并可并行化，突破了传统基于遗憾或压缩映射方法的局限。实验表明，该方法在收敛速度和精度上显著优于现有标准方法。
Querying an astronomical database using large language models: the ALeRCE text-to-SQL system
Authors: P. A. Estevez, J. Espejo-Moreira, S. Sanfeliu-Alvarez, F. Forster, A. M. Munoz Arancibia, G. Cabrera-Vives, F. E. Bauer, A. Bayo, M. Catelan, R. Dastidar, L. Hernandez-Garcia, J. A. Intriago, G. Pignata
First: 2026-06-16T16:12:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:12:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We develop a text-to-SQL (structured query language) system based on large language models (LLMs) using in-context learning and apply it to the Automatic Learning for the Rapid Classification of Events (ALeRCE) astronomical database. ALeRCE is a community broker for the Zwicky Transient Facility and the Vera C. Rubin Observatory. The system enables users to query the database in natural language (NL) and generates executable SQL queries. To develop and evaluate the system, we constructed a dataset of 110 NL/SQL pairs. We propose a step-by-step generation framework comprising four modules: schema linking, query classification, prompt decomposition, and self-correction. The performance of thirteen LLMs is evaluated using in-context learning and prompt engineering techniques. Text-to-SQL performance is assessed using the perfect-match (PM) rate for row identifiers (e.g., object identifiers) and column identifiers (i.e., column names). The proposed step-by-step framework consistently outperforms a direct-inference baseline, while the self-correction module consistently reduces execution errors. For Claude Opus 4.6, PM performance on row (column) identifiers is high for simple queries, reaching 0.97 (0.94), and decreases with query complexity to 0.44 (0.72) for medium queries and 0.59 (0.49) for hard queries. Among the thirteen evaluated models, the best-performing LLMs for the text-to-SQL task are Claude Opus 4.6, Gemini 2.5 Pro, Gemini 3 Flash, and GPT-5.2-Codex.

标题：使用大语言模型查询天文数据库：ALeRCE文本到SQL系统

Motivated by the need for natural language access to complex astronomical databases, this study develops ALeRCE text-to-SQL, a system using large language models to convert user queries into executable SQL. 为实现对复杂天文数据库（如ALeRCE）的自然语言查询，本研究基于大语言模型和上下文学习构建了文本到SQL系统。方法采用四模块分步生成框架：模式链接、查询分类、提示分解与自纠错。在110组自然语言与SQL配对数据上评估，该系统优于直接推理基线，自纠错模块显著降低执行错误。Claude Opus 4.6在简单查询中表现优异（行标识符0.97，列标识符0.94），但随查询复杂度下降（中等查询0.44/0.72，困难查询0.59/0.49）。13个模型中，Claude Opus 4.6、Gemini 2.5 Pro、Gemini 3 Flash和GPT-5.2-Codex表现最佳。
Deep Reinforcement Learning for Minimum Zero-Forcing Sets
Authors: Steve Halley, Maurício Gruppi
First: 2026-06-16T16:07:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:07:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper explores the problem of finding the minimum zero-forcing set on undirected graphs and proposes an adapted machine-learning framework to solve the problem. The minimum zero-forcing set problem is a graph coloring problem where the color of an initial set of nodes propagates throughout a network. The set of nodes is zero-forcing if it forces all uncolored nodes to change color under the constraint of the color-change rule. There are several applications to this problem across different domains such as network science, network control, and designing logical circuits. Finding the minimum zero-forcing set is shown to be NP-hard. We propose a reinforcement learning framework, SD-ZFS, that adapts the S2V-DQN architecture to the ZFS problem. We train several models on this adapted framework and analyze the performance across graph datasets that have varying structures. We evaluate how the models trained on the framework generalize, scale, and transfer to different network types. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of the framework when compared against the optimal solution and greedy heuristic. We provide further insight into how the ZFS problem can be solved through machine-learning and the influence of network structure on the problem.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于最小零强迫集的深度强化学习
本文探讨了在无向图上寻找最小零强迫集的问题，并提出了一种适配的机器学习框架来解决该问题。最小零强迫集问题是一个图着色问题，其中初始节点集合的颜色会通过网络传播至整个网络。若节点集合在颜色变化规则约束下能迫使所有未着色节点改变颜色，则该集合为零强迫集。该问题在不同领域（如网络科学、网络控制和逻辑电路设计）有广泛应用。寻找最小零强迫集被证明是NP难问题。我们提出了一种强化学习框架SD-ZFS，将S2V-DQN架构适配至ZFS问题。我们在该框架上训练多个模型，并分析其在结构各异的图数据集上的性能。我们评估了这些模型在不同网络类型中的泛化能力、可扩展性和迁移能力。结果表明，该框架在与最优解和贪心启发式方法对比时表现出有效性。我们进一步探讨了如何通过机器学习解决ZFS问题，以及网络结构对该问题的影响。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the NP-hard nature of finding minimum zero-forcing sets in undirected graphs and their applications in network science and circuit design, this paper proposes SD-ZFS, a reinforcement learning framework adapted from the S2V-DQN architecture to solve the problem. The method trains models on diverse graph datasets to learn optimal initial node selections that propagate color changes across the graph under the zero-forcing rule. Experimental results show that SD-ZFS outperforms greedy heuristics and approaches the optimal solution, while also demonstrating strong generalization and scalability across different graph structures.
鉴于寻找无向图最小零强迫集的NP难性质及其在网络科学和电路设计中的应用，本文提出SD-ZFS框架，该框架基于S2V-DQN架构进行调整。该方法通过强化学习训练模型，以识别能按零强迫规则传播颜色变化的初始节点集合。实验结果表明，SD-ZFS在多种图结构上优于贪心启发式方法，接近最优解，展现出良好的泛化能力、可扩展性和跨网络类型迁移能力。
OmniPlan: An Adaptive Framework for Timely and Near-Optimal Network Planning Optimization
Authors: Longlong Zhu, Jiashuo Yu, Zedi Chen, Yuhan Wu, Zhifan Jiang, Yuchen Xian, Yimeng Liu, Jiajie Su, Shaopeng Zhou, Xingyuan Li, Hongyan Liu, Xuan Liu, Dong Zhang, Chunming Wu, Xiang Chen
First: 2026-06-16T16:06:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:06:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Network planning optimization is a fundamental problem across diverse domains, including transportation systems, communication networks, and power grids. It requires simultaneous optimization of multiple competing objectives under complex constraints. Existing network planning optimization frameworks rely on mixed integer programming (MIP) solvers, heuristics, and deep reinforcement learning (DRL) models to compute planning decisions. However, they lack effective adaptability to diverse and dynamic user intents, thus leading to the trade-off between execution time and optimality. In this paper, we propose OmniPlan, an adaptive framework that achieves both timeliness and near-optimality in network planning optimization. To achieve the adaptability lacking in existing solutions, OmniPlan employs a large language model (LLM)-based interpreter to convert heterogeneous natural-language intents into a unified and quantifiable user-preference vector. Then it employs a mixture-of-experts architecture that integrates MIP solvers, heuristics, and DRL models as specialized experts, where OmniPlan adapts to diverse intents by dynamically selecting timely and near-optimal experts. Finally, it incorporates a DRL-based expert configuration module that fine-tunes optimization objective weights to align planning decisions with user-specific preferences. We evaluate OmniPlan with a representative real-world workload, i.e., distributed machine learning (ML), where we leverage OmniPlan to offload a wide spectrum of ML inference tasks, e.g., decision trees, SVM, naive Bayes, XGBoost, and random forests, onto a network of hardware devices. Our experiments on a real-world testbed indicate that OmniPlan achieves near-optimal and low-execution-time offloading for real-world ML inference tasks, reducing latency by up to 97.8\% and network device resource consumption by up to 11.5\%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OmniPlan：一种兼顾时效性与近最优性的自适应网络规划优化框架
网络规划优化是交通系统、通信网络和电网等多个领域中的基础性问题，需在复杂约束下同时优化多个相互竞争的目标。现有框架依赖混合整数规划（MIP）求解器、启发式算法和深度强化学习（DRL）模型来计算规划决策，但缺乏对多样且动态用户意图的有效适应，导致执行时间与最优性之间的权衡。本文提出OmniPlan，一种兼顾时效性与近最优性的自适应框架。为弥补现有方案的适应性不足，OmniPlan采用基于大语言模型（LLM）的解释器，将异构自然语言意图转化为统一且可量化的用户偏好向量；随后采用专家混合架构，整合MIP求解器、启发式算法和DRL模型作为专业专家，根据用户意图动态选择兼顾时效性与近最优性的专家；最后引入基于DRL的专家配置模块，微调优化目标权重，使规划决策与用户特定偏好对齐。我们在代表性真实工作负载——分布式机器学习（ML）中评估OmniPlan，利用其将决策树、SVM、朴素贝叶斯、XGBoost和随机森林等多种ML推理任务卸载至硬件设备网络。在真实测试平台上，实验表明OmniPlan可实现近最优且低执行时间的卸载，将延迟降低高达97.8%，网络设备资源消耗降低高达11.5%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to balance timeliness and optimality in dynamic network planning, OmniPlan introduces an adaptive framework that translates user intents via a large language model into quantifiable preference vectors, then dynamically selects among MIP solvers, heuristics, and DRL models using a mixture-of-experts architecture. A DRL-based module further tunes objective weights to align decisions with user preferences. Evaluated on distributed machine learning inference offloading across real hardware, OmniPlan achieves near-optimal results with significantly reduced latency—up to 97.8% improvement—and lower resource consumption—up to 11.5% reduction—compared to baseline methods.
为解决网络规划在动态环境中缺乏适应性的问题，OmniPlan提出一种兼顾时效性与近最优性的框架，通过大语言模型将用户自然语言意图转化为可量化偏好向量，并动态调用混合专家系统（含MIP求解器、启发式算法和深度强化学习模型）生成定制化决策。在真实分布式机器学习任务上的实验表明，OmniPlan可实现近最优的任务卸载，将延迟降低最高97.8%，网络设备资源消耗降低最高11.5%。
HistoRAG: Embedding Historical Methodology in Retrieval-Augmented Generation Through Critical Technical Practice
Authors: Noah J. Kim-Baumann, Torsten Hiltmann
First: 2026-06-16T16:03:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:03:37+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 6 figures. Companion preprint to a Journal of Digital History notebook article (under review)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is the prevailing architecture for grounding language model outputs in external evidence, yet its dominant evaluation paradigms and default configurations remain oriented toward factual question-answering. For interpretive disciplines such as historical studies, RAG embeds assumptions that conflict with scholarly practice. We introduce HistoRAG, a framework that translates historiographical principles into concrete architectural interventions. Separated retrieval and generation decouples source discovery from interpretation, temporal windowing enforces balanced source representation across the research period as a methodological requirement of historical inquiry, and LLM-as-judge evaluation makes relevance judgments transparent and contestable. We evaluate these interventions using SPIEGELragged, applied to 102,189 articles from Der Spiegel (1950-1979). Each intervention addresses a measurable deficiency in standard RAG: era-specific vocabulary retrieves zero chunks from the 1950s when using 1970s terminology, evidence of the temporal skew that motivates windowing; vector similarity and LLM-assessed relevance correlate only weakly (Spearman rho = 0.275), motivating post-retrieval evaluation; and keyword-based and semantic retrieval surface largely disjoint source pools, motivating an architecture in which both operate as complementary retrieval layers under a shared LLM evaluation filter. We also introduce the concept of Zwischentexte (intermediate texts that function as interpretive proposals rather than findings) as a framework for responsible integration of LLM-generated text into scholarly practice. The architecture offers a model for how domain-specific epistemological commitments can be translated into RAG design decisions, and may transfer to other interpretive disciplines working with large corpora.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HistoRAG：通过批判性技术实践将历史方法论嵌入检索增强生成
检索增强生成（RAG）是将语言模型输出锚定于外部证据的主流架构，但其主流评估范式和默认配置仍偏向事实问答。对于历史学等诠释性学科而言，RAG隐含了与学术实践相冲突的假设。我们提出HistoRAG框架，将史学原则转化为具体架构干预：分离检索与生成可解耦源发现与解释；时间窗口机制强制在研究时段内均衡呈现史料，作为历史研究的方法论要求；LLM作为评判者使相关性判断透明且可争议。我们使用SPIEGELragged评估这些干预措施，应用于《明镜》杂志1950-1979年间102,189篇文章。每项干预均针对标准RAG的可测量缺陷：以1970年代术语检索1950年代文献时零片段命中，揭示时间偏斜证据；向量相似度与LLM评估相关性仅弱相关（斯皮尔曼ρ=0.275），推动后检索评估；关键词与语义检索呈现高度不重叠的源库，推动构建双层互补检索架构，共享LLM评估过滤器。我们还引入“Zwischentexte”（作为解释性提案而非结论的中间文本）概念，作为负责任整合LLM生成文本至学术实践的框架。该架构提供将领域特定认识论承诺转化为RAG设计决策的模型，或可推广至其他处理大规模语料的诠释性学科。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the mismatch between standard RAG’s factual QA bias and the interpretive demands of historical scholarship, HistoRAG introduces architectural changes grounded in historiographical principles: it separates retrieval and generation, enforces temporal balance via windowing, and uses LLM-as-judge for transparent relevance evaluation. Evaluated on 102,189 Der Spiegel articles (1950–1979), the framework revealed that era-specific vocabulary retrieval fails without temporal windowing, vector similarity poorly correlates with human relevance (Spearman rho = 0.275), and keyword vs. semantic retrieval yield disjoint sources—prompting a dual-layer retrieval system under shared LLM filtering. The architecture also formalizes Zwischentexte as provisional interpretive proposals, offering a blueprint for translating domain-specific epistemologies into RAG design.
为解决标准RAG架构在历史研究等解释性学科中与事实问答范式不匹配的问题，HistoRAG提出三项架构改进：分离检索与生成以解耦史料发现与解释过程，引入时间窗口机制确保历史时期内资料均衡呈现，以及采用LLM作为评判者实现相关性判断的透明化与可争议性。在1950–1979年《明镜》杂志102,189篇文献上实验发现：不考虑时间窗口时，1950年代文献无法被1970年代术语检索到；向量相似度与LLM评估的相关性极弱（Spearman rho=0.275）；关键词与语义检索结果高度不重合——由此推动设计双层互补检索机制并统一LLM过滤。同时提出“中间文本”（Zwischentexte）概念，作为LLM生成内容融入学术实践的负责任框架，为将学科特定认识论转化为RAG设计提供范式。
DynFS-MoE: Dynamic Functional-Structural Mixture-of-Experts for Post-Traumatic Epilepsy Diagnosis
Authors: Jun-En Ding, Spencer Chen, Henry Noren, Daniel Valdivia, Christine Yohn, Suhina Patel, Taylor Zink, Hai Sun, Feng Liu
First: 2026-06-15T04:25:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:02:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Post-traumatic epilepsy (PTE) is a severe complication of traumatic brain injury (TBI), yet early identification remains challenging due to the complex structural and functional alterations it induces in the brain. To address this, we propose a dynamic multimodal Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) framework that integrates functional and structural MRI through time-aware functional-structural encoding and class-conditioned expert routing. Within this framework, modality-specific and cross-modal experts learn complementary representations, while a Modality-Class MoE (MCoE) module dynamically dispatches expert weights according to each classification objective. Experimental results across three binary classification tasks demonstrate that the framework consistently outperforms static fusion baselines, and high-interpretability analyses further reveal meaningful region-of-interest (ROI) interactions. This dynamic multimodal expert framework effectively captures class-dependent brain interaction patterns and provides an interpretable approach for PTE diagnosis and risk stratification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DynFS-MoE：用于创伤后癫痫诊断的动态功能-结构混合专家模型
创伤后癫痫（PTE）是脑外伤（TBI）的严重并发症，但由于其在大脑中引发的复杂结构和功能改变，早期识别仍具挑战性。为此，我们提出一种动态多模态混合专家（MoE）框架，通过时间感知的功能-结构编码和类别条件专家路由，整合功能与结构MRI。该框架中，模态特异性和跨模态专家学习互补表征，而模态-类别MoE（MCoE）模块根据每个分类目标动态分配专家权重。在三个二分类任务中的实验结果表明，该框架持续优于静态融合基线，高可解释性分析进一步揭示了有意义的感兴趣区域（ROI）交互。该动态多模态专家框架有效捕捉了与类别相关的脑交互模式，为PTE诊断与风险分层提供可解释方法。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of early diagnosis of post-traumatic epilepsy due to complex brain alterations, this study proposes DynFS-MoE, a dynamic multimodal Mixture-of-Experts framework that integrates functional and structural MRI using time-aware encoding and class-conditioned expert routing. The model employs modality-specific and cross-modal experts to learn complementary representations, and a Modality-Class MoE module dynamically assigns expert weights based on classification tasks. Experiments across three binary classification tasks show consistent performance gains over static fusion methods, with interpretability analyses revealing meaningful region-of-interest interactions that support PTE diagnosis and risk stratification.
为应对创伤后癫痫早期诊断困难的问题，该研究提出DynFS-MoE动态多模态专家混合框架，通过时间感知的脑功能-结构编码与类别条件专家路由整合功能与结构MRI数据。框架内模态特异及跨模态专家学习互补表征，Modality-Class MoE模块根据分类任务动态分配专家权重。在三项二分类任务中，该框架显著优于静态融合基线方法，可解释性分析揭示了有意义的感兴趣区域交互，有助于癫痫诊断与风险分层。
Trust the Right Teacher: Quality-Aware Self-Distillation for GUI Grounding
Authors: Jingyuan Huang, Zuming Huang, Yucheng Shi, Tianze Yang, Xiaoming Zhai, Wei Chu, Ninghao Liu
First: 2026-06-16T16:02:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:02:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Graphical user interface (GUI) grounding requires vision-language models (VLMs) to identify small target elements in high-resolution screenshots and predict precise screen coordinates. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is a promising post-training approach for this coordinate-sensitive task, since it provides dense token-level teacher signals beyond hard coordinate labels. However, naive OPSD is not well suited to GUI grounding: OPSD evaluates the teacher on student-generated prefixes, the quality of coordinate-token teacher signals can degrade when the prefix has already deviated from the target coordinate, leading to unreliable teacher signal. To mitigate this, We propose quality-aware self-distillation for VLM-based GUI grounding, which improves coordinate-token teacher-signal quality through soft correctness-aware gating and teacher-probability scaling. The soft correctness-aware gate checks whether the teacher's current coordinate-token prediction can still be completed into the ground-truth box under the student-generated prefix. If not, the corresponding teacher signal is down-weighted. Teacher-probability scaling then uses the teacher's confidence as a lightweight factor to further calibrate the strength of the gated supervision. A key empirical finding is that neither component alone improves overall performance, whereas combining them consistently improves performance. This suggests that the two mechanisms play complementary roles: correctness-aware gating suppresses unreliable coordinate-token supervision, while teacher-probability scaling calibrates the strength of the remaining signals. Experiments across six GUI grounding benchmarks show that our method consistently improves the base model and outperforms strong baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：信任正确的教师：面向GUI定位的质量感知自蒸馏
图形用户界面（GUI）定位要求视觉-语言模型（VLM）在高分辨率截图中识别微小目标元素并预测精确屏幕坐标。在线自蒸馏（OPSD）是该坐标敏感任务的有前景的后训练方法，因为它能提供超越硬坐标标签的密集token级教师信号。然而，朴素的OPSD并不适合GUI定位：OPSD在学生生成的前缀上评估教师，当前缀已偏离目标坐标时，坐标token教师信号质量会下降，导致教师信号不可靠。为解决此问题，我们提出面向VLM的GUI定位的质量感知自蒸馏方法，通过软正确性感知门控和教师概率缩放提升坐标token教师信号质量。软正确性感知门控检查教师当前的坐标token预测是否仍能在学生生成的前缀下完成至真实框；若不能，则相应教师信号被降权。教师概率缩放则利用教师置信度作为轻量因子，进一步校准门控监督的强度。一个关键经验发现是，单一组件无法提升整体性能，而组合使用则持续提升性能。这表明两种机制互补：正确性感知门控抑制不可靠的坐标token监督，而教师概率缩放校准剩余信号的强度。在六个GUI定位基准测试中，我们的方法持续提升基线模型并优于强基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of generating accurate coordinate predictions in GUI grounding tasks, this paper introduces quality-aware self-distillation for vision-language models, addressing the issue that naive on-policy self-distillation produces unreliable teacher signals when student prefixes deviate from target coordinates. The method employs a soft correctness-aware gate to suppress teacher signals that cannot be completed to ground-truth boxes given the student’s prefix, and teacher-probability scaling to further adjust supervision strength based on teacher confidence. Experiments across six benchmarks show that neither mechanism alone improves performance, but their combination consistently enhances results, indicating complementary roles: gating filters unreliable signals while scaling calibrates remaining supervision.
为了解决传统自蒸馏在GUI接地任务中因学生前缀偏离目标坐标而导致教师信号质量下降的问题，本文提出一种质量感知自蒸馏方法。该方法通过软正确性门控机制，在学生前缀无法完成至真实框时降低对应教师信号权重，并结合教师置信度进行概率缩放以校准剩余信号强度。实验表明，单一机制无法提升性能，但二者结合可稳定提升效果，说明门控机制用于过滤不可靠信号，而概率缩放机制用于调节有效信号强度。
Stable and Steerable Sparse Autoencoders with Weight Regularization
Authors: Piotr Jedryszek, Oliver M. Crook
First: 2026-03-04T15:46:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:00:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used to extract human-interpretable features from neural network activations, but their learned features can vary substantially across random seeds and training choices. To improve stability, we studied weight regularization by adding L1 or L2 penalties on encoder and decoder weights, and evaluate how regularization interacts with common SAE training defaults. On MNIST, we observe that L2 weight regularization produces a core of highly aligned features and, when combined with tied initialization and unit-norm decoder constraints, it dramatically increases cross-seed feature consistency. For TopK SAEs trained on language model activations (Pythia-70M-deduped), adding a small L2 weight penalty increased the fraction of features shared across three random seeds and roughly doubles steering success rates, while leaving the mean of automated interpretability scores essentially unchanged. Finally, in the regularized setting, activation steering success becomes better predicted by auto-interpretability scores, suggesting that regularization can align text-based feature explanations with functional controllability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：带权重正则化的稳定且可引导的稀疏自编码器
稀疏自编码器（SAEs）广泛用于从神经网络激活中提取人类可解释特征，但其学习到的特征在随机种子和训练选择上差异显著。为提高稳定性，我们研究了通过在编码器和解码器权重上添加L1或L2惩罚的权重正则化，并评估正则化与常见SAE训练默认设置的交互作用。在MNIST数据集上，我们观察到L2权重正则化能产生高度对齐的核心特征，当结合共享初始化和单位范数解码器约束时，可显著提升跨种子特征一致性。对于在语言模型激活（Pythia-70M-deduped）上训练的TopK SAEs，添加小量L2权重惩罚可使三随机种子间共享特征比例增加，且大致将引导成功率翻倍，同时自动化可解释性评分的均值基本不变。最后，在正则化设置下，激活引导成功率更可被自动可解释性评分预测，表明正则化可使基于文本的特征解释与功能可控性对齐。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability of sparse autoencoder features across training runs, this work investigates weight regularization—specifically L1 and L2 penalties on encoder and decoder weights—to stabilize feature extraction. Experiments on MNIST show that L2 regularization, combined with tied initialization and unit-norm decoder constraints, significantly improves cross-seed feature consistency by forming a core of highly aligned features. On language model activations, adding a small L2 penalty to TopK SAEs increases shared features across seeds and roughly doubles steering success rates, without altering interpretability scores. Crucially, regularization makes steering success more predictable from interpretability scores, indicating better alignment between textual feature explanations and functional controllability.
为解决稀疏自编码器特征在不同训练运行中不稳定的问题，该研究探讨了对编码器和解码器权重施加L1/L2正则化的影响。在MNIST数据集上，L2正则化结合权重共享初始化和单位范数解码器约束，显著提升了跨随机种子的特征一致性，并形成稳定的核心对齐特征。在语言模型激活上，对TopK SAE添加小量L2正则化可提高跨种子特征重叠率，并将控制成功率近倍提升，而自动化可解释性评分均值基本不变。更重要的是，正则化使控制成功率更可预测地由可解释性评分反映，表明文本特征解释与功能可控性更趋一致。
IsabeLLM: Automated Theorem Proving Applied to Formally Verifying Consensus
Authors: Elliot Jones, William Knottenbelt
First: 2026-06-16T16:00:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T16:00:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Advances in Artificial Intelligence (AI) have led AI for Theorem Proving to become a promising means of formally verifying computer systems. Whilst formal verification is traditionally reserved for safety-critical systems due to the required amount of expertise and effort, AI can help to automate a large amount of this workload and make it far more accessible. Blockchain-based systems are becoming increasingly popular and are frequently targeted by malicious actors, often resulting in huge financial losses, highlighting the need to better verify these systems and mitigate vulnerabilities. Arguably the most important component of these systems is the consensus protocol, which allows nodes to agree on decisions in a potentially adversarial environment. In this paper, we improve upon IsabeLLM, the automated theorem proving tool in Isabelle. Namely, we implement a Retrieval-Augmented Generation framework, Error tracing and counterexample generation for improved context supplied to the Large Language Model. Compatibility with the latest version of Isabelle and Sledgehammer is also implemented for improved efficiency. We compare the performance of the two versions of IsabeLLM in their ability to complete the verification of Bitcoin's Proof of Work consensus.
中文标题/摘要
标题：IsabeLLM：将自动定理证明应用于形式化验证共识
人工智能（AI）的进步使AI定理证明成为形式化验证计算机系统的有前景手段。尽管形式化验证传统上仅用于安全关键系统，因其需大量专业知识和人力，AI可帮助自动化大量工作并使其更易获取。基于区块链的系统日益流行，常遭恶意攻击，导致巨额经济损失，凸显了更好地验证这些系统并缓解漏洞的必要性。这些系统中最关键的组件是共识协议，它允许节点在潜在对抗环境中达成一致。本文改进了Isabelle中的自动定理证明工具IsabeLLM：我们实现了检索增强生成框架、错误追踪与反例生成，以提升大型语言模型的上下文供给；同时兼容最新版Isabelle与Sledgehammer，以提高效率。我们比较了两个版本IsabeLLM在完成比特币工作量证明共识验证能力上的表现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to formally verify blockchain consensus protocols against security threats, this paper enhances IsabeLLM—an automated theorem prover in Isabelle—by integrating a Retrieval-Augmented Generation framework, error tracing, and counterexample generation to improve context for the underlying Large Language Model. The updated version also ensures compatibility with the latest Isabelle and Sledgehammer for efficiency. Experimental results show that the enhanced IsabeLLM significantly outperforms the original in verifying Bitcoin’s Proof of Work consensus, demonstrating improved automation and reliability in formal verification tasks.
为提升日益脆弱的区块链系统（尤其是共识协议）的正式验证能力，本文改进了IsabeLLM这一Isabelle平台上的自动化定理证明工具，引入检索增强生成框架、错误追踪与反例生成机制，以优化大语言模型的上下文输入，并确保与最新版Isabelle和Sledgehammer兼容。实验结果表明，改进后的IsabeLLM在验证比特币工作量证明共识协议时，显著优于原版，展现出更强的自动化能力和验证可靠性。
WireCraft: A Simulation Benchmark for Industrial DLO Manipulation
Authors: Chongyu Zhu, Ramy ElMallah, Hyegang Kim, Zachary Tang, Jiachen Rao, Artem Arutyunov, Seungyeon Ha, Chi-Guhn Lee
First: 2026-06-16T15:59:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T15:59:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deformable Linear Objects (DLOs), such as wires and cables, are central to industrial assembly. Unlike rigid objects, whose state is captured by a 6-DoF pose, DLOs have an infinite-dimensional configuration space and deform continuously under contact with grippers, fixtures, and the workspace, making them a demanding benchmark for general dexterous manipulation. Despite their importance, policy development and comparison remain difficult: existing benchmarks are often tied to specific hardware setups, lack modular and customizable task assets, or study generic deformable-object tasks without the fixtures relevant to real-world industrial wire manipulation. Few benchmarks align simulation, real-world data, and shared evaluation protocols. To bridge this gap, we introduce WireCraft, a simulation benchmark for industrial DLO manipulation with configurable difficulty and assets, spanning three task families: connector insertion, clip routing, and channel seating. It supports two complementary DLO physics models, articulated and deformable, and the trajectories come from both simulation and a physical UR5. We benchmark reinforcement learning (RL), imitation learning (IL), and vision-language-action (VLA) policies under shared metrics. Privileged state-based RL solves a representative setting in each task family with over 82\% success, confirming the tasks are well-posed. For connector insertion, however, the transition from reaching the socket to contact-rich alignment remains a key bottleneck for vision RL, IL, and VLA policies. These results indicate that industrial DLO manipulation, though tractable under privileged state, remains an open challenge for current vision-based learning. The benchmark, data, and tools will be open-sourced upon acceptance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WireCraft：工业柔性线状物体操作的仿真基准
柔性线状物体（DLOs），如电线和电缆，在工业装配中至关重要。与刚性物体不同，其状态不能仅用6自由度位姿描述，DLOs具有无限维构型空间，且在与夹持器、固定装置和工作空间接触时持续变形，使其成为通用灵巧操作的严苛基准。尽管重要，现有基准常绑定特定硬件、缺乏模块化可定制任务资产，或研究通用柔性物体任务而忽略工业线缆操作所需的固定装置。极少基准同时涵盖仿真、真实世界数据与共享评估协议。为弥合此差距，我们提出WireCraft，一个支持可配置难度与资产的工业DLO操作仿真基准，涵盖三类任务：连接器插入、夹子布线与通道安装。它支持两种互补的DLO物理模型——铰接式与柔性式，并提供仿真与物理UR5机器人生成的轨迹。我们在统一指标下评估强化学习（RL）、模仿学习（IL）与视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略。基于特权状态的RL在每类任务代表性场景中成功率超82%，验证任务设计合理。然而，连接器插入任务中，从抵达插座到接触密集对齐的过渡仍是视觉RL、IL与VLA策略的关键瓶颈。结果表明，尽管在特权状态条件下工业DLO操作可行，当前基于视觉的学习方法仍面临开放挑战。基准、数据与工具将在论文被接受后开源。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for standardized, hardware-agnostic benchmarks for industrial deformable linear object (DLO) manipulation, WireCraft introduces a simulation framework with configurable tasks and physics models to evaluate policy performance. It supports three industrial task families—connector insertion, clip routing, and channel seating—using both articulated and deformable DLO physics, with trajectories derived from both simulation and a physical UR5 robot. Experimental results show that privileged state-based reinforcement learning achieves over 82% success across tasks, validating the benchmark’s design, but vision-based methods like imitation learning and vision-language-action policies struggle with the transition from reaching to contact-rich alignment in connector insertion, highlighting a key challenge for vision-driven approaches in real-world DLO manipulation.
为解决工业柔性线状物体（DLO）操作缺乏统一、可配置评估基准的问题，WireCraft提出一套支持三种任务类型（连接器插入、夹子布线、通道安装）的仿真框架，具备可调难度与可定制资产。该框架采用两种DLO物理模型（铰接式与柔性）并结合仿真与真实UR5机器人轨迹。在统一评估指标下，对强化学习、模仿学习和视觉-语言-动作策略进行测试，结果显示特权状态下的强化学习在各任务中成功率超82%，但视觉驱动方法在连接器插入任务中从到达目标到接触对齐阶段仍存在显著瓶颈，表明当前视觉驱动方法在工业DLO操作中仍面临核心挑战。
S4oP: Operator-level Pruning of Structured State Space Models for Resource-Constrained Devices
Authors: Marco Deano, Filippo Ziche, Nicola Bombieri
First: 2026-06-16T15:59:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T15:59:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Structured State Space Models (SSMs), including the S4 and S4D architectures, have recently emerged as powerful alternatives to attention-based models for capturing long-range dependencies in sequential data. Despite their strong empirical performance, deploying these models in time- and resource-constrained settings remains challenging due to their computational and memory demands. In this paper, we propose a novel incremental, operator-level pruning approach for S4- and S4D-based models that significantly reduces inference cost while preserving predictive performance. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to systematically investigate structured operator pruning for SSMs. Our method progressively prunes model operators by interleaving structured masking with fine-tuning, while jointly monitoring accuracy and inference latency. We implement this approach within a unified training and evaluation framework that enables systematic exploration of efficiency-accuracy trade-offs. Experiments across multiple benchmark datasets show that pruning up to 70% of the model operators preserves the performance of the original models in most cases, while substantially reducing inference latency. These results demonstrate that structured operator pruning is an effective and previously unexplored strategy for improving the efficiency of SSMs and facilitate their deployment in practical, resource-constrained scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：S4oP：面向资源受限设备的结构化状态空间模型算子级剪枝
结构化状态空间模型（SSMs），包括S4和S4D架构，近期作为捕捉序列数据长程依赖关系的注意力机制替代方案崭露头角。尽管其在实证性能上表现优异，但在时间和资源受限环境下部署仍面临计算与内存需求高的挑战。本文提出了一种针对S4和S4D模型的新型增量式算子级剪枝方法，显著降低推理成本同时保持预测性能。据我们所知，这是首次系统性研究SSMs结构化算子剪枝的工作。我们的方法通过交替进行结构化掩码与微调，逐步剪枝模型算子，并同时监控准确率与推理延迟。该方法在统一的训练与评估框架中实现，支持系统性探索效率-精度权衡。在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，剪枝高达70%的模型算子在多数情况下仍能保持原始模型性能，同时大幅降低推理延迟。结果表明，结构化算子剪枝是一种有效且此前未被探索的提升SSMs效率的策略，有助于其在实际资源受限场景中的部署。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational and memory demands of structured state space models like S4 and S4D, which hinder their deployment on resource-constrained devices, this paper introduces S4oP: an operator-level pruning method that incrementally removes model operators while preserving performance. The approach interleaves structured masking with fine-tuning and jointly tracks accuracy and latency during pruning, enabling efficient exploration of efficiency-accuracy trade-offs. Experimental results across multiple benchmarks show that pruning up to 70% of operators typically preserves original model performance while significantly reducing inference latency, demonstrating the effectiveness of this novel pruning strategy for practical deployment.
为应对S4和S4D等结构化状态空间模型在资源受限设备上部署时的高计算与内存开销问题，本文提出S4oP方法，通过增量式剪枝模型运算符并结合结构化掩码与微调，在保持预测性能的同时显著降低推理延迟。实验表明，在多数数据集上剪枝高达70%的运算符仍能维持原始模型性能，验证了该结构化剪枝策略在提升SSM效率和实际部署可行性方面的有效性。
Any2Any: Efficient Cross-Embodiment Transfer for Humanoid Whole-Body Tracking
Authors: Ming Yang, Tao Yu, Feng Li, Hua Chen
First: 2026-05-22T15:10:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T15:57:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Whole-body tracking (WBT) models have become a key foundation for humanoid robots, enabling them to imitate diverse motions with high fidelity. Training such models from scratch requires large-scale data and computation, making rapid deployment on new humanoid platforms costly. This raises a natural question: Can pretrained WBT models transfer across embodiments with minimal adaptation? To answer this question, we propose Any2Any, a paradigm that efficiently transfers an existing WBT specialist to a new humanoid embodiment with only a small amount of data and compute. Any2Any first performs kinematic alignment between source and target humanoids, aligning their input and output spaces so that the pretrained source policy can be meaningfully reused on the target embodiment.Any2Any then performs dynamics adaptation by applying lightweight parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) components to selected dynamics-sensitive modules, preserving useful behavioral priors while enabling targeted adaptation to the target robot. Extensive experiments on multiple humanoid platforms and pretrained backbones show that Any2Any substantially accelerates convergence and reduces training cost compared with training from scratch, while achieving competitive or superior tracking performance. Notably, using only 1% of the compute and data required for full training, Any2Any successfully transfers Sonic models pre-trained on Unitree G1 to LimX Oli and LimX Luna. These results suggest that pretrained WBT specialists can be efficiently reused across embodiments, providing a scalable path toward deploying humanoid whole-body control on new robots.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Any2Any：高效跨机体的人形全身跟踪迁移
全身跟踪（WBT）模型已成为人形机器人的关键基础，使其能够高保真地模仿多样化的动作。从零开始训练此类模型需要大规模数据和计算资源，导致在新的人形平台上快速部署成本高昂。这自然引发一个问题：预训练的WBT模型能否仅需少量调整即可在不同机体间迁移？为回答此问题，我们提出了Any2Any范式，该范式仅需少量数据和计算资源，即可高效地将现有WBT专家模型迁移到新的人形机体上。Any2Any首先对源机体与目标机体进行运动学对齐，使输入输出空间对齐，从而有意义地复用预训练的源策略；随后通过轻量级参数高效微调（PEFT）组件对选定的动态敏感模块进行动态适应，在保留有用行为先验的同时实现针对目标机器人的定向调整。在多个机器人平台和预训练骨干网络上的大量实验表明，Any2Any相比从零训练显著加速收敛并降低训练成本，同时实现具有竞争力或更优的跟踪性能。值得注意的是，仅使用完整训练所需计算和数据的1%，Any2Any成功将预训练于Unitree G1的Sonic模型迁移至LimX Oli和LimX Luna。这些结果表明，预训练的WBT专家模型可在不同机体间高效复用，为在新机器人上部署人形全身控制提供了可扩展的路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost of training whole-body tracking models from scratch for new humanoid robots, this work proposes Any2Any, a method enabling efficient cross-embodiment transfer of pretrained models. It first aligns the kinematics between source and target robots to map input-output spaces, then applies lightweight parameter-efficient fine-tuning to dynamics-sensitive modules to adapt behavior while preserving prior knowledge. Experiments across multiple platforms show Any2Any reduces training compute and data by 99% compared to full training, while achieving competitive or superior tracking performance, successfully transferring Sonic models from Unitree G1 to LimX Oli and LimX Luna.
为降低在新人形机器人上从零训练全身跟踪模型的成本，本文提出Any2Any方法，实现预训练模型在不同机器人躯体间的高效迁移。该方法首先对源机器人与目标机器人进行运动学对齐，使输入输出空间匹配，再对关键动力学模块应用轻量级参数高效微调，保留行为先验并实现针对性适配。实验表明，Any2Any在多个平台上的表现可媲美或超越从头训练，同时将计算与数据需求降低99%，成功将预训练于Unitree G1的模型迁移到LimX Oli和LimX Luna，仅需原训练资源的1%。
EAGG: Embodiment-Aligned Grasp Generation via Geometry-Aware Graph Conditioning
Authors: Wanhao Niu, Qiyan Ke, Yuan Sun, Hao Sun, Jie Xu, Muyuan Ma, Ruiqi Hu, Fuchun Sun
First: 2026-06-16T15:56:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T15:56:44+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 8 figures. Code is available at https://github.com/wanhaoniu/EAGG
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Cross-end-effector grasp generation seeks a unified model that generalizes across objects and across embodiments ranging from parallel grippers to dexterous end effectors. Existing grasp generators are typically designed for a fixed embodiment or encode embodiment identity with a static descriptor, which weakens transfer when topology, actuation coupling, and contact geometry differ substantially. We present EAGG, an embodiment-aligned grasp generator that represents each embodiment with a topology-aware end-effector graph and an embodiment-specific low-dimensional end-effector control space. A frozen end-effector-cognition backbone converts the current articulated state into geometry-aware tokens that act as a reusable morphology prior, and iterative geometry injection refreshes these tokens throughout sampling so that conditioning remains synchronized with the evolving end-effector geometry. On the MultiGripperGrasp benchmark, EAGG reaches 56.17% average success across six training end effectors, remaining within 1.10 percentage points of specialized training while preserving transfer to finetuning and zero-shot end effectors. Iterative geometry injection further reduces the pooled median contact distance from 0.239 cm to 0.189 cm. These results show that cross-end-effector grasp generation is strengthened by aligning embodiment structure inside a shared generator rather than suppressing embodiment differences. Code is available at https://github.com/wanhaoniu/EAGG.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EAGG：通过几何感知图条件化实现具身对齐的抓取生成
跨末端执行器抓取生成旨在构建一个统一模型，使其能在不同物体和不同具身（从并行夹爪到灵巧末端执行器）间泛化。现有抓取生成器通常针对固定具身设计，或使用静态描述符编码具身身份，这在拓扑结构、驱动耦合和接触几何差异显著时削弱了迁移能力。我们提出EAGG，一种具身对齐的抓取生成器，通过拓扑感知的末端执行器图和具身特异的低维末端执行器控制空间表示每个具身。一个冻结的末端执行器认知骨干网络将当前关节状态转换为几何感知的令牌，作为可复用的形态先验；迭代几何注入在采样过程中刷新这些令牌，确保条件化始终与演化的末端执行器几何同步。在MultiGripperGrasp基准测试中，EAGG在六个训练末端执行器上平均成功率达56.17%，与专用训练模型差距仅1.10个百分点，同时保持对微调和零样本末端执行器的迁移能力。迭代几何注入还将聚合中位接触距离从0.239厘米降低至0.189厘米。结果表明，通过在共享生成器内对齐具身结构，而非抑制具身差异，可增强跨末端执行器抓取生成能力。代码见https://github.com/wanhaoniu/EAGG。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a unified grasp generation model that generalizes across diverse robotic end-effectors, EAGG introduces a geometry-aware graph conditioning framework that aligns embodiment structure within a shared generator. It represents each end-effector as a topology-aware graph and a low-dimensional control space, using a frozen backbone to convert articulated states into reusable geometry tokens, which are iteratively refined during sampling to stay synchronized with evolving end-effector geometry.
