From Reasoning Traces to Reusable Modules: Understanding Compositional Generalization in Language Model Reasoning
Authors: Lingjing Kong, Xin Liu, Guangyi Chen, Martin Q. Ma, Xiangchen Song, Yuekai Sun, Mikhail Yurochkin, Taylor W. Killian, Ruslan Salakhutdinov, Kun Zhang, Eric P. Xing, Zhengzhong Liu
First: 2026-06-16T15:55:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T15:55:28+00:00
Comments: ICML2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Post-training pipelines that combine supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with reinforcement learning (RL) have emerged as the key recipe for transforming large language models (LLMs) into robust reasoners. We argue that this combined success is driven by compositional generalization, which we formalize through a hierarchical latent selection model. In this framework, reasoning traces are generated by a cascade of discrete latent selection variables corresponding to reusable atomic modules, including both skills (local operations) and routing mechanisms (how intermediate information is selected, reused, and composed). Within this model, we theoretically show that SFT and RL play asymmetric, complementary roles: SFT supplies the raw module materials in compositional traces, and RL decomposes those traces to identify the latent atomic modules and enable compositional generalization. We design controlled experiments to validate this theory. Our results demonstrate that RL can extract atomic modules from compound traces supplied by SFT and recombine them to solve new configurations. Moreover, we find that training on compound traces yields stronger generalization than training on isolated atomic modules. Finally, we investigate the relationship between SFT and RL data and identify an effective protocol in which SFT ensures coverage of all atomic modules through compositional traces, while RL focuses on novel compositions outside the SFT support to drive exploration.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从推理轨迹到可复用模块：理解语言模型推理中的组合泛化
结合监督微调（SFT）与强化学习（RL）的后训练流程已成为将大型语言模型（LLMs）转化为稳健推理器的关键方法。我们认为，这种联合成功源于组合泛化，我们通过分层潜在选择模型对其进行形式化。在此框架中，推理轨迹由对应可复用原子模块的离散潜在选择变量级联生成，包括技能（局部操作）和路由机制（如何选择、重用和组合中间信息）。在该模型中，我们理论证明SFT与RL扮演不对称且互补的角色：SFT提供组合轨迹中的原始模块材料，而RL则分解这些轨迹以识别潜在原子模块并实现组合泛化。我们设计了受控实验验证该理论。结果表明，RL可从SFT提供的复合轨迹中提取原子模块并重组以解决新配置。此外，我们发现训练于复合轨迹比训练于孤立原子模块能获得更强的泛化能力。最后，我们研究了SFT与RL数据之间的关系，并识别出一种有效协议：SFT通过组合轨迹确保覆盖所有原子模块，而RL则聚焦于SFT支持范围外的新组合以驱动探索。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the success of SFT-RL pipelines in enhancing LLM reasoning, the paper investigates compositional generalization as the underlying mechanism, formalized via a hierarchical latent selection model that decomposes reasoning into reusable atomic modules and routing mechanisms. The authors theoretically demonstrate that SFT provides compositional traces containing raw module materials, while RL decomposes these traces to identify latent modules and enable generalization through recombination. Experimental results confirm that RL can extract and recombine atomic modules from SFT-generated compound traces to solve novel problems, and that training on compound traces outperforms training on isolated modules. The study further identifies an optimal protocol where SFT ensures comprehensive module coverage and RL drives exploration through novel compositions.
为解释监督微调与强化学习结合提升语言模型推理能力的原因，本文提出组合泛化是关键机制，并通过分层潜在选择模型进行形式化，该模型将推理轨迹建模为可复用原子模块的离散潜在变量组合。理论分析表明，SFT提供组合轨迹作为原材料，而RL负责分解并重组原子模块以实现泛化。实验结果证实，RL能从SFT生成的复合轨迹中提取并重组模块解决新配置，且训练复合轨迹比孤立模块表现更优，最优策略是SFT覆盖所有模块，RL专注于SFT未覆盖的组合以驱动探索。
Multimodal Graph Negative Learning
Authors: Zhengyu Wu, Xu Wang, Hongchao Qin, Xunkai Li, Guang Zeng, Rong-Hua Li, Guoren Wang
First: 2026-06-11T03:47:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T15:47:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal attributed graphs (MAGs) integrate graph topology with heterogeneous modality attributes, such as text and images, thereby enabling richer modeling of complex relational systems. However, such expressiveness also makes learning on MAGs depend on multiple semantic sources, including structural topology, textual and visual attributes, each of which can be regarded as a branch for node representation. Node-level branch semantic imbalance arises when these branches differ across nodes in semantic informativeness and reliability: a branch that provides discriminative semantics for one node may mislead another due to bias in modality quality or structural context. Existing methods often mitigate such heterogeneity through cross-branch agreement or alignment, implicitly treating the dominant prediction as reliable supervision. When the dominant branch is biased, forced imitation may propagate its bias to other branches and suppress original semantics that are useful for classification. We propose GraphMNL, a graph-aware multimodal negative learning framework that addresses this issue by using Negative Learning as cross-branch guidance. Instead of forcing inferior branches to imitate a teacher prediction, the model teaches them which classes a node is unlikely to belong to. GraphMNL builds a branch library, identifies dominant and inferior branches via graph-aware reliability arbitration, gates unstable transfer, and applies target-preserving negative learning over non-target classes. This design decouples target supervision from branch guidance so that supervised losses learn the correct class, while Negative Learning suppresses unlikely alternatives when branch agreement is unreliable. Through the comprehensive experimental evaluation, GraphMNL achieves the best performance on Grocery datasets with 72.47% accuracy and 76.60 F1 score on Reddit M datasets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多模态图负样本学习
多模态属性图（MAGs）将图拓扑与异构模态属性（如文本和图像）相结合，从而实现对复杂关系系统的更丰富建模。然而，这种表达能力也使得在MAGs上学习依赖于多个语义源，包括结构拓扑、文本和视觉属性，每个可视为节点表示的一个分支。当这些分支在节点间语义信息量和可靠性不一致时，会出现节点级分支语义失衡：一个分支对某节点提供判别性语义，但因模态质量或结构上下文偏差，可能误导另一节点。现有方法常通过跨分支一致性或对齐缓解此类异质性，隐式地将主导预测视为可靠监督。当主导分支存在偏差时，强制模仿可能将该偏差传播至其他分支，并抑制对分类有用的原始语义。我们提出GraphMNL，一种图感知的多模态负样本学习框架，通过负样本学习作为跨分支指导解决此问题。该框架不强制劣质分支模仿教师预测，而是教导它们节点不可能属于哪些类别。GraphMNL构建分支库，通过图感知可靠性仲裁识别主导与劣质分支，门控不稳定迁移，并在非目标类别上应用目标保持的负样本学习。该设计将目标监督与分支指导解耦，使监督损失学习正确类别，而负样本学习在分支一致性不可靠时抑制不可能的替代选项。通过全面实验评估，GraphMNL在Grocery数据集上达到72.47%准确率和76.60 F1分数，在Reddit M数据集上表现最佳。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of semantic imbalance across heterogeneous modalities in multimodal attributed graphs, GraphMNL introduces a graph-aware negative learning framework to mitigate bias propagation from dominant branches. It constructs a branch library, identifies reliable branches via graph-aware reliability arbitration, and gates unstable transfers, then applies target-preserving negative learning to guide inferior branches toward suppressing unlikely classes. Experiments show GraphMNL outperforms existing methods, achieving 72.47% accuracy and 76.60 F1 score on Reddit M datasets, demonstrating its effectiveness in decoupling supervision from branch guidance.
为解决多模态图中不同模态分支语义不平衡导致的偏差传播问题，GraphMNL提出一种图感知的负样本学习框架。该方法通过图感知可靠性仲裁识别可靠与不可靠分支，并引导弱分支学习节点不可能所属的类别，而非强制模仿主导分支。实验表明，GraphMNL在Reddit M数据集上达到72.47%准确率和76.60 F1分数，优于现有方法，验证了其在解耦监督与分支引导、抑制误导性语义方面的有效性。
Edge Flow: A Tractable and Predictive Continuous-Time Model for Gradient Descent at the Edge of Stability
Authors: Pierre Marion
First: 2026-06-16T15:45:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T15:45:47+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 13 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Gradient descent in deep learning may operate at the edge of stability (EoS), a regime in which the largest eigenvalue of the loss Hessian hovers near the stability threshold $2/η$, where $η$ is the learning rate. Classical analysis tools such as gradient flow and the descent lemma do not apply here, motivating the search for a continuous-time model valid at EoS. We propose Edge Flow, a system of three coupled ordinary differential equations that provides a tractable, faithful, and predictive model of gradient descent dynamics at EoS. Edge Flow decomposes the dynamics into a center, an oscillation direction, and an oscillation magnitude. The center follows a modified gradient flow on a symmetrized loss; the direction tracks a top eigenvector of the Hessian via Rayleigh quotient dynamics; and the magnitude grows or decays exponentially depending on whether the sharpness exceeds or falls below the threshold $2/η$. Crucially, sharpness stabilization emerges from the coupled dynamics via a self-stabilization feedback loop. Discretizing Edge Flow only requires two gradient evaluations and one Hessian--vector product at each iteration. We demonstrate empirically that Edge Flow tracks the dynamics of gradient descent at least as faithfully as previously proposed continuous-time EoS models, while in addition resolving the oscillation of the sharpness at the onset of EoS, and that it provides a principled framework for understanding and mitigating instabilities in this regime.
中文标题/摘要
标题：边缘流：一种适用于稳定性边缘梯度下降的可处理且可预测的连续时间模型
深度学习中的梯度下降可能运行在稳定性边缘（EoS）区域，该区域中损失Hessian的最大特征值接近稳定性阈值2/η（η为学习率）。经典分析工具如梯度流和下降引理在此不适用，因此需要寻找适用于EoS的连续时间模型。我们提出边缘流，一个由三个耦合常微分方程组成的系统，为EoS下的梯度下降动力学提供了一个可处理、忠实且可预测的模型。边缘流将动力学分解为中心、振荡方向和振荡幅度。中心遵循对称损失上的修改梯度流；方向通过Rayleigh商动力学追踪Hessian的主特征向量；幅度根据尖锐度是否超过或低于阈值2/η呈指数增长或衰减。关键的是，尖锐度的稳定化通过耦合动力学中的自稳定反馈环路自然产生。对边缘流进行离散化仅需每迭代一次两个梯度评估和一个Hessian-向量乘积。我们实证表明，边缘流至少与之前提出的连续时间EoS模型一样忠实追踪梯度下降动力学，同时解决了EoS起始时尖锐度的振荡问题，并提供了一个理解与缓解该区域不稳定性问题的原理性框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model gradient descent dynamics at the edge of stability — where the loss Hessian’s largest eigenvalue approaches the stability threshold 2/η — the paper introduces Edge Flow, a continuous-time model composed of three coupled ODEs that decomposes dynamics into center, oscillation direction, and magnitude. The center evolves via modified gradient flow on a symmetrized loss, the direction follows the top Hessian eigenvector via Rayleigh quotient dynamics, and the magnitude grows or decays exponentially based on whether sharpness exceeds or falls below 2/η. Edge Flow captures sharpness oscillations at EoS onset and exhibits self-stabilization via feedback, and its discrete implementation requires only two gradient evaluations and one Hessian-vector product per step. Empirically, it matches or outperforms prior continuous-time models in fidelity while resolving sharpness oscillations and offering a principled framework for analyzing and stabilizing training near instability.
为了解决在损失函数Hessian最大特征值接近稳定性阈值2/η时经典分析工具失效的问题，本文提出了Edge Flow模型，该模型由三个耦合常微分方程组成，将动态分解为中心、振荡方向和振荡幅度。中心通过修改的梯度流演化，方向通过Rayleigh商动力学追踪Hessian的主特征向量，幅度则根据锐度是否超过阈值呈指数增长或衰减。实验表明，Edge Flow能准确追踪梯度下降动态，解决锐度在EoS起始时的振荡问题，且每步仅需两次梯度计算和一次Hessian-向量乘积，优于现有模型，为理解与缓解该区域的不稳定性提供了理论框架。
A Unified Framework for Context-Aware and Relation-Aware Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation
Authors: Haoyang Zhong, Yifei Sun, Antong Zhang, Chunping Wang, Lei Chen, Yang Yang
Venue: WWW
First: 2026-06-16T15:44:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T15:44:10+00:00
Comments: Accepted at The ACM Web Conference 2026 (WWW '26)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as a paradigm for enhancing large language models (LLMs) with external knowledge, yet existing graph-based methods face a fundamental limitation: entity-centric and chunk-centric approaches operate on representations anchored to original text without true knowledge fusion. While entity-centric methods connect logically related content and chunk-centric methods preserve context, both retrieve information separately through similarity search, missing emergent understanding from their synthesis. In this paper, we propose HyGRAG, a hierarchical graph RAG framework that transcends source documents by addressing three core challenges: constructing summaries that genuinely integrate contextual and relational information, leveraging these synthesized representations to access emergent knowledge during retrieval, and efficiently updating hierarchical structures for dynamic corpora. Specifically, we design hierarchical index structures over hybrid graphs with both chunk and entity nodes, then iteratively cluster them and generate LLM-based summaries. Then, we design context and relation-aware retrieval that searches across all abstraction levels while expanding through community membership. Moreover, we enable dynamic knowledge update through attachment-based algorithms with only local re-summarization. Experimental results show that HyGRAG improves the average accuracy of multi-hop reasoning tasks by 9.7%, while maintaining reasonable efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种面向上下文感知与关系感知的图结构检索增强生成统一框架
检索增强生成（RAG）已成为增强大型语言模型（LLM）外部知识的范式，但现有图方法存在根本性局限：实体中心和片段中心方法均基于原始文本锚定的表示进行操作，缺乏真正的知识融合。实体中心方法连接逻辑相关内容，片段中心方法保留上下文，但二者均通过相似性搜索独立检索信息，未能从其合成中获得涌现理解。本文提出HyGRAG，一种分层图RAG框架，超越源文档，解决三大核心挑战：构建真正融合上下文与关系信息的摘要、利用合成表示在检索中访问涌现知识、以及高效更新分层结构以适应动态语料库。具体而言，我们在混合图（含片段与实体节点）上设计分层索引结构，随后迭代聚类并生成基于LLM的摘要。接着，我们设计上下文与关系感知的检索机制，跨所有抽象层级搜索并通过社区成员扩展。此外，通过基于附加的算法仅局部重摘要实现动态知识更新。实验表明，HyGRAG在多跳推理任务中平均准确率提升9.7%，同时保持合理效率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing graph-based RAG methods that fail to fuse contextual and relational knowledge effectively, this paper introduces HyGRAG, a hierarchical graph RAG framework. It constructs hybrid graphs with chunk and entity nodes, iteratively clusters them, and generates LLM-based summaries to capture integrated knowledge. The framework supports context- and relation-aware retrieval across abstraction levels using community membership expansion and enables dynamic updates via local re-summarization. Experiments demonstrate a 9.7% improvement in average accuracy for multi-hop reasoning tasks while preserving reasonable efficiency.
为解决现有图结构RAG方法在知识融合上的不足，本文提出HyGRAG框架，通过构建融合上下文与关系的分层摘要、跨抽象层级的社区感知检索及局部重摘要驱动的动态更新机制，有效提升知识检索能力。实验表明，该方法在多跳推理任务中平均准确率提升9.7%，同时保持合理效率。
Tensor-based second-order causal discovery
Authors: Nathan Ouyang, Kexin Wan, Anna Seigal
First: 2026-06-16T15:43:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T15:43:44+00:00
Comments: 27 pages, 7 figures. Code available at https://github.com/QWE123665/Tensor-based-Second-order-Causal-Discovery
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Causal discovery seeks to uncover the causal dependencies among variables. For this purpose, we propose an algorithm called Tensor-based Second-order Causal Discovery (TSCD). Its input is a tensor obtained from the covariance matrices of observational and interventional data. Assuming the causal dependencies follow a linear structural equation model on a directed acyclic graph (DAG), TSCD outputs the DAG and the functions on its edges, requiring only that the noise variables are uncorrelated. We also implement a version of the approach for nonlinear models. Our focus on second-order statistics (via the covariance matrices) is motivated by their statistical and computational efficiency relative to higher-order moments, their identifiability relative to first-order statistics, and that they work regardless of whether the variables are Gaussian. We show that TSCD has identifiable causal order and parameters from a number of interventions that is logarithmic in the number of variables. Experiments show that TSCD is robust to noise, competitive with existing methods, and scales to hundreds of variables.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于张量的二阶因果发现
因果发现旨在揭示变量间的因果依赖关系。为此，我们提出了一种名为张量二阶因果发现（TSCD）的算法。其输入为从观测数据和干预数据的协方差矩阵得到的张量。假设因果依赖遵循有向无环图（DAG）上的线性结构方程模型，TSCD输出该DAG及其边上的函数，仅需噪声变量不相关。我们还实现了适用于非线性模型的版本。我们关注二阶统计量（通过协方差矩阵）是因其相对于高阶矩具有统计和计算效率，相对于一阶统计量具有可识别性，且无论变量是否服从高斯分布均适用。我们证明TSCD可从变量数对数数量级的干预中识别因果顺序和参数。实验表明，TSCD对噪声鲁棒，与现有方法竞争性相当，且可扩展至数百个变量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and identifiable causal discovery under minimal assumptions, the paper introduces Tensor-based Second-order Causal Discovery (TSCD), an algorithm that leverages covariance matrices from observational and interventional data to infer causal structures. TSCD assumes linear structural equation models on DAGs with uncorrelated noise and outputs both the causal graph and edge functions. The method focuses on second-order statistics for their computational efficiency, identifiability beyond first-order methods, and applicability to non-Gaussian variables. Experimental results demonstrate that TSCD can recover causal order and parameters from a logarithmic number of interventions relative to variable count, is robust to noise, competes with existing methods, and scales effectively to hundreds of variables.
为在最小假设下实现高效且可识别的因果发现，本文提出基于张量的二阶因果发现算法（TSCD），该算法利用观测与干预数据的协方差矩阵推断因果结构。在假设变量服从线性结构方程模型、噪声变量不相关且变量无需服从高斯分布的前提下，TSCD通过张量分解重建因果图及边函数，仅依赖二阶统计量以实现计算与统计效率，且对非高斯变量有效。实验表明，TSCD仅需变量数的对数级干预即可实现可识别性，对噪声鲁棒，性能可与现有方法媲美，并能有效扩展至数百变量规模。
Volterra Generative Models
Authors: Yusen Jia, Bingyan Han
First: 2026-06-16T15:40:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T15:40:09+00:00
Comments: 36 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Score-based diffusion models typically use Brownian perturbations, which provide tractable reverse-time dynamics but impose memoryless noising. We introduce Volterra generative models, a continuous-time score-based framework whose forward process injects path-dependent noise through fractional kernels. To handle the non-Markovian and non-semimartingale dynamics, we construct finite-dimensional Markovian lifts using Gaussian quadrature in both regimes and a hybrid finite-difference exponential approximation in the smooth regime. We prove squared error bounds, derive an augmented linear-Gaussian forward process, and show that the learning can remain data-dimensional by considering residual states and analytic auxiliary Gaussian scores. We also identify covariance and reverse-time degeneracies caused by shared Brownian factors and signed smooth-regime weights. The degeneracy motivates stabilized conditioning and, for stiff larger lifts, a Gaussian-bridge reconstruction sampler. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 show that persistent fractional perturbations with small Markovian lifts can improve score-based generation on MNIST and provide a promising extension to natural images, while the bridge sampler provides a stability mechanism for larger lifts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Volterra生成模型
基于分数的扩散模型通常使用布朗扰动，这提供了可处理的反向时间动力学，但施加了无记忆的噪声。我们引入了Volterra生成模型，这是一种连续时间的基于分数的框架，其前向过程通过分数核注入路径依赖的噪声。为处理非马尔可夫性和非半鞅动力学，我们在两种情形下均使用高斯求积构造有限维马尔可夫提升，并在光滑情形下采用混合有限差分指数近似。我们证明了平方误差界，推导出一个增强的线性高斯前向过程，并表明通过考虑残差状态和解析辅助高斯分数，学习可保持数据维度。我们还识别了由共享布朗因子和符号光滑区域权重引起的协方差和反向时间退化。这种退化促使了稳定化条件，并针对刚性较大的提升，提出高斯桥重建采样器。在MNIST和CIFAR-10上的实验表明，具有小马尔可夫提升的持续分数扰动可改善MNIST上的基于分数的生成，并为自然图像提供有前景的扩展，而桥采样器为较大提升提供了稳定性机制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the memoryless noising limitation of standard score-based diffusion models, this work introduces Volterra generative models that incorporate path-dependent noise via fractional kernels in continuous time. To manage the resulting non-Markovian dynamics, the authors construct finite-dimensional Markovian lifts using Gaussian quadrature and hybrid finite-difference approximations. Theoretical analysis establishes error bounds and an augmented linear-Gaussian forward process, while residual states and auxiliary Gaussian scores enable data-efficient learning. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 demonstrate that fractional perturbations with small lifts enhance generation quality on MNIST and offer scalability potential for natural images, while a Gaussian-bridge sampler stabilizes training for larger lifts.
为克服传统分数基扩散模型的无记忆噪声缺陷，本文提出Volterra生成模型，通过分数核在连续时间中注入路径依赖噪声。为处理非马尔可夫动态，作者采用高斯求积与混合有限差分指数近似构造有限维马尔可夫提升，并推导出增强的线性高斯前向过程并证明误差界。实验表明，小规模提升的分数扰动可提升MNIST生成质量，并有望扩展至自然图像，而高斯桥采样器能稳定大提升模型的训练。
Conformal Path Reasoning: Trustworthy Knowledge Graph Question Answering via Path-Level Calibration
Authors: Shuhang Lin, Chuhao Zhou, Xiao Lin, Zihan Dong, Kuan Lu, Zhencan Peng, Jie Yin, Dimitris N. Metaxas
First: 2026-05-08T17:57:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T15:40:09+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 3 figures, 2 tables;
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Knowledge Graph Question Answering (KGQA) offers grounded, interpretable reasoning, but existing methods often fail to provide reliable coverage guarantees over retrieved answers. While Conformal Prediction (CP) offers a principled framework for producing prediction sets with statistical guarantees, prior conformal KGQA methods suffer from two critical pitfalls: violated coverage guarantees due to invalid calibration, and weak score discriminability that yields excessively large prediction sets. We propose Conformal Path Reasoning (CPR), a novel trustworthy KGQA framework built on two key innovations. First, query-level conformal calibration over path-level scores preserves exchangeability to ensure valid coverage guarantees. Second, we introduce the Residual Conformal Value Network (RCVNet), a lightweight module trained via PUCT-guided exploration to learn discriminative path-level nonconformity scores. Extensive experiments show that CPR significantly improves the Empirical Coverage Rate by 45% while reducing prediction set size by 52% on average over conformal baselines across benchmark datasets, highlighting its effectiveness for reliable conformal reasoning over knowledge graphs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：符合性路径推理：通过路径级校准实现可信知识图谱问答
知识图谱问答（KGQA）提供基于事实且可解释的推理，但现有方法往往无法对检索到的答案提供可靠的覆盖保证。虽然符合性预测（CP）提供了一种生成具有统计保证的预测集的原理框架，但先前的符合性KGQA方法存在两个关键缺陷：因校准无效导致覆盖保证被违反，以及得分区分度弱导致预测集过大。我们提出符合性路径推理（CPR），一种基于两项关键创新的新型可信KGQA框架。首先，通过路径级得分进行查询级符合性校准，保持交换性以确保有效的覆盖保证。其次，我们引入残差符合性值网络（RCVNet），一种轻量级模块，通过PUCT引导的探索训练，学习具有区分性的路径级非符合性得分。大量实验表明，CPR在基准数据集上相比符合性基线方法，经验覆盖率达到45%的提升，预测集平均缩小52%，凸显其在知识图谱上实现可靠符合性推理的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable coverage guarantees in Knowledge Graph Question Answering, the paper introduces Conformal Path Reasoning (CPR), a framework that calibrates path-level scores to ensure valid statistical coverage while improving discriminability. CPR employs query-level conformal calibration to preserve exchangeability and uses a lightweight Residual Conformal Value Network (RCVNet) trained via PUCT-guided exploration to generate discriminative nonconformity scores. Experimental results demonstrate that CPR achieves a 45% improvement in empirical coverage rate and a 52% reduction in prediction set size compared to existing conformal KGQA methods across benchmark datasets.
为提升知识图谱问答的可靠性和可解释性，本文提出了一种名为Conformal Path Reasoning（CPR）的新框架，旨在解决现有方法在校准失效和得分区分度不足的问题。CPR通过在路径级别对查询进行校准，确保覆盖保证的有效性，并引入轻量级的残差校准值网络（RCVNet），该网络通过PUCT引导探索训练，学习具有区分能力的非一致性得分。实验表明，CPR在多个基准数据集上相较现有校准基线，将经验覆盖率提升45%，预测集合大小平均减少52%。
Blended Chart Surfaces: A Seamless Explicit Representation for Smooth Surface Fitting
Authors: Romy Williamson, Niloy Mitra
First: 2026-06-16T15:39:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T15:39:38+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 16 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A surface representation suitable for geometry processing should be compact and explicit, provide global smoothness guarantees, support a wide range of surface topologies, and offer reliable access to differential quantities such as normals and surface energies, while remaining compatible with modern differentiable optimization. Existing neural representations typically sacrifice one or more of these properties: implicit fields typically require iso-surfacing for downstream use, while explicit neural maps are constrained by canonical-domain parametrizations or exhibit seam artifacts between local charts. We introduce Blended Chart Surfaces, a compact, network-free, explicit representation that is smooth by construction and anchored to user-provided topology. Given a coarse proxy mesh encoding the intended surface topology and approximate geometry, Blended Chart Surfaces jointly optimize for a polynomial map at each proxy vertex using an off-the-shelf optimizer to fit to an implicit target shape, avoiding the need for an input parametrization. Neighboring maps are fused using a smooth 'one-ring coordinate' blending scheme, decoupling topology and coarse geometry (carried by the proxy) from geometric details (carried by the local patches). The surface is globally smooth, fully differentiable, and enables stable evaluation of derivatives, making differential quantities and surface energies directly accessible. Additionally, our construction is equivariant to rigid motions and scaling of the proxy mesh. We evaluate Blended Chart Surfaces on various topologies and geometric complexity, and compare against explicit alternatives including interpolating-function baselines and mesh-displacement MLPs. Across these, Blended Chart Surfaces achieve a favorable trade-off among compactness, simplicity, access to differential quantities, and expressivity while remaining smooth across patch boundaries.
中文标题/摘要
标题：混合图表曲面：用于平滑曲面拟合的无缝显式表示
适用于几何处理的曲面表示应具备紧凑显式、全局平滑性保证、支持多种曲面拓扑结构，并能可靠访问法向量、曲面能量等微分量，同时兼容现代可微优化。现有神经表示通常牺牲其中一项或多项性质：隐式场需通过等值面提取用于下游应用，而显式神经映射受限于规范域参数化或在局部图表间出现接缝伪影。我们提出混合图表曲面，这是一种紧凑、无网络、显式的表示方法，其构造即保证平滑性，并锚定用户提供的拓扑结构。给定粗略代理网格编码目标曲面拓扑与近似几何，混合图表曲面在每个代理顶点处联合优化多项式映射，使用现成优化器拟合至隐式目标形状，无需输入参数化。相邻映射通过平滑的“一环坐标”混合方案融合，将拓扑与粗几何（由代理网格承载）与几何细节（由局部贴片承载）解耦。该曲面全局平滑、完全可微，可稳定计算导数，使微分量与曲面能量直接可得。此外，该构造对代理网格的刚体运动与缩放保持等变性。我们在多种拓扑与几何复杂度下评估混合图表曲面，并与显式替代方案（包括插值函数基线与网格位移MLP）对比。在这些方案中，混合图表曲面在紧凑性、简洁性、微分量访问能力与表达能力之间取得良好平衡，同时保持贴片边界处的平滑性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a compact, explicit surface representation that supports global smoothness, arbitrary topologies, and reliable differential quantities while being compatible with differentiable optimization, the paper introduces Blended Chart Surfaces. This method constructs a smooth, network-free surface by optimizing polynomial maps at each vertex of a coarse proxy mesh to fit an implicit target shape, then blends neighboring maps using a 'one-ring coordinate' scheme to ensure seamless transitions. Experimental results show that this approach achieves a favorable balance between compactness, simplicity, and expressivity, while maintaining global smoothness and enabling stable computation of surface derivatives and energies across diverse topologies and geometric complexities, outperforming existing explicit methods including interpolating-function baselines and mesh-displacement MLPs.
为满足几何处理中对紧凑、显式、全局平滑、支持任意拓扑结构并可稳定计算微分量的需求，本文提出Blended Chart Surfaces方法。该方法通过在粗略代理网格的每个顶点处优化多项式映射，拟合隐式目标形状，使用现成优化器实现，无需输入参数化。相邻映射通过平滑的‘一环坐标’融合方案无缝衔接，实现拓扑与几何细节的解耦。生成的表面全局平滑、可微分，支持直接计算法向量与曲面能量。实验表明，该方法在多种拓扑和几何复杂度下，相比现有显式方法在紧凑性、简洁性、平滑性与表达能力之间取得了更优平衡。
Agentic AI-based Framework for Mitigating Premature Diagnostic Handoff and Silent Hallucination in Healthcare Applications
Authors: Divyansh Srivastava, Shreya Ghosh, Anshul Verma, Rajkumar Buyya
First: 2026-06-16T15:39:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T15:39:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) and multi-agent systems have driven the rise of Agentic AI, showing promise for medical reasoning. However, open-ended conversational agents remain prone to two critical failure modes: premature diagnostic handoff and silent clinical hallucinations that may go undetected before reaching the patient. In this work, we propose a multi-agent framework that addresses both issues by replacing ``LLM-as-a-judge'' routing with deterministic orchestration constraints. The framework incorporates two safety mechanisms. First, a neuro-symbolic state-tracking gate enforces completeness of the OLDCARTS clinical protocol (Onset, Location, Duration, Character, Aggravating/Alleviating factors, Radiation, Timing, and Severity) by blocking diagnostic transitions until all required dimensions are collected. Second, an epistemic uncertainty quantification (UQ) gate computes semantic entropy (H) across K=5 independent diagnostic samples to identify and intercept divergent outputs before delivery. We evaluate the system using simulated patient agents powered by the llama-3.1-70b-instruct model on 150 test cases. The full architecture achieves 49.3% diagnostic precision, representing an absolute improvement of 11.3 percentage points over an unconstrained baseline. Additionally, we observe a statistically significant negative correlation (r = -0.181, p < 0.05) between OLDCARTS completeness (σ) and semantic entropy (H), suggesting that structured information gathering is associated with reduced diagnostic uncertainty.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于代理AI的框架，用于缓解医疗应用中的过早诊断移交与静默幻觉
大型语言模型（LLMs）和多智能体系统的最新进展推动了代理AI的发展，显示出在医学推理方面的潜力。然而，开放式对话代理仍易出现两种关键故障模式：过早诊断移交和静默临床幻觉，这些可能在到达患者前未被发现。本文提出一种多智能体框架，通过将“LLM作为裁判”的路由替换为确定性编排约束，同时解决这两个问题。该框架包含两种安全机制：第一，神经符号状态跟踪门强制执行OLDCARTS临床协议（发作、部位、持续时间、特征、加重/缓解因素、放射、时间、严重程度）的完整性，直到收集所有必需维度才允许诊断转换；第二，认识论不确定性量化（UQ）门通过K=5个独立诊断样本计算语义熵（H），以识别并拦截交付前的分歧输出。我们在150个测试案例中，使用基于llama-3.1-70b-instruct模型的模拟患者代理评估该系统。完整架构实现49.3%的诊断精度，相较无约束基线绝对提升11.3个百分点。此外，我们观察到OLDCARTS完整性（σ）与语义熵（H）之间存在统计显著的负相关（r = -0.181, p < 0.05），表明结构化信息收集与降低诊断不确定性相关。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risks of premature diagnostic handoff and silent hallucinations in conversational medical AI, this work introduces a multi-agent framework that replaces LLM-based routing with deterministic orchestration. It employs two safety gates: a neuro-symbolic state-tracking gate enforcing OLDCARTS clinical protocol completeness before diagnosis, and an epistemic uncertainty quantification gate using semantic entropy across five diagnostic samples to detect divergent outputs. Evaluated on 150 simulated patient cases using llama-3.1-70b-instruct, the system achieved 49.3% diagnostic precision—11.3 percentage points higher than an unconstrained baseline—and revealed a statistically significant negative correlation (r = -0.181, p < 0.05) between OLDCARTS completeness and semantic entropy, indicating structured data collection reduces diagnostic uncertainty.
为应对医疗对话AI中过早诊断移交与沉默幻觉的风险，本研究提出一种多智能体框架，用确定性编排替代概率性LLM路由。框架包含两个安全机制：神经符号状态跟踪器强制执行OLDCARTS临床协议，确保诊断前收集所有必要维度；以及基于五次独立诊断样本语义熵的元认知不确定性量化器，拦截不一致输出。在150个模拟病例上使用llama-3.1-70b-instruct模型评估，系统诊断准确率达49.3%，较无约束基线提升11.3个百分点，并发现OLDCARTS完整性与语义熵呈显著负相关（r = -0.181, p < 0.05），表明结构化信息收集可降低诊断不确定性。
NoiseTilt: Noise-Tilted Reverse Kernels for Diffusion Reward Alignment
Authors: Jisung Hwang, Yunhong Min, Jaihoon Kim, I-Chao Shen, Minhyuk Sung
First: 2026-06-16T15:38:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-16T15:38:44+00:00
Comments: 52 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce the Noise-Tilted Reverse Kernel (NTRK), a reward-guided diffusion sampler that injects reward gradients through the noise term, leaving the pretrained reverse kernel unchanged and requiring only a single sample per step. Reward-guided sampling at inference time has greatly expanded the versatility of pretrained diffusion models. Yet existing methods face a trade-off. Gradient-based guidance shifts the reverse mean, steering generation but pushing intermediate states outside the region that the model was trained on and degrading quality. Search-based methods preserve quality but gain no gradient signal. No prior method achieves both. NTRK resolves this by keeping the reverse mean fixed and biasing the noise term toward high reward. We introduce a whitening operator, the central mechanism behind NTRK, that makes the reward gradient safe to inject as noise without losing its guiding signal. Across various reward alignment tasks, NTRK outperforms recent state-of-the-art baselines without losing sample quality. Remarkably, on aesthetic generation, NTRK surpasses the reward of the best baseline at 500 NFEs using only 25 NFEs, a 20$\times$ reduction in compute.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NoiseTilt：用于扩散奖励对齐的噪声倾斜反向核
我们提出了噪声倾斜反向核（NTRK），这是一种奖励引导的扩散采样器，通过噪声项注入奖励梯度，同时保持预训练的反向核不变，且每步仅需一次采样。推理时的奖励引导采样极大地扩展了预训练扩散模型的适用性。然而，现有方法面临权衡：基于梯度的引导会移动反向均值，引导生成但使中间状态脱离模型训练区域并降低质量；基于搜索的方法则保持质量但无法获得梯度信号。此前无方法能同时实现这两者。NTRK通过固定反向均值并使噪声项偏向高奖励区域解决此问题。我们引入了一种白化算子，这是NTRK的核心机制，使其能安全注入奖励梯度作为噪声而不丢失引导信号。在各类奖励对齐任务中，NTRK在不降低样本质量的前提下超越了近期最先进基线。在美学生成任务中，NTRK仅用25个NFEs便超越了最佳基线在500个NFEs下的奖励表现，计算量减少20倍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to align diffusion models with reward signals without sacrificing sample quality, the authors propose NoiseTilt: a reward-guided sampling method that injects reward gradients into the noise term rather than altering the reverse kernel’s mean. This approach preserves the pretrained model’s behavior while steering generation toward higher rewards. The core mechanism, a whitening operator, ensures the reward gradient can be safely added as noise without degrading quality. Experiments show NTRK outperforms recent baselines across multiple reward alignment tasks, and notably achieves superior aesthetic quality in just 25 NFEs—20x faster than the best baseline—without quality loss.
为在不损害样本质量的前提下实现扩散模型与奖励信号的对齐，作者提出NoiseTilt方法，通过将奖励梯度注入噪声项而非改变反向核的均值来实现奖励引导采样。其核心机制——白化算子——确保奖励梯度可安全作为噪声添加而不降低生成质量。实验表明，NTRK在多个奖励对齐任务中优于近期最先进基线，尤其在美学生成任务中，仅用25步NFE即可达到500步NFE基线的最佳奖励水平，计算量减少20倍。
